# TRADE-OFFS IN STATIC AND DYNAMIC EVALUATION OF HIERARCHICAL QUERIES

层次化查询静态与动态评估中的权衡取舍

AHMET KARA ${}^{a}$ , MILOS NIKOLIC ${}^{b}$ , DAN OLTEANU ${}^{a}$ , AND HAOZHE ZHANG ${}^{c}$

艾哈迈德·卡拉${}^{a}$、米洛斯·尼科利奇${}^{b}$、丹·奥尔泰亚努${}^{a}$和张浩哲${}^{c}$

${}^{a}$ University of Zurich

${}^{a}$苏黎世大学

e-mail address: kara@ifi.uzh.ch, olteanu@ifi.uzh.ch, zhang@ifi.uzh.ch

电子邮箱：kara@ifi.uzh.ch、olteanu@ifi.uzh.ch、zhang@ifi.uzh.ch

${}^{b}$ University of Edinburgh

${}^{b}$爱丁堡大学

e-mail address: milos.nikolic@ed.ac.uk

电子邮箱：milos.nikolic@ed.ac.uk

Abstract. We investigate trade-offs in static and dynamic evaluation of hierarchical queries with arbitrary free variables. In the static setting, the trade-off is between the time to partially compute the query result and the delay needed to enumerate its tuples. In the dynamic setting, we additionally consider the time needed to update the query result under single-tuple inserts or deletes to the database.

摘要。我们研究含任意自由变量的层次化查询在静态与动态评估中的权衡关系。静态场景下，权衡体现在部分计算结果所需时间与枚举元组所需延迟之间；动态场景下，还需考虑数据库单条元组增删时更新查询结果的时间。

Our approach observes the degree of values in the database and uses different computation and maintenance strategies for high-degree (heavy) and low-degree (light) values. For the latter it partially computes the result, while for the former it computes enough information to allow for on-the-fly enumeration.

本方法通过观测数值在数据库中的度（degree），对高度数（重型）和低度数（轻型）数值分别采用计算与维护策略：对后者进行部分结果预计算，对前者则构建支持实时枚举的充分信息。

We define the preprocessing time, the update time, and the enumeration delay as functions of the light/heavy threshold. By appropriately choosing this threshold, our approach recovers a number of prior results when restricted to hierarchical queries.

我们将预处理时间、更新时间和枚举延迟定义为轻重阈值函数。通过合理设置该阈值，本方法在层次化查询限定下可复现多项已有成果。

We show that for a restricted class of hierarchical queries, our approach achieves worst-case optimal update time and enumeration delay conditioned on the Online Matrix-Vector Multiplication Conjecture.

证明表明：对于特定类别的层次化查询，基于在线矩阵向量乘法猜想（Online Matrix-Vector Multiplication Conjecture），本方法可实现最坏情况最优的更新时间与枚举延迟。

## 1. INTRODUCTION

## 1. 引言

The problems of static evaluation, i.e., computing the result of a query [Yan81, OZ15, KNS17, NPRR18], and dynamic evaluation, i.e., maintaining the result of a query under inserts and deletes of tuples to the input relations [Koc10, CY12, K+14, BKS17a, IUV17, KNN*19a], are fundamental to relational databases.

静态评估（即查询结果计算[Yan81, OZ15, KNS17, NPRR18]）与动态评估（即在输入关系表元组增删时维护查询结果[Koc10, CY12, K+14, BKS17a, IUV17, KNN*19a]）是关系数据库的核心问题。

We consider a refinement of these two problems that decomposes the overall evaluation time into the preprocessing time, which is used to compute a data structure that represents the query result, the update time, which is the time to update the data structure under inserts and deletes to the input data, and the enumeration delay, which is the time between the start of the enumeration process and the output of the first tuple in the query result, the time between outputting any two consecutive tuples, and the time between outputting the last tuple and the end of the enumeration process [DG07]. In this paper we investigate the relationship between preprocessing, update, and delay and answer questions such as, how much preprocessing time is needed to achieve sublinear enumeration delay.

我们提出这两个问题的改进方案，将总体评估时间分解为：预处理时间（用于构建表示查询结果的数据结构）、更新时间（在输入数据增删时更新数据结构所需时间）、枚举延迟（枚举过程开始至首条结果输出间隔、相邻结果输出间隔、末条结果输出至枚举结束间隔[DG07]）。本文探究预处理、更新与延迟的关系，解答诸如"实现次线性枚举延迟需要多少预处理时间"等问题。

---

Key words and phrases: adaptive evaluation, incremental maintenance.

关键词：自适应评估，增量维护。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

We consider the static and dynamic evaluation of a subclass of $\alpha$ -acyclic queries called hierarchical queries:

我们研究称为层次化查询的$\alpha$非循环查询子类的静态与动态评估：

Definition 1.1 [SORK11, BKS17a]. A conjunctive query is hierarchical if for any two variables, their sets of atoms in the query are either disjoint or one is contained in the other.

定义1.1 [SORK11, BKS17a]。当任意两个变量的原子集合在查询中要么互斥、要么存在包含关系时，该连接查询称为层次化查询。

For instance,the query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( {B,C}\right)$ is hierarchical,while $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right)$ , $S\left( {B,C}\right) ,T\left( C\right)$ is not,for any $\mathcal{F} \subseteq  \{ A,B,C\}$ . In our study,we do not set any restriction on the set of free variables of a hierarchical query.

例如，查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( {B,C}\right)$具有层次性，而$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right)$、$S\left( {B,C}\right) ,T\left( C\right)$则不具备，对于任何$\mathcal{F} \subseteq  \{ A,B,C\}$皆然。在本研究中，我们对层次性查询的自由变量集合不作任何限制。

Hierarchical queries enjoy highly desirable tractability properties in a variety of computational settings, making them an important yardstick for database computation. The notion of hierarchical queries used in this paper has been initially introduced in the context of probabilistic databases [SORK11]. The Boolean conjunctive queries without repeating relation symbols that can be computed in polynomial time on tuple-independent probabilistic databases are hierarchical; non-hierarchical queries are hard for #P [SORK11]. This dichotomy was extended to non-Boolean queries with negation [FO16]. Hierarchical queries are the conjunctive queries whose provenance admits a factorized representation where each input tuple occurs a constant number of times; any factorization of the provenance of a non-hierarchical query would require a number of occurrences of the provenance of some input tuple dependent on the input database size [OZ12]. For hierarchical queries without self-joins, this read-once factorized representation explains their tractability for exact probability computation over probabilistic databases. In the Massively Parallel Computation (MPC) model, the hierarchical queries admit parallel evaluation with one communication step [KS11b]. The $r$ -hierarchical queries,which are conjunctive queries that become hierarchical by repeatedly removing the atoms whose complete set of variables occurs in another atom, can be evaluated in the MPC model using a constant number of steps and optimal load on every single database instance [HY19]. Hierarchical queries also admit one-step streaming evaluation in the finite cursor model $\left\lbrack  {{\mathrm{{GGL}}}^{ + }{09}}\right\rbrack$ . Under updates,the $q$ -hierarchical queries are the conjunctive queries that admit constant-time update and delay [BKS17a]. The $q$ -hierarchical queries are a proper subclass of both the free-connex $\alpha$ -acyclic and hierarchical queries. Besides being hierarchical, the following condition holds on the free variables of a $q$ -hierarchical query: if the set of atoms of a free variable is strictly contained in the set of another variable, then the latter must also be free.

层次性查询在多种计算场景中展现出极佳的可处理性，使其成为数据库计算的重要基准。本文采用的层次性查询概念最初源于概率数据库研究[SORK11]。在元组独立的概率数据库上，无重复关系符号的布尔连接查询若能在多项式时间内计算，则属于层次性查询；非层次性查询则属于#P难题[SORK11]。这一二分法后来被推广至含否定的非布尔查询[FO16]。层次性查询的连接查询其溯源可分解为常数次出现的输入元组表示；而非层次性查询的溯源分解必然存在某些输入元组的出现次数与数据库规模相关[OZ12]。对于无自连接的层次性查询，这种"只读一次"的分解形式解释了其在概率数据库精确计算中的可处理性。在大规模并行计算(MPC)模型中，层次性查询只需单次通信即可完成并行评估[KS11b]。而$r$-层次性查询（通过递归移除变量全集已存在于其他原子的原子得到的连接查询）在MPC模型中能以常数步骤和最优负载完成计算[HY19]。层次性查询在有限游标模型$\left\lbrack  {{\mathrm{{GGL}}}^{ + }{09}}\right\rbrack$中还支持单步流式评估。在更新场景下，$q$-层次性查询可实现常数时间更新与延迟[BKS17a]。这类查询是自由连接$\alpha$-无环且层次性查询的真子类，除具备层次性外，其自由变量还需满足：若某自由变量的原子集合严格包含于另一变量，则后者也必须是自由变量。

In this paper we characterize trade-offs in the static and dynamic evaluation of hierarchical queries. In the static setting, we are interested in the trade-off between preprocessing time and enumeration delay. In the dynamic case, we additionally consider the update time. Section 2 states our main result in the static setting and explains how it recovers prior results on static query evaluation. Section 3 gives our main result in the dynamic setting and discusses its implications. These two sections also overview prior work on static and dynamic query evaluation. Section 4 introduces the basic notions underlying our approach. Sections 5-7 detail the preprocessing, enumeration, and update stages of our approach. Section 8 shows that for a restricted class of hierarchical queries, our approach achieves worst-case optimal update time and enumeration delay, conditioned on the Online Matrix-Vector Multiplication Conjecture. We illustrate our approach using two detailed examples in Section 9 and conclude in Section 10. The proofs of the main theorems in Sections 2 and 3 and the propositions in Sections 5-7 are deferred to Appendices A-E. The proofs of the propositions in Sections 4 and 8 can be found in the technical report [KNOZ19].

本文系统阐述了层次性查询在静态与动态评估中的权衡机制。静态场景关注预处理时间与枚举延迟的权衡，动态场景则额外考虑更新时间。第2节陈述静态环境的主要结论，阐释其如何涵盖既往静态查询评估成果；第3节给出动态环境的核心发现并讨论其意义，这两节同时综述了相关领域的前沿工作。第4节介绍方法论基础，第5-7节详述预处理、枚举及更新阶段的技术实现。第8节证明对于特定受限的层次性查询类，基于在线矩阵-向量乘法猜想，我们的方法可实现最坏情况下最优的更新时间和枚举延迟。第9节通过两个详例演示方法论，第10节总结全文。第2-3节主定理及第5-7节命题的证明见附录A-E，第4、8节命题证明详见技术报告[KNOZ19]。

A preliminary version of this work appeared in PODS 2020 [KNOZ20]. We extended it as follows. We overviewed in greater depth and breadth the related work for a more complete picture of the state of the art (Sections 1-3). We added new motivating examples to demonstrate that our approach achieves better overall evaluation time than existing approaches both in the static and dynamic cases (Sections 2 and 3). We included necessary background on the computational model and width measures (Section 4). We added a detailed description of the algorithms (UNION and Product) used by the enumeration procedure of our approach (Section 6). We included the procedures for major and minor rebalancing in case of updates and gave the procedure for the maintenance of a query result under sequences of updates (Section 7). Finally, we included complete proofs of the main results and the main statements on the preprocessing, enumeration, and update stages of our approach (Appendices A-E).

本研究的初步版本曾发表于PODS 2020会议[KNOZ20]。我们进行了以下扩展：更深入全面地综述了相关工作，以更完整呈现领域研究现状（第1-3节）；新增激励案例，证明在静态和动态场景下我们的方法都能获得比现有方案更优的总体评估时间（第2-3节）；补充了计算模型和宽度度量所需的基础知识（第4节）；详细描述了枚举过程中使用的UNION和Product算法（第6节）；增加了更新时主/次再平衡流程及连续更新下的查询结果维护流程（第7节）；最后提供了全部主要结果的完整证明，以及预处理、枚举和更新阶段的核心论述（附录A-E）。

## 2. Trade-offs in Static Query Evaluation

## 2. 静态查询评估中的权衡

Our main result for the static evaluation of hierarchical queries is stated next.

接下来阐述我们在层次化查询静态评估方面的主要成果。

Theorem 2.1. Given a hierarchical query with static width $\mathbf{w}$ ,a database of size $N$ ,and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,the query result can be enumerated with $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ delay after $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ preprocessing time.

定理2.1. 给定静态宽度为$\mathbf{w}$的层次化查询、规模为$N$的数据库及$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，经过$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$预处理时间后，可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$延迟内枚举查询结果。

The measure $\mathrm{w}$ ,previously introduced as ${s}^{ \uparrow  }$ [OZ15],generalizes the fractional hypertree width [Mar10] from Boolean to arbitrary conjunctive queries. This is equivalent to the FAQ-width in case of Functional Aggregate Queries over a single semiring [AKNR16]. In this paper, we refer to this measure as the static width of the query (Definition 4.6).

该$\mathrm{w}$度量最初作为${s}^{ \uparrow  }$提出[OZ15]，将布尔查询的分数超树宽度[Mar10]推广至任意合取查询。对于单半环上的函数聚合查询(FAQ)，该度量等价于FAQ宽度[AKNR16]。本文称之为查询的静态宽度（定义4.6）。

Theorem 2.1 expresses the runtime components as functions of a parameter $\epsilon$ . The dotted green line and the purple line in the left plot in Figure 1 depict the preprocessing time and respectively the enumeration delay. The middle plot in Figure 1 visualizes the trade-off between the two components. Our approach achieves each blue point and each point on the blue line. Prior approaches are represented by the blue points in the trade-off space. By appropriately setting $\epsilon$ ,our approach recovers prior results restricted to hierarchical queries. For $\epsilon  = 0$ ,both the preprocessing time and the delay become $O\left( N\right)$ ,as for $\alpha$ -acyclic queries [BDG07]. For $\epsilon  = 1$ ,we obtain $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}}\right)$ preprocessing time and $O\left( 1\right)$ delay as for conjunctive queries [OZ15]. Free-connex acyclic queries are a special class of queries that enjoy linear preprocessing time and constant delay [BDG07]. We recover this result as follows. First,we observe that any free-connex hierarchical query has static width $\mathrm{w} = 1$ . This means that the preprocessing time remains $O\left( N\right)$ regardless of $\epsilon$ ; we then choose $\epsilon  = 1$ to obtain $O\left( 1\right)$ delay. For bounded-degree databases,i.e.,where each value appears at most $c$ times for some constant $c = {N}^{\beta }$ ,first-order queries admit $O\left( N\right)$ preprocessing time and $O\left( 1\right)$ delay [DG07,KS11a]. We recover the $O\left( 1\right)$ delay using $\epsilon  = 1$ . The preprocessing time becomes $\mathcal{O}\left( {N \cdot  {\left( {N}^{\beta }\right) }^{\mathrm{w} - 1}}\right)  = \mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ if our approach uses the constant upper bound $c$ instead of the upper bound ${N}^{\epsilon }$ on the degrees. The left Venn diagram in Figure 2 depicts the relationship of our result in Theorems 2.1 with prior results.

定理2.1将运行时组件表示为参数$\epsilon$的函数。图1左图中绿色虚线与紫色线分别描绘了预处理时间和枚举延迟。图1中图展示了两者间的权衡关系。我们的方法实现了每个蓝色点及蓝线上各点。现有方法以蓝色点呈现在权衡空间中。通过适当设置$\epsilon$，我们的方法可还原现有局限于层次化查询的结果。当$\epsilon  = 0$时，预处理时间与延迟均变为$O\left( N\right)$，如同$\alpha$非循环查询[BDG07]的情况。当$\epsilon  = 1$时，我们获得$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}}\right)$预处理时间和$O\left( 1\right)$延迟，与合取查询[OZ15]一致。自由连接非循环查询作为特殊查询类别，享有线性预处理时间和恒定延迟[BDG07]。我们通过以下方式复现该结果：首先注意到任何自由连接层次化查询都具有静态宽度$\mathrm{w} = 1$，这意味着预处理时间保持$O\left( N\right)$而与$\epsilon$无关；随后选择$\epsilon  = 1$以获得$O\left( 1\right)$延迟。对于有界度数据库（即每个值最多出现$c$次，其中$c = {N}^{\beta }$为常数），一阶查询允许$O\left( N\right)$预处理时间和$O\left( 1\right)$延迟[DG07,KS11a]。我们使用$\epsilon  = 1$复现了$O\left( 1\right)$延迟。若采用常数上界$c$而非度数上界${N}^{\epsilon }$，预处理时间将变为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N \cdot  {\left( {N}^{\beta }\right) }^{\mathrm{w} - 1}}\right)  = \mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$。图2左侧维恩图展示了定理2.1结果与现有研究的关系。

The next example demonstrates how the complexities of our approach in the static case imply lower overall evaluation time than existing approaches.

下例展示静态情况下我们方法的复杂度如何实现比现有方法更低的总体评估耗时。

Example 2.2. Consider the hierarchical query $Q\left( {A,C}\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( {B,C}\right)$ . Let us assume that the input relations are of size $N$ . Then,it takes quadratic time to compute the list of tuples in the query result of $Q$ . (As it will become clearer later,this query has static width 2,which explains the $O\left( {N}^{2}\right)$ time complexity for the evaluation of $Q$ .)

示例2.2 考虑层次化查询$Q\left( {A,C}\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( {B,C}\right)$。假设输入关系规模为$N$，则计算$Q$查询结果元组列表需平方级耗时。（后文将阐明，该查询具有静态宽度2，这解释了$Q$评估的$O\left( {N}^{2}\right)$时间复杂度。）

An eager evaluation does just this: It readily computes the list of tuples in the query result of $Q$ . This requires quadratic preprocessing time,after which the tuples in the query result can be enumerated with constant delay [OZ12].

急切评估法直接计算$Q$查询结果的元组列表，需平方级预处理时间，之后可以恒定延迟枚举结果元组[OZ12]。

In contrast, a lazy evaluation approach computes the first tuple in the query result then the second tuple and so on. This can be done using linear preprocessing time followed by linear enumeration delay for each tuple in the result [BDG07]. It is conjectured that the delay cannot be lowered to constant after linear-time preprocessing for $Q$ [BDG07]. (The explanation is that $Q$ is not free-connex,a notion we will introduce in Section 4.)

相反，惰性评估法先计算查询结果的第一个元组，再计算第二个，依此类推。该方法采用线性预处理时间，随后对结果中每个元组产生线性枚举延迟[BDG07]。据推测，对于$Q$，线性时间预处理后延迟无法降至恒定[BDG07]。（原因在于$Q$非自由连接类型，该概念将在第4节介绍。）

Our approach achieves $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \epsilon }\right)$ preprocessing time and $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ enumeration delay for any $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ . The complexities of the eager,lazy,and our approach are as follows:

我们的方法对任意$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$实现$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \epsilon }\right)$预处理时间和$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$枚举延迟。急切法、惰性法及我们方法的复杂度对比如下：

<table><tbody><tr><td>方法</td><td>预处理</td><td>延迟</td></tr><tr><td>惰性</td><td>$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$</td><td>$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$</td></tr><tr><td>急切</td><td>$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{2}\right)$</td><td>O(1)</td></tr><tr><td>我们的</td><td>$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \epsilon }\right)$</td><td>$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$</td></tr></tbody></table>

<!-- Media -->

Our approach recovers the lazy approach at $\epsilon  = 0$ and the eager approach at $\epsilon  = 1$ . For any $\epsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,it achieves new trade-offs between preprocessing time and enumeration delay.

我们的方法在$\epsilon  = 0$处恢复惰性策略（lazy approach），在$\epsilon  = 1$处恢复积极策略（eager approach）。对于任意$\epsilon  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$，该方法实现了预处理时间与枚举延迟之间的新权衡。

Given that the input relations have size $N$ ,the AGM bound [AGM13] implies that the result of the query has at most ${N}^{2}$ tuples. Assume that we want to enumerate ${N}^{\gamma }$ tuples from the result,for some $0 \leq  \gamma  \leq  2$ . In the following table,the second to fourth rows give the exponents of the overall evaluation times achieved by the lazy, eager, and our approaches for different values of $\gamma$ . The last row gives the $\epsilon$ values at which we achieve the complexities of our approach.

假设输入关系的大小为$N$，AGM边界[AGM13]表明查询结果最多包含${N}^{2}$个元组。若需从结果中枚举${N}^{\gamma }$个元组（其中$0 \leq  \gamma  \leq  2$为特定值），下表第二至第四行分别列出了惰性策略、积极策略及我们的方法在不同$\gamma$值下达到的总体评估时间指数。最后一行展示了实现本方法复杂度所需的$\epsilon$值。

<!-- Media -->

For instance,if $\gamma  = 1\frac{1}{2}$ ,the lazy approach requires $\mathcal{O}\left( {N + {N}^{1 + \frac{1}{2}} \cdot  N}\right)  = \mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{2 + \frac{1}{2}}\right)$ ,the eager approach requires $\mathcal{O}\left( {{N}^{2} + {N}^{1 + \frac{1}{2}} \cdot  1}\right)  = \mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{2}\right)$ ,and our approach needs only $\mathcal{O}\left( {{N}^{1 + \frac{3}{4}} + }\right.$ $\left. {{N}^{1 + \frac{1}{2}}{N}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\right)  = \mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \frac{3}{4}}\right)$ time at $\epsilon  = \frac{3}{4}$ . In case $\gamma$ is equal to $\frac{1}{2},1$ ,or $1\frac{1}{2}$ ,the overall computation time of our approach (highlighted in green) is strictly lower than the eager and lazy approaches. For the other two cases shown in the table, our approach recovers the lower complexity of the prior approaches (highlighted in yellow).

例如，若$\gamma  = 1\frac{1}{2}$，惰性方法需要$\mathcal{O}\left( {N + {N}^{1 + \frac{1}{2}} \cdot  N}\right)  = \mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{2 + \frac{1}{2}}\right)$，积极方法需要$\mathcal{O}\left( {{N}^{2} + {N}^{1 + \frac{1}{2}} \cdot  1}\right)  = \mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{2}\right)$，而我们的方法仅需$\mathcal{O}\left( {{N}^{1 + \frac{3}{4}} + }\right.$$\left. {{N}^{1 + \frac{1}{2}}{N}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\right)  = \mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \frac{3}{4}}\right)$时间（当$\epsilon  = \frac{3}{4}$时）。当$\gamma$等于$\frac{1}{2},1$或$1\frac{1}{2}$时，本方法的总计算时间（绿色高亮）严格低于积极与惰性方法。表中所示另外两种情况下，本方法恢复了先前方法的较低复杂度（黄色高亮）。

2.1. Further Prior Work on Static Query Evaluation. We complement our discussion with further prior work on static query evaluation. Figure 3 gives a taxonomy of works in this area.

2.1 静态查询评估的进一步先前工作。我们通过补充静态查询评估领域的更多先前研究来完善讨论。图3展示了该领域工作的分类体系。

Prior work exhibits a dependency between the space and enumeration delay for conjunctive queries with access patterns [DK18]. It constructs a succinct representation of the query result that allows for enumeration of tuples over some variables under value bindings for all other variables. It does not support enumeration for queries with projection, as addressed in our work. It also states Example 9.1 as an open problem.

先前研究揭示了带访问模式的合取查询在空间与枚举延迟间的依赖性[DK18]。该研究构建了查询结果的简洁表示，支持在绑定其他变量值的情况下枚举某些变量的元组。如我们工作所述，它不支持带投影操作的查询枚举，并将示例9.1列为未解决问题。

The result of any $\alpha$ -acyclic conjunctive query can be enumerated with constant delay after linear-time preprocessing if and only if it is free-connex. This is under the conjecture that Boolean multiplication of $n \times  n$ matrices cannot be done in $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ time [BDG07]. More recently, this was shown to hold also under the hypothesis that the existence of a triangle in a hypergraph of $n$ vertices cannot be tested in time $\mathcal{O}\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ and that for any $k$ ,testing the presence of a $k$ -dimensional tetrahedron cannot be decided in linear time [BB13]. The free-connex characterization generalizes in the presence of functional dependencies [CK18]. An in-depth pre-2015 overview on constant-delay enumeration is provided by Segoufin [Seg15].

当且仅当满足自由连接性时，任何$\alpha$非循环合取查询都可在线性时间预处理后实现常数延迟枚举——该结论基于$n \times  n$矩阵布尔乘法无法在$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$时间内完成[BDG07]的猜想。最新研究进一步表明，该结论还依赖于：在$n$顶点超图中检测三角形无法在$\mathcal{O}\left( {n}^{2}\right)$时间内完成，且对任意$k$维四面体的检测无法在线性时间[BB13]内判定。自由连接性特征在存在函数依赖[CK18]时仍成立。Segoufin[Seg15]提供了2015年前常数延迟枚举研究的深度综述。

There are also enumeration algorithms for document spanners [ABMN19] and satisfying valuations of circuits [ABJM17].

还存在针对文档解析器[ABMN19]和电路满足赋值[ABJM17]的枚举算法。

## 3. Trade-offs in Dynamic Query Evaluation

## 3. 动态查询评估中的权衡

Our main result for the dynamic query evaluation generalizes the static case.

我们在动态查询评估上的主要结果推广了静态情形。

Theorem 3.1. Given a hierarchical query with static width $\mathbf{w}$ and dynamic width $\delta$ ,a database of size $N$ ,and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,the query result can be enumerated with $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ delay after $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ preprocessing time and $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ amortized update time for single-tuple updates.

定理3.1. 给定具有静态宽度$\mathbf{w}$和动态宽度$\delta$的层次化查询、规模为$N$的数据库及$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$预处理时间和单元组更新$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$摊销更新时间后，能以$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$延迟枚举查询结果。

The left plot in Figure 1 depicts the preprocessing time (dotted green line), the update time (dashed red lines), and the enumeration delay (purple line) of our approach in the dynamic case. For hierarchical queries,the dynamic width $\delta$ can be equal to either the static width $w$ or $w - 1$ (Proposition 4.8). The plot hence shows two dashed red lines for the update time. The right plot in Figure 1 depicts the trade-off between the three components. Our approach can achieve sublinear amortized update time and delay for hierarchical queries with arbitrary free variables (Figure 1 left and right). For any $\epsilon  = \frac{1}{\delta  + \alpha } > 0$ with $\alpha  > 0$ ,our algorithm has update time $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \alpha  \cdot  \frac{1}{\delta  + \alpha }}\right)$ and delay $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \frac{1}{\delta  + \alpha }}\right)$ .

图1左图展示了动态情形下我们方法的预处理时间(绿色虚线)、更新时间(红色虚线)和枚举延迟(紫色实线)。对于层次化查询，动态宽度$\delta$可能等于静态宽度$w$或$w - 1$(命题4.8)，因此图中用两条红色虚线表示更新时间。图1右图描绘了三者间的权衡关系。对于含任意自由变量的层次化查询，我们的方法能实现次线性摊销更新时间与延迟(图1左右)。对任意满足$\alpha  > 0$的$\epsilon  = \frac{1}{\delta  + \alpha } > 0$，算法更新时间$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \alpha  \cdot  \frac{1}{\delta  + \alpha }}\right)$，延迟$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \frac{1}{\delta  + \alpha }}\right)$。

The update time for a single tuple is at most the preprocessing time: ${\delta \epsilon } \leq  \mathrm{w}\epsilon  \leq$ $\epsilon  + \left( {w - 1}\right) \epsilon  \leq  1 + \left( {w - 1}\right) \epsilon$ . If $\delta  = w - 1$ ,then ${\delta \epsilon } = \left( {w - 1}\right) \epsilon$ ,i.e.,the update time is an $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ factor less than the preprocessing time. The complexity of preprocessing thus amounts to inserting $N$ tuples in an initially empty database using our update mechanism. If $\delta  = \mathrm{w}$ ,then inserting $N$ tuples would need $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon  + \epsilon }\right)$ time,which is an $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$ factor more than the complexity of one bulk update using our preprocessing algorithm. This suggests a gap between single-tuple updates and bulk updates. A similar gap highlighting a fundamental limitation of single-tuple updates has been shown for the Loomis-Whitney query that generalizes the triangle query from a join of three binary relations to a join of $n\left( {n - 1}\right)$ -ary relations: The amortized update time for single-tuple updates is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1/2}\right)$ , which is worst-case optimal unless the Online Matrix-Vector Multiplication conjecture fails $\left\lbrack  {{\mathrm{{KNN}}}^{ + }{19}\mathrm{\;b}}\right\rbrack$ . Inserting $N$ tuples in the empty database would cost $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$ ,yet the query can be computed in the static setting in time $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{n}{n - 1}}\right)$ [NPRR18].

单条元组的更新时间最多等于预处理时间：${\delta \epsilon } \leq  \mathrm{w}\epsilon  \leq$ $\epsilon  + \left( {w - 1}\right) \epsilon  \leq  1 + \left( {w - 1}\right) \epsilon$。若$\delta  = w - 1$，则${\delta \epsilon } = \left( {w - 1}\right) \epsilon$，即更新时间比预处理时间少一个$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$因子。因此预处理复杂度相当于使用我们的更新机制在初始空数据库中插入$N$条元组。若$\delta  = \mathrm{w}$，插入$N$条元组将需要$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon  + \epsilon }\right)$时间，这比使用预处理算法进行批量更新的复杂度多出$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$因子。这表明单条更新与批量更新之间存在效率差距。类似地，在将三角形查询从三个二元关系到$n\left( {n - 1}\right)$元关系连接的Loomis-Whitney查询中，已证明单条更新的摊销更新时间为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1/2}\right)$——除非在线矩阵向量乘法猜想不成立$\left\lbrack  {{\mathrm{{KNN}}}^{ + }{19}\mathrm{\;b}}\right\rbrack$，否则该时间复杂度是最坏情况最优的。在空数据库中插入$N$条元组需要$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$代价，而静态环境下该查询可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{n}{n - 1}}\right)$时间内完成计算[NPRR18]。

Amortized $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ update time means that,given any sequence of updates,the average cost of a single update is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ . Since updates can change the data structure,our approach needs to do a rebalancing step whenever the data structure gets out of balance. The time needed for a single update without rebalancing is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ in the worst case. A rebalancing step can require super-linear time (Propositions 7.3 and 7.4). We show that for any update sequence, the overall time needed for the updates and rebalancing steps, when averaged over the number of updates in the sequence,remains $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ in the worst case (Proposition 7.5). Using classical de-amortization techniques [KP98], we can adapt our update mechanism to obtain non-amortized $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ update time. The de-amortization strategy is analogous to the one used for the update mechanism of triangle queries (Section 10 in $\left\lbrack  {{\mathrm{{KNN}}}^{ + }{20}}\right\rbrack$ ),which performs more frequent but less time-consuming rebalancing steps.

摊销$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$更新时间是指：给定任意更新序列，单次更新的平均成本为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$。由于更新会改变数据结构，当数据结构失衡时，我们的方法需执行再平衡步骤。在最坏情况下，无需再平衡的单次更新耗时为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$。再平衡步骤可能需要超线性时间（命题7.3和7.4）。我们证明对于任意更新序列，其更新与再平衡步骤的总时间按更新次数平均后，最坏情况下仍保持$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$（命题7.5）。采用经典的去摊销技术[KP98]，可调整更新机制实现非摊销的$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$更新时间。该去摊销策略类似于三角形查询更新机制（$\left\lbrack  {{\mathrm{{KNN}}}^{ + }{20}}\right\rbrack$第10节）所采用的方法，通过增加再平衡频率但降低单次耗时来实现。

Theorem 3.1 recovers prior work on conjunctive queries [NO18], free-connex acyclic queries[IUV17],and q-hierarchical queries [BKS17a] by setting $\epsilon  = 1$ (Figure 1 right). For hierarchical queries in general, our approach achieves the same complexities as prior work on conjunctive queries when restricted to hierarchical queries. For free-connex queries, we obtain linear-time preprocessing and update and constant-time delay since $\mathrm{w} = 1$ (Proposition 4.9) and then $\delta  \in  \{ 0,1\}$ (Proposition 4.8) for these queries. For q-hierarchical queries,we obtain linear-time preprocessing and constant-time update and delay since $\mathrm{w} = 1$ and $\delta  = 0$ . Existing maintenance approaches, e.g, classical first-order IVM [CY12] and higher-order recursive IVM $\left\lbrack  {{\mathrm{K}}^{ + }{14}}\right\rbrack$ ,DynYannakakis $\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{{IUV}}{17}}\right\rbrack$ ,and F-IVM $\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{{NO}}{18}}\right\rbrack$ ,can achieve constant delay for general hierarchical queries yet after at least linear-time updates. The right Venn diagram in Figure 2 relates Theorem 3.1 with prior results.

定理3.1通过设定$\epsilon  = 1$（图1右侧），复现了先前关于合取查询[NO18]、自由连接无环查询[IUV17]以及q-层次查询[BKS17a]的研究成果。对于一般层次查询，当限定在层次查询时，我们的方法实现了与合取查询前人工作相同的复杂度。对于自由连接查询，由于$\mathrm{w} = 1$（命题4.9）和$\delta  \in  \{ 0,1\}$（命题4.8），我们获得了线性时间预处理、更新以及常数时间延迟。对于q-层次查询，由于$\mathrm{w} = 1$和$\delta  = 0$，我们实现了线性时间预处理和常数时间更新与延迟。现有维护方法如经典一阶IVM[CY12]、高阶递归IVM$\left\lbrack  {{\mathrm{K}}^{ + }{14}}\right\rbrack$、DynYannakakis$\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{{IUV}}{17}}\right\rbrack$和F-IVM$\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{{NO}}{18}}\right\rbrack$虽然能在至少线性时间更新后实现通用层次查询的常数延迟，但图2右侧维恩图展示了定理3.1与先前成果的关系。

The next example illustrates that our approach achieves better overall evaluation time than existing approaches when considering a sequence of updates.

下例表明，在考虑更新序列时，我们的方法能获得比现有方法更优的整体评估时间。

Example 3.2. Let us consider the (free-connex hierarchical) query $Q\left( A\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( B\right)$ . The query has static and dynamic width 1 . We assume that the input relations are of size $N$ and consider the dynamic setting.

示例3.2. 考虑（自由连接层次）查询$Q\left( A\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( B\right)$。该查询具有静态和动态宽度1。假设输入关系规模为$N$，并考察动态场景。

A lazy evaluation approach requires no preprocessing: For each single-tuple update, it only updates the input relations without propagating the changes to the query result. Before enumerating the $A$ -values in the query result,it first scans the relation $R$ to collect all $A$ -values that are paired with $B$ -values contained in $S$ . This takes linear time. Afterwards, the approach can enumerate the $A$ -values with constant delay.

惰性求值法无需预处理：对于每个单元组更新，它仅更新输入关系而不将变更传播到查询结果。在枚举查询结果中的$A$值前，该方法先扫描关系$R$以收集所有与$S$所含$B$值配对的$A$值，耗时线性。随后可以常数延迟枚举$A$值。

An eager evaluation approach precomputes the initial result in linear time. On a single-tuple update, it computes the delta query obtained by fixing the variables of one relation to constants. For an update ${\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right)$ to $R$ ,the delta query ${\delta Q}\left( a\right)  = {\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right) ,S\left( b\right)$ can be computed in constant time. For an update ${\delta S}\left( b\right)$ to $S$ ,the delta query ${\delta Q}\left( A\right)  = R\left( {A,b}\right) ,{\delta S}\left( b\right)$ can be computed in linear time. In general, the update time is linear. Since the query result is materialized and eagerly maintained,the $A$ -values in the result can be enumerated after an update with constant delay.

积极求值法以线性时间预计算初始结果。对于单元组更新，它通过将某关系的变量固定为常量来计算增量查询。更新$R$的${\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right)$时，增量查询${\delta Q}\left( a\right)  = {\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right) ,S\left( b\right)$可常数时间计算；更新$S$的${\delta S}\left( b\right)$时，增量查询${\delta Q}\left( A\right)  = R\left( {A,b}\right) ,{\delta S}\left( b\right)$需线性时间计算。总体更新时间为线性。由于物化并积极维护查询结果，更新后可常数延迟枚举结果中的$A$值。

For this query,our approach achieves $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ preprocessing time, $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$ update time, and $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ enumeration delay for any $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ . The following table summarizes the preprocessing-update-delay trade-off achieved by the three approaches:

对于该查询，我们的方法实现$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$预处理时间、$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$更新时间和$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$枚举延迟（对任意$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$）。下表总结三种方法的预处理-更新-延迟权衡：

Our approach recovers the lazy and eager approaches by setting $\epsilon$ to 0 and respectively 1 , with one exception: it cannot recover the constant preprocessing time in the lazy approach as it requires one pass over the input data.

我们的方法通过将$\epsilon$分别设为0和1来恢复惰性(lazy)和积极(eager)两种处理方式，但存在一个例外：由于需要对输入数据进行一次遍历，该方法无法恢复惰性处理方式中恒定的预处理时间。

Consider now a sequence of ${N}^{m}$ updates,each followed by one access request to enumerate ${N}^{\gamma }$ values out of the at most ${NA}$ -values in the query result,for $m,\gamma  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ . With the eager and lazy approaches, this sequence takes time (excluding preprocessing) $\mathcal{O}\left( {{N}^{m}\left( {N + {N}^{\gamma }}\right) }\right)$ ,which is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{m + 1}\right)$ since $\gamma  \leq  1$ . With our approach,the sequence takes $\mathcal{O}\left( {{N}^{m}\left( {{N}^{\epsilon } + {N}^{\gamma }{N}^{1 - \epsilon }}\right) }\right)  = \mathcal{O}\left( {{N}^{m + \epsilon } + {N}^{m + \gamma  + 1 - \epsilon }}\right)$ . Depending on the values of $m$ and $\gamma$ ,we can tune our approach (by appropriately setting $\epsilon$ ) to minimize the overall time to execute the bulk of updates and access requests. For $\gamma  < 1$ and any $m$ ,our approach has consistently lower complexity than the lazy/eager approaches,while for $\gamma  = 1$ and any $m$ it matches that of the lazy/eager approaches. The complexity of processing the sequence of updates and access requests is shown in the next table for various values of $m$ and $\gamma$ :

现在考虑一个包含${N}^{m}$次更新的序列，每次更新后跟随一个访问请求以枚举查询结果中最多${NA}$个值中的${N}^{\gamma }$个值（针对$m,\gamma  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$）。采用积极和惰性方法时，该序列耗时（不含预处理）为$\mathcal{O}\left( {{N}^{m}\left( {N + {N}^{\gamma }}\right) }\right)$，由于$\gamma  \leq  1$，该值为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{m + 1}\right)$。而使用我们的方法时，序列耗时为$\mathcal{O}\left( {{N}^{m}\left( {{N}^{\epsilon } + {N}^{\gamma }{N}^{1 - \epsilon }}\right) }\right)  = \mathcal{O}\left( {{N}^{m + \epsilon } + {N}^{m + \gamma  + 1 - \epsilon }}\right)$。根据$m$和$\gamma$的取值，我们可以通过合理设置$\epsilon$来调优方法，从而最小化执行批量更新和访问请求的总耗时。当$\gamma  < 1$且$m$为任意值时，我们的方法始终具有低于惰性/积极方法的复杂度；而当$\gamma  = 1$且$m$为任意值时，其复杂度与惰性/积极方法相当。下表展示了针对不同$m$和$\gamma$值时，处理更新和访问请求序列的复杂度：

<!-- Media -->

3.1. Further Prior Work on Dynamic Query Evaluation. We discuss further prior work on dynamic query evaluation. Figure 4 gives a taxonomy of works in this field.

3.1 动态查询评估的进一步先前研究。我们讨论动态查询评估的其他现有成果。图4给出了该领域研究的分类体系。

The q-hierarchical queries are the conjunctive queries that admit linear-time preprocessing and constant-time update and delay [BKS17a, IUV17]. If a conjunctive query without repeating relation symbols is not q-hierarchical,there is no $\gamma  > 0$ such that the query result can be enumerated with $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{1}{2} - \gamma }\right)$ delay and update time,unless the Online Matrix Vector Multiplication conjecture fails. The constant delay and update time carry over to first-order queries with modulo-counting quantifiers on bounded degree databases, unions of q-hierarchical queries [BKS18], and q-hierarchical queries with small domain constraints [BKS17b].

q-层次化查询是指允许线性时间预处理、常数时间更新和延迟的合取查询[BKS17a, IUV17]。若不含重复关系符号的合取查询非q-层次化，则不存在$\gamma  > 0$使得查询结果能以$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{1}{2} - \gamma }\right)$延迟和更新时间枚举——除非在线矩阵向量乘法猜想不成立。这种常数延迟和更新时间可推广到有限度数据库上带模计数量词的一阶查询、q-层次化查询的并集[BKS18]，以及小域约束的q-层次化查询[BKS17b]。

Prior work characterizes the preprocessing-space-update trade-off for counting triangles under updates $\left\lbrack  {{\mathrm{{KNN}}}^{ + }{19}\mathrm{a}}\right\rbrack$ . A follow-up work generalizes this approach to the triangle queries with arbitrary free variables,adding the enumeration delay to the trade-off space $\left\lbrack  {{\mathrm{{KNN}}}^{ + }{20}}\right\rbrack$ . In this work, we consider arbitrary hierarchical queries instead of the triangle queries, and we use a less trivial adaptive maintenance technique, where the same relation may be subject to partition on different tuples of variables and where the overall number of cases for each partition is reduced to only two: the all-light case and the at-least-one-heavy case.

先前研究刻画了更新$\left\lbrack  {{\mathrm{{KNN}}}^{ + }{19}\mathrm{a}}\right\rbrack$下三角形计数在预处理-空间-更新之间的权衡。后续工作将该方法推广至含任意自由变量的三角形查询，将枚举延迟纳入权衡空间$\left\lbrack  {{\mathrm{{KNN}}}^{ + }{20}}\right\rbrack$。本文考虑任意层次化查询而非仅三角形查询，并采用更精细的自适应维护技术——同一关系可能按不同变量元组分区，且每种分区的总情况数缩减至两种：全轻量情况与至少一重量情况。

MSO queries on strings admit linear-time preprocessing, constant delay, and logarithmic update time. Here, updates can relabel, insert, or remove positions in the string. Further work considers MSO queries on trees under updates [LM14, ABM18].

字符串上的MSO查询允许线性时间预处理、常数延迟和对数更新时间。此处更新可重标记、插入或删除字符串位置。进一步研究考虑树结构在更新下的MSO查询[LM14, ABM18]。

DBToaster $\left\lbrack  {{\mathrm{K}}^{ + }{14}}\right\rbrack$ ,F-IVM $\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{{NO}}{18}}\right\rbrack$ ,and DynYannakakis $\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{{IUV}}{17},{\mathrm{{IUV}}}^{ + }{18}}\right\rbrack$ are recent systems implementing incremental view maintenance approaches.

DBToaster$\left\lbrack  {{\mathrm{K}}^{ + }{14}}\right\rbrack$、F-IVM$\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{{NO}}{18}}\right\rbrack$和DynYannakakis$\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{{IUV}}{17},{\mathrm{{IUV}}}^{ + }{18}}\right\rbrack$是近期实现增量视图维护方法的系统。

## 4. Preliminaries

## 4. 预备知识

Data Model. A schema $\mathcal{X} = \left( {{X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{n}}\right)$ is a non-empty tuple of distinct variables. Each variable ${X}_{i}$ has a discrete domain $\operatorname{Dom}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$ . We treat schemas and sets of variables interchangeably,assuming a fixed ordering of variables. A tuple $\mathbf{x}$ of data values over schema $\mathcal{X}$ is an element from $\operatorname{Dom}\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  = \operatorname{Dom}\left( {X}_{1}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  \operatorname{Dom}\left( {X}_{n}\right)$ .

数据模型。模式$\mathcal{X} = \left( {{X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{n}}\right)$是由不同变量组成的非空元组。每个变量${X}_{i}$有离散域$\operatorname{Dom}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$。我们将模式与变量集视为可互换（假定变量固定排序）。模式$\mathcal{X}$上的数据值元组$\mathbf{x}$是$\operatorname{Dom}\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  = \operatorname{Dom}\left( {X}_{1}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  \operatorname{Dom}\left( {X}_{n}\right)$的元素。

A relation $R$ over schema $\mathcal{X}$ is a function $R : \operatorname{Dom}\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \rightarrow  \mathbb{Z}$ such that the multiplicity $R\left( \mathbf{x}\right)$ is non-zero for finitely many tuples $\mathbf{x}$ . A tuple $\mathbf{x}$ is in $R$ ,denoted by $\mathbf{x} \in  R$ ,if $R\left( \mathbf{x}\right)  \neq  0$ . The notation $\exists R$ denotes the use of $R$ with set semantics: $\exists R\left( \mathbf{x}\right)$ equals 1 if $\mathbf{x} \in  R$ and 0 otherwise; also, $\nexists R\left( \mathbf{x}\right)  = 1 - \exists R\left( \mathbf{x}\right)$ . The size $\left| R\right|$ of $R$ is the size of the set $\{ \mathbf{x} \mid  \mathbf{x} \in  R\}$ . A database is a set of relations and has size given by the sum of the sizes of its relations.

在模式$\mathcal{X}$上的关系$R$是一个函数$R : \operatorname{Dom}\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \rightarrow  \mathbb{Z}$，其重数$R\left( \mathbf{x}\right)$对于有限多个元组$\mathbf{x}$非零。若$R\left( \mathbf{x}\right)  \neq  0$，则称元组$\mathbf{x}$属于$R$，记作$\mathbf{x} \in  R$。符号$\exists R$表示采用集合语义的$R$用法：当$\mathbf{x} \in  R$时$\exists R\left( \mathbf{x}\right)$等于1，否则为0；且$\nexists R\left( \mathbf{x}\right)  = 1 - \exists R\left( \mathbf{x}\right)$。关系$R$的大小$\left| R\right|$即集合<b11></b1>的基数。数据库是关系的集合，其大小为各关系大小之和。

Given a tuple $\mathbf{x}$ over schema $\mathcal{X}$ and $\mathcal{S} \subseteq  \mathcal{X},\mathbf{x}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$ denotes the restriction of $\mathbf{x}$ to $\mathcal{S}$ such that the values in $\mathbf{x}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$ follow the ordering in $\mathcal{S}$ . For instance, $\left( {a,b,c}\right) \left\lbrack  \left( {C,A}\right) \right\rbrack   = \left( {c,a}\right)$ for the tuple(a,b,c)over the schema(A,B,C). For a relation $R$ over $\mathcal{X}$ ,schema $\mathcal{S} \subseteq  \mathcal{X}$ ,and tuple $\mathbf{t} \in  \operatorname{Dom}\left( \mathcal{S}\right) ,{\sigma }_{\mathcal{S} = \mathbf{t}}R = \{ \mathbf{x} \mid  \mathbf{x} \in  R \land  \mathbf{x}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack   = \mathbf{t}\}$ denotes the set of tuples in $R$ that agree with $\mathbf{t}$ on the variables in $\mathcal{S}$ ,while ${\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}R = \{ \mathbf{x}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack   \mid  \mathbf{x} \in  R\}$ denotes the set of restrictions of the tuples in $R$ to the variables in $\mathcal{S}$ .

给定模式$\mathcal{X}$上的元组$\mathbf{x}$，$\mathcal{S} \subseteq  \mathcal{X},\mathbf{x}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$表示将$\mathbf{x}$限制到$\mathcal{S}$的子集，使得$\mathbf{x}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$中的值遵循$\mathcal{S}$的排序。例如，模式(A,B,C)上元组(a,b,c)的$\left( {a,b,c}\right) \left\lbrack  \left( {C,A}\right) \right\rbrack   = \left( {c,a}\right)$。对于模式$\mathcal{S} \subseteq  \mathcal{X}$上关系$R$，元组$\mathbf{t} \in  \operatorname{Dom}\left( \mathcal{S}\right) ,{\sigma }_{\mathcal{S} = \mathbf{t}}R = \{ \mathbf{x} \mid  \mathbf{x} \in  R \land  \mathbf{x}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack   = \mathbf{t}\}$表示$R$中与$\mathbf{t}$在$\mathcal{S}$变量上一致的元组集合，而${\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}R = \{ \mathbf{x}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack   \mid  \mathbf{x} \in  R\}$表示$R$中元组限制到$\mathcal{S}$变量的投影集合。

Computational Model. We consider the RAM model of computation where schemas and data values are of constant size. We assume that each relation $R$ over schema $\mathcal{X}$ is implemented by a data structure that stores key-value entries $\left( {\mathbf{x},R\left( \mathbf{x}\right) }\right)$ for each tuple $\mathbf{x}$ with $R\left( \mathbf{x}\right)  \neq  0$ and needs $O\left( \left| R\right| \right)$ space. This data structure can: (1) look up,insert,and delete entries in constant time,(2) enumerate all stored entries in $R$ with constant delay, and (3) report $\left| R\right|$ in constant time. For a schema $\mathcal{S} \subset  \mathcal{X}$ ,we use an index data structure that for any $\mathbf{t} \in  \operatorname{Dom}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ can: (4) enumerate all tuples in ${\sigma }_{\mathcal{S} = \mathbf{t}}R$ with constant delay,(5) check $\mathbf{t} \in  {\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}R$ in constant time; (6) return $\left| {{\sigma }_{\mathcal{S} = \mathbf{t}}R}\right|$ in constant time; and (7) insert and delete index entries in constant time.

计算模型。我们采用RAM计算模型，其中模式与数据值均为常量大小。假设每个基于模式$\mathcal{X}$的关系$R$通过以下数据结构实现：存储键值项$\left( {\mathbf{x},R\left( \mathbf{x}\right) }\right)$对应每个元组$\mathbf{x}$（需满足$R\left( \mathbf{x}\right)  \neq  0$条件），占用$O\left( \left| R\right| \right)$空间。该数据结构支持：(1) 常数时间内完成查找、插入及删除操作；(2) 以恒定延迟枚举$R$中所有存储项；(3) 常数时间返回$\left| R\right|$。对于模式$\mathcal{S} \subset  \mathcal{X}$，索引数据结构可：(4) 以恒定延迟枚举${\sigma }_{\mathcal{S} = \mathbf{t}}R$中所有元组；(5) 常数时间验证$\mathbf{t} \in  {\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}R$；(6) 常数时间返回$\left| {{\sigma }_{\mathcal{S} = \mathbf{t}}R}\right|$；(7) 常数时间完成索引项的插入与删除。

In an idealized setting, the above requirements can be ensured using hashing. In practice, hashing can only achieve amortized constant time for some of the above operations. In our paper, whenever we claim constant time for hash operations, we mean amortized constant time. We give a hash-based example data structure that supports the above operations in amortized constant time. Consider a relation $R$ over schema $\mathcal{X}$ . A hash table with chaining stores key-value entries $\left( {\mathbf{x},R\left( \mathbf{x}\right) }\right)$ for each tuple $\mathbf{x}$ over $\mathcal{X}$ with $R\left( \mathbf{x}\right)  \neq  0$ . The entries are doubly linked to support enumeration with constant delay. The hash table can report the number of its entries in constant time and supports lookups, inserts, and deletes in amortized constant time. To support index operations on a schema $\mathcal{F} \subset  \mathcal{X}$ ,we create another hash table with chaining where each table entry stores an $\mathcal{F}$ -value $\mathbf{t}$ as key and a doubly-linked list of pointers to the entries in $R$ having $\mathbf{t}$ as $\mathcal{F}$ -value. Looking up an index entry given $\mathbf{t}$ takes amortized constant time,and its doubly-linked list enables enumeration of the matching entries in $R$ with constant delay. Inserting an index entry into the hash table additionally prepends a new pointer to the doubly-linked list for a given $\mathbf{t}$ ; overall,this operation takes amortized constant time. For efficient deletion of index entries, each entry in $R$ also stores back-pointers to its index entries (one back-pointer per index for $R$ ). When an entry is deleted from $R$ ,locating and deleting its index entries in doubly-linked lists takes constant time per index. An alternative data structure that can meet our requirements is a tree-structured index such as a ${\mathrm{B}}^{ + }$ -tree. This would,however,require worst-case logarithmic time and imply an additional logarithmic factor in our complexity results.

理想情况下，上述要求可通过哈希技术实现。实际应用中，哈希仅能对部分操作达成均摊常数时间复杂度。本文中提及哈希操作的常数时间均指均摊常数时间。我们给出基于哈希的示例数据结构，支持均摊常数时间完成上述操作。考虑模式$\mathcal{X}$上的关系$R$：采用链式哈希表存储键值项$\left( {\mathbf{x},R\left( \mathbf{x}\right) }\right)$（对应$\mathcal{X}$模式中满足$R\left( \mathbf{x}\right)  \neq  0$的元组$\mathbf{x}$），通过双向链表支持恒定延迟枚举。该哈希表可常数时间返回条目数量，并支持均摊常数时间的查找、插入与删除。为支持模式$\mathcal{F} \subset  \mathcal{X}$的索引操作，另建链式哈希表：每个表项以$\mathcal{F}$值$\mathbf{t}$为键，存储指向$R$中对应$\mathbf{t}$值的双向指针链表。给定$\mathbf{t}$查找索引项耗时均摊常数，其双向链表实现匹配项的恒定延迟枚举。索引项插入操作需在给定$\mathbf{t}$的双向链表首部新增指针，整体耗时保持均摊常数。为实现高效索引删除，$R$中每个条目还存储指向其索引项的反向指针（每个索引对应一个反向指针）。当从$R$删除条目时，定位并删除双向链表中的索引项耗时与索引数量成线性关系。另一种满足要求的数据结构是树形索引（如${\mathrm{B}}^{ + }$树），但此类结构需要最坏情况对数时间，会导致复杂度结果额外增加对数因子。

Modeling Updates Using Multiplicities. We restrict multiplicities of tuples in the input relations to be strictly positive. Multiplicity 0 means the tuple is not present. A single-tuple update to a relation $R$ is expressed as ${\delta R} = \{ \mathbf{x} \rightarrow  m\}$ . The update is an insert of the tuple $\mathbf{x}$ in $R$ if the multiplicity $m$ is strictly positive. It is a delete of $\mathbf{x}$ from $R$ if $m$ is negative. Such a delete is rejected if the existing multiplicity of $\mathbf{x}$ in $R$ is less than $\left| m\right|$ . A batch update may consist of both inserts and deletes. Applying ${\delta R}$ to $R$ means creating a new version of $R$ that is the union of ${\delta R}$ and $R$ .

基于多重性的模型更新方法。我们将输入关系中元组的多重性限制为严格正值。多重性为0表示该元组不存在。对关系$R$的单条元组更新表示为${\delta R} = \{ \mathbf{x} \rightarrow  m\}$。当多重性$m$为严格正值时，该更新表示向$R$中插入元组$\mathbf{x}$；若$m$为负值，则表示从$R$中删除$\mathbf{x}$。若$\mathbf{x}$在$R$中的现有多重性小于$\left| m\right|$，则拒绝执行删除操作。批量更新可同时包含插入和删除操作。将${\delta R}$应用于$R$意味着创建$R$的新版本，即${\delta R}$与$R$的并集。

Partitioning. The number of occurrences of a value in a relation is called the degree of the value in the relation. We partition relations based on value degree.

数据分区。关系中某个值的出现次数称为该值在关系中的度。我们基于值的度对关系进行分区。

Definition 4.1. Given a relation $R$ over schema $\mathcal{X}$ ,a schema $\mathcal{S} \subset  \mathcal{X}$ ,and a threshold $\theta$ ,the pair(H,L)of relations is a partition of $R$ on $\mathcal{S}$ with threshold $\theta$ if it satisfies the following four conditions:

定义4.1。给定模式$\mathcal{X}$上的关系$R$、模式$\mathcal{S} \subset  \mathcal{X}$及阈值$\theta$，若关系对(H,L)满足以下四个条件，则称其为$R$在$\mathcal{S}$上基于阈值$\theta$的划分：

$$
\text{(union)}R\left( \mathbf{x}\right)  = H\left( \mathbf{x}\right)  + L\left( \mathbf{x}\right) \text{for}\mathbf{x} \in  \operatorname{Dom}\left( \mathcal{X}\right) 
$$

(domain partition) ${\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}H \cap  {\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}L = \varnothing$

(定义域划分) ${\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}H \cap  {\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}L = \varnothing$

$$
\text{(heavy part) for all}\mathbf{t} \in  {\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}H : \left| {{\sigma }_{\mathcal{S} = \mathbf{t}}H}\right|  \geq  \frac{1}{2}\theta 
$$

$$
\text{(light part) for all}\mathbf{t} \in  {\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}L : \left| {{\sigma }_{\mathcal{S} = \mathbf{t}}L}\right|  < \frac{3}{2}\theta 
$$

The pair(H,L)is a strict partition of $R$ on $\mathcal{S}$ with threshold $\theta$ if it satisfies the union and domain partition conditions and strict versions of the heavy and light part conditions:

若关系对(H,L)满足并集条件、定义域划分条件及严格版的重部与轻部条件，则称其为$R$在$\mathcal{S}$上基于阈值$\theta$的严格划分：

(strict heavy part) for all $\mathbf{t} \in  {\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}H : \left| {{\sigma }_{\mathcal{S} = \mathbf{t}}H}\right|  \geq  \theta$

(严格重部条件) 对所有$\mathbf{t} \in  {\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}H : \left| {{\sigma }_{\mathcal{S} = \mathbf{t}}H}\right|  \geq  \theta$

(strict light part) for all $\mathbf{t} \in  {\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}L : \left| {{\sigma }_{\mathcal{S} = \mathbf{t}}L}\right|  < \theta$

(严格轻部条件) 对所有$\mathbf{t} \in  {\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}L : \left| {{\sigma }_{\mathcal{S} = \mathbf{t}}L}\right|  < \theta$

The relations $H$ and $L$ are the heavy and light parts of $R$ .

关系$H$与$L$分别构成$R$的重部和轻部。

Assuming $\left| R\right|  = N$ and the strict partition(H,L)of $R$ on $\mathcal{S}$ with threshold $\theta  = {N}^{\epsilon }$ for $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,we have: $\forall \mathbf{t} \in  {\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}L : \left| {{\sigma }_{\mathcal{S} = \mathbf{t}}L}\right|  < \theta  = {N}^{\epsilon }$ ; and $\left| {{\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}H}\right|  \leq  \frac{\left| R\right| }{\theta } = {N}^{1 - \epsilon }$ . We subsequently denote the light part of $R$ on $\mathcal{S}$ by ${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$ .

假设$\left| R\right|  = N$成立，且(H,L)为$R$在$\mathcal{S}$上针对$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$基于阈值$\theta  = {N}^{\epsilon }$的严格划分，则有：$\forall \mathbf{t} \in  {\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}L : \left| {{\sigma }_{\mathcal{S} = \mathbf{t}}L}\right|  < \theta  = {N}^{\epsilon }$；且$\left| {{\pi }_{\mathcal{S}}H}\right|  \leq  \frac{\left| R\right| }{\theta } = {N}^{1 - \epsilon }$。后续我们将$R$在$\mathcal{S}$上的轻部记作${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$。

Queries. A conjunctive query (CQ) has the form

查询处理。合取查询(CQ)具有如下形式：

$$
Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  = {R}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{X}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{n}\left( {\mathcal{X}}_{n}\right) .
$$

We denote by: ${\left( {R}_{i}\right) }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }$ the relation symbols; ${\left( {R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right) \right) }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }$ the atoms; $\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)  = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }}{\mathcal{X}}_{i}$ the set of variables; free $\left( Q\right)  = \mathcal{F} \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ the set of free variables; bound $\left( Q\right)  = \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)  -$ free(Q)the set of bound variables; atoms $\left( Q\right)  = \left\{  {{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)  \mid  i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }\right\}$ the set of the atoms; and atoms(X)the set of the atoms containing $X$ . The query $Q$ is full if $\operatorname{free}\left( Q\right)  = \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ .

我们用以下符号表示：${\left( {R}_{i}\right) }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }$关系符号；${\left( {R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right) \right) }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }$原子；$\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)  = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }}{\mathcal{X}}_{i}$变量集合；自由变量$\left( Q\right)  = \mathcal{F} \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$表示自由变量集合；约束变量$\left( Q\right)  = \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)  -$free(Q)表示约束变量集合；原子$\left( Q\right)  = \left\{  {{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{X}}_{i}\right)  \mid  i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }\right\}$表示原子集合；而atoms(X)表示包含$X$的原子集合。当$\operatorname{free}\left( Q\right)  = \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$时，查询$Q$是完整的。

The hypergraph $G = \left( {\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) ,\operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }\right)$ of a query $Q$ has one node per variable and one hyperedge per atom that covers all nodes representing its variables. A join tree for $Q$ is a tree with the following properties: (1) Its nodes are exactly the atoms of $Q$ ; (2) if any two nodes have variables in common, then all nodes along the path between them also have these variables. The query $Q$ is called $\alpha$ -acyclic if it has a join tree. It is free-connex if it is $\alpha$ -acyclic and remains $\alpha$ -acyclic when we add to its body a fresh atom over its free variables [BB13]. It is hierarchical if for any two of its variables, either their sets of atoms are disjoint or one is contained in the other. It is $q$ -hierarchical if it is hierarchical and for every variable $A \in  \operatorname{free}\left( Q\right)$ ,if there is a variable $B$ such that atoms $\left( A\right)  \subset  \operatorname{atoms}\left( B\right)$ then $B \in$ free(Q)[BKS17a].

查询$Q$的超图$G = \left( {\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) ,\operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }\right)$为每个变量设一个节点，每个原子设一条覆盖其所有变量节点的超边。对于$Q$的连接树需满足：(1)其节点恰好是$Q$的原子；(2)若任意两节点含有共同变量，则它们路径上所有节点都包含这些变量。若存在连接树，则称查询$Q$为$\alpha$无环的。若它既是$\alpha$无环的，又能在其主体中添加自由变量的新原子后保持$\alpha$无环性[BB13]，则称为自由连通。若任意两个变量的原子集合要么互不相交，要么一个包含另一个，则称为层次化。若它既是层次化的，且对每个变量$A \in  \operatorname{free}\left( Q\right)$，存在变量$B$使得atoms$\left( A\right)  \subset  \operatorname{atoms}\left( B\right)$蕴含$B \in$free(Q)[BKS17a]，则称为$q$层次化。

Example 4.2. The following query is $\alpha$ -acyclic:

示例4.2 下列查询是$\alpha$无环的：

$$
Q\left( {A,C,F}\right)  = R\left( {A,B,C}\right) ,S\left( {A,B,D}\right) ,T\left( {A,E,F}\right) ,U\left( {A,E,G}\right) 
$$

A join tree is the path $U\left( {AEG}\right)  - T\left( {AEF}\right)  - R\left( {ABC}\right)  - S\left( {ABD}\right)$ . It is free-connex since we can extend this join tree as follows: $U\left( {AEG}\right)  - T\left( {AEF}\right)  - Q\left( {ACF}\right)  - R\left( {ABC}\right)  - S\left( {ABD}\right)$ .

连接树即路径$U\left( {AEG}\right)  - T\left( {AEF}\right)  - R\left( {ABC}\right)  - S\left( {ABD}\right)$。由于可如下扩展该连接树：$U\left( {AEG}\right)  - T\left( {AEF}\right)  - Q\left( {ACF}\right)  - R\left( {ABC}\right)  - S\left( {ABD}\right)$，故其为自由连通。

Figure 5. Canonical (left) and non-canonical but free-top (right) variable order for the query $Q\left( {A,C,F}\right)  = R\left( {A,B,C}\right) ,S\left( {A,B,D}\right) ,T\left( {A,E,F}\right)$ , $U\left( {A,E,G}\right)$ in Example 4.2. Free variables are underlined.

图5. 示例4.2中查询$Q\left( {A,C,F}\right)  = R\left( {A,B,C}\right) ,S\left( {A,B,D}\right) ,T\left( {A,E,F}\right)$、$U\left( {A,E,G}\right)$的规范（左）与非规范但自由优先（右）变量顺序。自由变量带下划线标注。

<!-- Media -->

It is also hierarchical but not q-hierarchical: The bound variables $B$ and $E$ dominate the free variables $C$ and respectively $F$ .

该查询虽为层次化但非q层次化：约束变量$B$与$E$分别支配自由变量$C$和$F$。

Variable Orders. Two variables depend on each other if they occur in the same atom.

变量顺序。若两个变量出现在同一原子中，则称它们相互依赖。

Definition 4.3 (adapted from [OZ15]). A variable order $\omega$ for a conjunctive query $Q$ is a pair $\left( {T,{de}{p}_{\omega }}\right)$ such that the following holds:

定义4.3（改编自[OZ15]）。对于合取查询$Q$而言，变量序$\omega$是一个二元组$\left( {T,{de}{p}_{\omega }}\right)$，需满足以下条件：

- $T$ is a rooted forest with one node per variable in $Q$ . The variables of each atom in $Q$ lie along the same root-to-leaf path in $T$ .

- $T$是以$Q$中每个变量为节点的有根森林。$Q$中每个原子的变量在$T$中必须位于同一条根到叶的路径上。

- The function ${\operatorname{dep}}_{\omega }$ maps each variable $X$ to the subset of its ancestor variables in $T$ on which the variables in the subtree rooted at $X$ depend,i.e.,for every variable $Y$ that is a child of variable $X,{\operatorname{dep}}_{\omega }\left( Y\right)  \subseteq  {\operatorname{dep}}_{\omega }\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$ .

- 函数${\operatorname{dep}}_{\omega }$将每个变量$X$映射到其祖先变量子集$T$，该子集中的变量是以$X$为根的子树的依赖变量。即对于作为变量$X,{\operatorname{dep}}_{\omega }\left( Y\right)  \subseteq  {\operatorname{dep}}_{\omega }\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$子节点的每个变量$Y$。

An extended variable order is a variable order where we add as new leaves the atoms corresponding to relations. We add each atom as the child of its variable placed lowest in the variable order. Whenever we refer to a variable order, we mean its extension with atoms at leaves. For ease of presentation,we often use $\omega$ to refer to the tree of $\omega$ .

扩展变量序是在原变量序基础上，将关系对应的原子作为新叶节点加入。每个原子作为其在变量序中最低位置变量的子节点加入。本文所述变量序均指带叶节点原子的扩展版本。为表述简洁，常用$\omega$指代$\omega$树结构。

The subtree of a variable order $\omega$ rooted at $X$ is denoted by ${\omega }_{X}$ . The sets vars $\left( \omega \right)$ , atoms $\left( \omega \right)$ ,and anc(X)consist of all variables of $\omega$ ,the atoms at the leaves of $\omega$ ,and the variables on the path from $X$ to the root excluding $X$ ,respectively. The flag has_sibling(X) is true if $X$ has siblings. The variable order $\omega$ is free-top if no bound variable is an ancestor of a free variable (called d-tree extension [OZ15]). It is canonical if the variables of the leaf atom of each root-to-leaf path are the inner nodes of the path. The sets freeTopVO(Q), canon $\operatorname{VO}\left( Q\right)$ ,and $\operatorname{VO}\left( Q\right)$ consist of free-top,canonical,and all variable orders of $Q$ .

以$X$为根的变量序$\omega$子树记作${\omega }_{X}$。集合vars$\left( \omega \right)$、atoms$\left( \omega \right)$和anc(X)分别表示$\omega$的所有变量、叶节点原子，以及从$X$到根节点路径上（不含$X$本身）的变量。当$X$存在兄弟节点时，标志位has_sibling(X)为真。若变量序$\omega$中无约束变量是自由变量的祖先（称为d-树扩展[OZ15]），则称其为自由顶序。当每条根到叶路径的叶节点原子变量构成该路径内部节点时，称该变量序为规范序。集合freeTopVO(Q)、canon$\operatorname{VO}\left( Q\right)$和$\operatorname{VO}\left( Q\right)$分别表示$Q$的自由顶序、规范序及所有变量序。

Example 4.4. The left variable order in Figure 5 is a canonical variable order for the query from Example 4.2. This variable order is not free-top since the bound variables $B$ and $E$ sit on top of the free variables $C$ and respectively $F$ . The right variable order in Figure 5 is a free-top variable order for the query. This variable order is not canonical: the atom at the leaf of the path $A - C - B - D - S\left( {ABD}\right)$ does not have the variable $C$ .

示例4.4。图5左侧变量序是示例4.2查询的规范变量序，但因约束变量$B$和$E$位于自由变量$C$和$F$之上，故非自由顶序。图5右侧变量序是该查询的自由顶序，但路径$A - C - B - D - S\left( {ABD}\right)$叶节点原子不含变量$C$，故非规范序。

Hierarchical queries admit canonical variable orders, while q-hierarchical queries admit canonical free-top variable orders. The canonical variable order of a hierarchical query is unique up to orderings of variables sharing the same set of atoms.

层次化查询允许规范变量序存在，而q-层次化查询允许规范自由顶序存在。层次化查询的规范变量序在共享相同原子集的变量排序上具有唯一性。

Width Measures. Given a conjunctive query $Q$ and $\mathcal{F} \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ ,a fractional edge cover of $\mathcal{F}$ is a solution $\mathbf{\lambda } = {\left( {\lambda }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right) }\right) }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }$ to the following linear program [AGM13]:

宽度度量。给定合取查询$Q$和$\mathcal{F} \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$，$\mathcal{F}$的分数边覆盖是以下线性规划[AGM13]的解$\mathbf{\lambda } = {\left( {\lambda }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right) }\right) }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }$：

minimize $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }}{\lambda }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right) }$

最小化$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }}{\lambda }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right) }$

$$
\text{subject to}\;\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) \text{ s.t. }X \in  \mathcal{X}}}{\lambda }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right) } \geq  1
$$

${\lambda }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right) } \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ for all $R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \in$ atoms(Q)

${\lambda }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right) } \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 对所有$R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \in$ atoms(Q)

The optimal objective value of the above program is called the fractional edge cover number of the variable set $\mathcal{F}$ and is denoted as ${\rho }_{Q}^{ * }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ . An integral edge cover of $\mathcal{F}$ is a feasible solution to the variant of the above program with ${\lambda }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right) } \in  \{ 0,1\}$ for each $R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)$ . The optimal objective value of this program is called the integral edge cover number of $\mathcal{F}$ and is denoted as ${\rho }_{Q}\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ . If $Q$ is clear from the context,we omit the index $Q$ in the expressions ${\rho }_{Q}^{ * }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ and ${\rho }_{Q}\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ . For a database of size $N$ ,the result of the query $Q$ can be computed in time $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$ [NPRR18].

上述规划问题的最优目标值称为变量集$\mathcal{F}$的分数边覆盖数，记作${\rho }_{Q}^{ * }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$。而$\mathcal{F}$的整数边覆盖是指将原规划问题中每个$R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)$的${\lambda }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right) } \in  \{ 0,1\}$约束改为整数约束后的可行解，其最优目标值称为整数边覆盖数，记作${\rho }_{Q}\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$。若上下文已明确$Q$，则省略${\rho }_{Q}^{ * }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$和${\rho }_{Q}\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$中的下标$Q$。对于规模为$N$的数据库，查询$Q$的结果可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$时间内计算完成[NPRR18]。

For hierarchical queries, the integral and fractional edge cover numbers are equal. The proofs of the following propositions in this section are given in the technical report [KNOZ19] (Appendices B and C).

对于层次化查询，其整数边覆盖数与分数边覆盖数相等。本节下列命题的证明详见技术报告[KNOZ19]（附录B和C）。

Proposition 4.5. For any hierarchical query $Q$ and $\mathcal{F} \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ ,it holds ${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  = \rho \left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ .

命题4.5. 对任意层次化查询$Q$及$\mathcal{F} \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$，恒有${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  = \rho \left( \mathcal{F}\right)$成立。

Definition 4.6. The static width of a conjunctive query $Q$ is

定义4.6. 合取查询$Q$的静态宽度为

$$
\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{\omega  \in  \operatorname{free}\operatorname{TopVO}\left( Q\right) }}\mathrm{w}\left( \omega \right) 
$$

$$
\mathrm{w}\left( \omega \right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{X \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) }}{\rho }^{ * }\left( {\{ X\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{\omega }\left( X\right) }\right) 
$$

If $Q$ is Boolean,then $\mathrm{w}$ is the fractional hypertree width [Mar10]. FAQ-width generalizes w to queries over several semirings ${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{{AKNR}}{16}\right\rbrack  }^{1}$ .

若$Q$为布尔查询，则$\mathrm{w}$即为分数超树宽度[Mar10]。FAQ-width将w推广至多个半环${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{{AKNR}}{16}\right\rbrack  }^{1}$上的查询。

Definition 4.7. The dynamic width of a conjunctive query $Q$ is

定义4.7. 合取查询$Q$的动态宽度为

$$
\delta \left( Q\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{\omega  \in  \text{ free }\operatorname{TopVO}\left( Q\right) }}\delta \left( \omega \right) 
$$

$$
\delta \left( \omega \right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{X \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) }}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right) }}{\rho }^{ * }\left( {\left( {\{ X\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{\omega }\left( X\right) }\right)  - \mathcal{Y}}\right) 
$$

While the static width of a free-top variable order $\omega$ is defined over the sets $\{ X\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{\omega }\left( X\right)$ with $X \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ ,the dynamic width of $\omega$ is defined over restrictions of these sets obtained by dropping the variables in the schema of one atom. For any canonical variable order $\omega$ ,variable $X$ in $\omega$ ,and atom $R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$ in $\operatorname{atoms}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$ ,the set $\left( {\{ X\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{\omega }\left( X\right) }\right)  - \mathcal{Y}$ is empty. Hence, queries that admit canonical free-top variable orders have dynamic width 0 .

自由顶变量序$\omega$的静态宽度基于满足$X \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$的集合$\{ X\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{\omega }\left( X\right)$定义，而其动态宽度则通过剔除一个原子模式中的变量后得到的限制集合定义。对任何规范变量序$\omega$、其中的变量$X$及$\operatorname{atoms}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$中的原子$R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$，集合$\left( {\{ X\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{\omega }\left( X\right) }\right)  - \mathcal{Y}$为空。因此，允许规范自由顶变量序的查询其动态宽度为0。

Proposition 4.8. Given a hierarchical query with static width $\mathbf{w}$ and dynamic width $\delta$ ,it holds that $\delta  = \mathrm{w}$ or $\delta  = \mathrm{w} - 1$ .

命题4.8. 给定静态宽度为$\mathbf{w}$且动态宽度为$\delta$的层次化查询，必有$\delta  = \mathrm{w}$或$\delta  = \mathrm{w} - 1$成立。

Free-connex hierarchical queries have static width 1 . Proposition 4.9. Any free-connex hierarchical query has static width 1.

自由连接层次化查询的静态宽度为1。命题4.9. 任何自由连接层次化查询的静态宽度均为1。

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{1}$ To simplify presentation,we focus on queries that contain at least one atom with non-empty schema. This implies that the static width of queries is at least 1. Queries where all atoms have empty schemas obviously admit constant preprocessing time, update time, and enumeration delay.

${}^{1}$ 为简化表述，我们重点关注至少含一个非空模式原子的查询。这意味着查询的静态宽度至少为1。所有原子模式均为空的查询显然具有常数级预处理时间、更新时间和枚举延迟。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

We give a syntactic classification of hierarchical queries based on their dynamic width.

我们根据动态宽度对层次化查询进行语法分类。

Definition 4.10. A hierarchical query is ${\delta }_{i}$ -hierarchical for $i \in  \mathbb{N}$ if $i$ is the smallest number such that for each bound variable $X$ and atom $R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$ of $X$ ,there are $i$ atoms ${R}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{Y}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{Y}}_{i}\right)$ such that all free variables in the atoms of $X$ are included in $\mathcal{Y} \cup  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack  }}{\mathcal{Y}}_{j}$ .

定义4.10. 当$i$是满足以下条件的最小数值时，称层次化查询对于$i \in  \mathbb{N}$是${\delta }_{i}$-层次化的：对于每个受限变量$X$及其原子$R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$，存在$i$个原子${R}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{Y}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{Y}}_{i}\right)$，使得$X$所有原子中的自由变量都包含在$\mathcal{Y} \cup  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack  }}{\mathcal{Y}}_{j}$中。

For instance,the query $Q\left( {{Y}_{0},\ldots ,{Y}_{i}}\right)  = {R}_{0}\left( {X,{Y}_{0}}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{i}\left( {X,{Y}_{i}}\right)$ is a ${\delta }_{i}$ -hierarchical query for $i \in  \mathbb{N}$ . The class of hierarchical queries can be partitioned into subclasses of ${\delta }_{i}$ - hierarchical queries for $i \in  \mathbb{N}$ . Then,the ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical queries are precisely the q-hierarchical queries from prior work [BKS17a].

例如，查询$Q\left( {{Y}_{0},\ldots ,{Y}_{i}}\right)  = {R}_{0}\left( {X,{Y}_{0}}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{i}\left( {X,{Y}_{i}}\right)$对于$i \in  \mathbb{N}$是${\delta }_{i}$-层次化查询。层次化查询类可划分为针对$i \in  \mathbb{N}$的${\delta }_{i}$-层次化查询子类。此时，${\delta }_{0}$-层次化查询正是先前研究[BKS17a]中的q-层次化查询。

Proposition 4.11. A query is q-hierarchical if and only if it is ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical.

命题4.11. 当且仅当查询是${\delta }_{0}$-层次化时，该查询才是q-层次化的。

As depicted in Figure 2 (right),all free-connex hierarchical queries are either ${\delta }_{0}$ - or ${\delta }_{1}$ -hierarchical.

如图2（右）所示，所有自由连接层次化查询要么是${\delta }_{0}$-层次化，要么是${\delta }_{1}$-层次化。

Proposition 4.12. Any free-connex hierarchical query is ${\delta }_{0}$ - or ${\delta }_{1}$ -hierarchical.

命题4.12. 任何自由连接层次化查询都是${\delta }_{0}$-或${\delta }_{1}$-层次化的。

The following proposition relates ${\delta }_{i}$ -hierarchical queries to their dynamic width.

下列命题阐述了${\delta }_{i}$-层次化查询与其动态宽度的关系。

Proposition 4.13. A hierarchical query is ${\delta }_{i}$ -hierarchical for $i \in  \mathbb{N}$ if and only if it has dynamic width $i$ .

命题4.13. 当且仅当层次化查询具有动态宽度$i$时，该查询对于$i \in  \mathbb{N}$才是${\delta }_{i}$-层次化的。

Proposition 4.13 and Theorem 3.1 imply the following corollary.

命题4.13与定理3.1可推导出以下推论。

Corollary 4.14. Given a ${\delta }_{i}$ -hierarchical query with $i \in  \mathbb{N}$ and static width $\mathrm{w}$ ,a database of size $N$ ,and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,the query result can be enumerated with $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ delay after $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ preprocessing time and $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{i\epsilon }\right)$ amortized time for single-tuple updates.

推论4.14. 给定具有$i \in  \mathbb{N}$和静态宽度$\mathrm{w}$的${\delta }_{i}$-层次化查询、规模为$N$的数据库及$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$预处理时间后能以$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$延迟枚举查询结果，单元组更新的均摊时间为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{i\epsilon }\right)$。

## 5. Preprocessing

## 5. 预处理

In the preprocessing stage, we construct a data structure that represents the result of a given hierarchical query. The data structure consists of a set of view trees, where each view tree computes one part of the query result. A view tree is a tree-shaped hierarchy of materialized views with input relations as leaves and upper views defined in terms of their child views. The construction of view trees exploits the structure of the query and the degree of data values in base relations. We construct different sets of view trees for the static and dynamic evaluation of a given hierarchical query.

预处理阶段需构建表示层次化查询结果的数据结构。该结构由视图树集合构成，每棵视图树计算查询结果的一个组成部分。视图树是以输入关系为叶子节点、上层视图通过子视图定义形成的树形物化视图层次结构。视图树的构建过程充分利用了查询结构和基关系数据值的度数特征，针对静态和动态查询评估分别构建不同的视图树集合。

We next assume that the canonical variable order of the given hierarchical query consists of a single connected component. For several connected components, the preprocessing procedure is executed on each connected component separately.

下文假设给定层次化查询的规范变量序由单个连通分量组成。若存在多个连通分量，预处理过程需对每个分量独立执行。

5.1. View Trees Encoding the Query Result. Given a hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ and a canonical variable order $\omega$ for $Q$ ,the function BUILDVT in Figure 6 constructs a view tree that encodes the query result. The function proceeds recursively on the structure of $\omega$ and constructs a view over schema ${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ at each inner node $X$ ; the leaves correspond to the atoms in the query. The view is defined over the join of its child views projected onto ${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ (Figure 7). The schema ${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ includes the ancestors of $X$ in $\omega$ since they are needed for joins at nodes above $X$ . Each constructed view has a name to help us identify the place and purpose of the view in the view tree.

5.1 编码查询结果的视图树。给定一个层次化查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$和规范变量顺序$\omega$，图6中的BUILDVT函数会构建一个编码查询结果的视图树。该函数基于$\omega$的结构递归执行，并在每个内部节点$X$处构建一个模式为${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$的视图；叶子节点对应查询中的原子。该视图定义在其子视图连接后投影到${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$的结果之上（图7）。模式${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$包含$\omega$中$X$的祖先节点，因为这些节点在$X$上方节点进行连接操作时需要用到。每个构建的视图都有名称，以便我们在视图树中识别该视图的位置和作用。

<!-- Media -->

BUILDVT(string prefix,variable order $\omega$ ,schema $\mathcal{F}$ ) : view tree

BUILDVT(字符串前缀，变量顺序$\omega$，模式$\mathcal{F}$)：视图树

NEWVT(string viewname,schema $\mathcal{S}$ ,view trees ${T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{k}$ ) : view tree

新建视图树(字符串视图名,模式$\mathcal{S}$,视图树${T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{k}$)：视图树

---

	let ${V}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{i}\right)  =$ root of ${T}_{i},\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$

	令${V}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{i}\right)  =$为${T}_{i},\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$的根节点

	let $V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  =$ join of ${V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{k}\right)$ projected onto $\mathcal{S}$

	令$V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  =$为${V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{k}\right)$在$\mathcal{S}$上的投影连接

	V. name := viewname

	视图名称 := 视图名

4 return $\left\{  \begin{matrix} {T}_{1} & ,k = 1 \land  \mathcal{S} = \mathcal{S} \\  V\left( \mathcal{S}\right) & ,\text{ otherwise } \\  {T}_{1}\cdots {T}_{k} & ,\text{ otherwise } \end{matrix}\right.$

4 返回$\left\{  \begin{matrix} {T}_{1} & ,k = 1 \land  \mathcal{S} = \mathcal{S} \\  V\left( \mathcal{S}\right) & ,\text{ otherwise } \\  {T}_{1}\cdots {T}_{k} & ,\text{ otherwise } \end{matrix}\right.$

<!-- Media -->

If $X$ is free,then it is included in the schema of the view constructed at $X$ (and not included if bound). It is also kept in the schemas of the views on the path to the root until it reaches a view whose schema does not have bound variables. The constructed view tree has the upper levels only with views over the free variables. The hierarchy of such views represents the query result and allows its enumeration with constant delay.

若$X$为自由变量，则其将被包含在$X$处构建视图的模式中（若为绑定变量则不包含）。该变量会保留在通往根节点的路径视图模式中，直至遇到不含绑定变量的视图。所构建的视图树仅在上层保留自由变量视图，此类视图的层次结构代表查询结果，支持以恒定延迟进行枚举。

In the dynamic case,at each child $Z$ of $X$ we construct a view with schema $\operatorname{anc}\left( Z\right)$ on top of the view created at $Z$ (Figure 8). This auxiliary view aggregates away $Z$ from the latter view. The children of the view created at $X$ then share the same schema ${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ . This property enables the efficient maintenance of the view at $X$ since processing a change coming from any child view requires only constant-time lookups into that child's sibling views.

动态场景下，在$X$的每个子节点$Z$处，我们基于$Z$处创建的视图构建模式为$\operatorname{anc}\left( Z\right)$的视图（图8）。该辅助视图会聚合移除下层视图中的$Z$变量。此时$X$处创建视图的子节点共享相同模式${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$。该特性使得$X$处视图能高效维护，因为处理来自任意子视图的变更只需在兄弟视图中进行常数时间查找。

Our preprocessing is particularly efficient for free-connex hierarchical queries in the static case and for their strict subclass of ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical queries in the dynamic case.

我们的预处理方法对静态场景下的自由连接层次化查询特别高效，对动态场景下严格子类${\delta }_{0}$-层次化查询同样有效。

For a canonical variable order of a hierarchical query, the free-connex property fails if there are free variables such that they are below a bound join variable and are not covered by one atom. Indeed,assume two branches out of a bound join variable $X$ and with free variables $Y$ and respectively $Z$ . Then,there are two atoms in $Q$ whose sets of variables include $\{ X,Y\}$ and respectively $\{ X,Z\}$ ,while $\{ Y,Z\}$ are included in the head atom of $Q$ . This creates a cycle in the hypergraph of $Q$ ,which means that $Q$ is not free-connex.

对于层次化查询的规范变量序，若存在自由变量位于绑定连接变量下方且未被单个原子覆盖，则自由连接性失效。假设绑定连接变量$X$分出两个分支，分别带有自由变量$Y$和$Z$，那么$Q$中存在两个原子：其变量集分别包含$\{ X,Y\}$和$\{ X,Z\}$，而$\{ Y,Z\}$包含在$Q$的头原子中。这会在$Q$的超图中形成环路，意味着$Q$不满足自由连接性。

AuxVIEW(node $Z$ ,view tree $T$ ) : view tree

辅助视图(节点$Z$,视图树$T$)：视图树

---

let $V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  =$ root of $T$

令$V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  =$为$T$的根节点

let viewname $=$ V.name + "'"

令视图名$=$为V.name + "'"

if mode $=$ ’dynamic’ $\land$ has_sibling $\left( Z\right)  \land$ anc $\left( Z\right)  \subset  \mathcal{S}$

若模式$=$为'动态'且$\land$存在兄弟节点$\left( Z\right)  \land$祖先$\left( Z\right)  \subset  \mathcal{S}$

	return $\operatorname{NEWVT}\left( {\text{viewname},\operatorname{anc}\left( Z\right) ,\{ T\} }\right)$

	返回$\operatorname{NEWVT}\left( {\text{viewname},\operatorname{anc}\left( Z\right) ,\{ T\} }\right)$

return $T$

返回 $T$

---

FIGURE 8. A tree $T$ constructed at variable $Z$ is extended with a new root view that aggregates away $Z$ .

图8. 在变量$Z$处构建的树$T$通过新增根视图进行扩展，该视图聚合移除了$Z$。

<!-- Media -->

For ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical queries,there is no bound variable whose set of atoms strictly contains the atoms of a free variable. Such queries thus admit canonical free-top variable orders where all free variables occur above the bound ones.

对于${\delta }_{0}$层次化查询，不存在某个绑定变量的原子集合严格包含自由变量的原子集合。因此这类查询允许规范的自由-顶部变量排序，即所有自由变量都出现在绑定变量之上。

For any free-connex hierarchical query, each view created by BUILDVT is defined over variables from one atom of the query and can be materialized in linear time. We can thus recover the linear-time preprocessing for such queries used for static [BDG07] and dynamic [BKS17a, IUV17] evaluation.

对于任何自由连接层次化查询，BUILDVT创建的每个视图都基于查询中单个原子的变量定义，并可在线性时间内物化。因此我们可以恢复用于静态[BDG07]和动态[BKS17a, IUV17]评估的此类查询的线性时间预处理。

Example 5.1. Consider the free-connex query

示例5.1. 考虑自由连接查询

$$
Q\left( {A,D,E}\right)  = R\left( {A,B,C}\right) ,S\left( {A,B,D}\right) ,T\left( {A,E}\right) 
$$

and its canonical variable order in Figure 9. We construct the view tree bottom-up as follows. At $C$ ,we create the view ${V}_{C}\left( {A,B}\right)$ that aggregates away the bound variable $C$ but keeps its ancestors $A$ and $B$ to define views up in the tree. Since $D$ is free and has only one child,we skip creating a view at $D$ ; see the first case in Line 4 of NEWVT from Figure 7. Similarly, no view is created at $E$ . At $B$ ,we create the view ${V}_{B}\left( {A,D}\right)  = {V}_{C}\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( {A,B,D}\right)$ , which keeps $D$ as it is free and $A$ as the ancestor of $B$ . At $A$ ,we create the views ${V}_{A}\left( A\right)  = {V}_{B}\left( {A,D}\right) ,T\left( {A,E}\right)$ in the static case and ${V}_{A}\left( A\right)  = {V}_{B}^{\prime }\left( A\right) ,{T}^{\prime }\left( A\right)$ in the dynamic case,where ${V}_{B}^{\prime }\left( A\right)  = {V}_{B}\left( {A,D}\right)$ and ${T}^{\prime }\left( A\right)  = T\left( {A,E}\right)$ . Each view can be computed in linear time by aggregating away variables and semi-join reduction. The result of $Q$ can be enumerated using ${V}_{A}\left( A\right) ,{V}_{B}\left( {A,D}\right)$ ,and $T\left( {A,E}\right)$ with constant delay.

及其在图9中的规范变量顺序。我们按以下方式自底向上构建视图树：在$C$处创建视图${V}_{C}\left( {A,B}\right)$，该视图聚合移除了绑定变量$C$但保留其祖先$A$和$B$以定义上层视图；由于$D$是自由变量且仅有一个子节点，跳过$D$处的视图创建（见图7中NEWVT第4行的第一种情况）；类似地，$E$处也不创建视图；在$B$处创建视图${V}_{B}\left( {A,D}\right)  = {V}_{C}\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( {A,B,D}\right)$，保留自由变量$D$及$B$的祖先$A$；在$A$处分别创建静态场景下的视图${V}_{A}\left( A\right)  = {V}_{B}\left( {A,D}\right) ,T\left( {A,E}\right)$和动态场景下的视图${V}_{A}\left( A\right)  = {V}_{B}^{\prime }\left( A\right) ,{T}^{\prime }\left( A\right)$，其中${V}_{B}^{\prime }\left( A\right)  = {V}_{B}\left( {A,D}\right)$和${T}^{\prime }\left( A\right)  = T\left( {A,E}\right)$。每个视图都可通过变量聚合和半连接归约在线性时间内计算完成。$Q$的结果可使用${V}_{A}\left( A\right) ,{V}_{B}\left( {A,D}\right)$枚举，$T\left( {A,E}\right)$则能以恒定延迟处理。

5.2. Skew-Aware View Trees. For free-connex queries, the procedure BUILDVT constructs in linear time a data structure that allows for constant-time enumeration delay (Proposition 6.1 and Lemma C.4). For ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical queries,it also admits constant-time updates (Lemma E.1). We now focus on the bound join variables that violate the free-connex property in the static case or the ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical property in the dynamic case. For each such violating bound variable $X$ ,we use two evaluation strategies.

5.2. 偏斜感知视图树。对于自由连接查询，BUILDVT过程在线性时间内构建支持恒定枚举延迟的数据结构（命题6.1及引理C.4）；对于${\delta }_{0}$层次化查询，还支持恒定时间更新（引理E.1）。我们现重点关注在静态场景违反自由连接性质、或动态场景违反${\delta }_{0}$层次化性质的绑定连接变量。对每个此类违规变量$X$，我们采用两种评估策略。

The first strategy materializes a subset of the query result obtained for the light values over the set of variables $\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$ in the variable order. It also aggregates away the bound variables in the subtree rooted at $X$ . Since the light values have a bounded degree, this materialization is inexpensive.

第一种策略物化了变量顺序中针对轻量值在变量集$\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$上获得的查询结果子集，同时聚合移除了以$X$为根的子树中的绑定变量。由于轻量值具有有限度数，该物化过程成本较低。
