Figure 9. Canonical variable order and view tree for $Q\left( {A,D,E}\right)  =$ $R\left( {A,B,C}\right) ,S\left( {A,B,D}\right) ,T\left( {A,E}\right)$ in Example 5.1. The views ${V}_{B}^{\prime }$ and ${T}^{\prime }$ are created in the dynamic case. Free variables are underlined.

图9. 示例5.1中$Q\left( {A,D,E}\right)  =$$R\left( {A,B,C}\right) ,S\left( {A,B,D}\right) ,T\left( {A,E}\right)$的规范变量顺序与视图树。视图${V}_{B}^{\prime }$和${T}^{\prime }$为动态场景下创建。自由变量以下划线标示。

<!-- Media -->

The second strategy computes a compact representation of the rest of the query result obtained for those values over $\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$ that are heavy (i.e.,have high degree) in at least one relation. This second strategy treats $X$ as a free variable and proceeds recursively to resolve further bound variables located below $X$ in the variable order and to potentially fork into more strategies.

第二种策略计算针对那些在至少一个关系中属于重度值（即具有高度数）的$\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$所获查询结果剩余部分的紧凑表示。该策略将$X$视为自由变量，递归地解析变量顺序中位于$X$下方的其他绑定变量，并可能分叉出更多策略。

The union of these strategies precisely cover the entire query result, yet not necessarily disjointly. To enumerate the distinct tuples in the query result, we then use an adaptation of the union algorithm [DS11] where the delay is given by the number of heavy values of the variables we partitioned on and by the number of strategies.

这些策略的并集能完整覆盖整个查询结果，但未必互不相交。为了枚举查询结果中的不同元组，我们采用改进版的并集算法[DS11]，其延迟时间取决于我们划分变量时的重度值数量及策略总数。

Heavy and Light Indicators. We consider a bound join variables that violates the free-connex property in the static case or the ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical property in the dynamic case. We compute heavy and light indicator views consisting of disjoint sets of values for each such variable $X$ . The heavy indicator has the values that exist in all relations and are heavy in at least one relation. The light indicator has the values that exist in all relations and are light in all relations. Indicator views have set semantics. They allow us to rewrite the query into an equivalent union of two queries.

重度与轻度指示器。我们考察静态情况下违反自由连接性质、或动态情况下违反${\delta }_{0}$层次性质的绑定连接变量。针对每个此类变量$X$，我们计算由互斥值集构成的重度和轻度指示器视图。重度指示器包含在所有关系中存在且在至少一个关系中为重度值的数值，轻度指示器则包含在所有关系中存在且均为轻度值的数值。指示器视图具有集合语义，使我们能将查询重写为两个查询的等价并集。

Partitioning the query result only based on the degree of $X$ -values may blow up the enumeration delay: the path from $X$ to the root may contain several bound join variables, each creating buckets of values per bucket of their ancestors, thus leading to an explosion of the number of buckets that need to be unioned together during enumeration. However, one remarkable property holds for hierarchical queries: each base relation located in the subtree rooted at $X$ contains $X$ but also all the ancestors of $X$ . Thus,by partitioning each relation jointly on $X$ and its ancestors,we can ensure the enumeration delay remains linear in the number of distinct heavy values over $\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$ .

仅基于$X$值的度数划分查询结果可能导致枚举延迟激增：从$X$到根的路径可能包含多个绑定连接变量，每个变量都会根据其祖先桶创建值桶，从而导致枚举期间需要合并的桶数量爆炸式增长。但层次化查询具有一个显著特性：位于$X$为根的子树的每个基关系不仅包含$X$，还包含其所有祖先。因此通过对<b0></b1>及其祖先进行联合关系划分，可确保枚举延迟与$\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$上不同重度值的数量保持线性关系。

Figure 10 shows how to construct a triple of view trees for computing the indicators for $\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$ ,where $X$ is the root of a variable order $\omega$ that is a subtree in the variable order of a hierarchical query (thus anc(X)may be non-empty). We first construct a view tree that computes the tuples of values for variables $\operatorname{keys} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$ over the join of the relations from $\omega$ . We then build a similar view tree for the light indicator for keys using a modified variable order ${\omega }^{\text{keys }}$ of the same structure as $\omega$ but with each relation $R$ replaced by the light part of $R$ partitioned on keys. Finally,the view tree for the heavy indicator computes the difference of all keys-values and those from the light indicator.

图10展示了如何构建用于计算$\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$指示器的视图树三元组，其中$X$是层次化查询变量顺序中子树$\omega$的根节点（故anc(X)可能非空）。我们首先构建视图树来计算$\omega$关系连接上变量$\operatorname{keys} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$的值元组，接着使用与$\omega$结构相同但将每个关系$R$替换为按键划分的$R$轻度部分的修改版变量顺序${\omega }^{\text{keys }}$，为轻度指示器构建类似视图树。最终，重度指示器的视图树通过计算全键值与轻度指示器值的差集获得。

View Trees with Indicators. Figure 11 gives the algorithm for constructing the view trees for a variable order $\omega$ of a hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ . The algorithm traverses the variable order $\omega$ top-down,maintaining the invariant that all ancestors of a node are free variables (or treated as such in case of bound join variables whose values are heavy).

带指示器的视图树。图11给出了为层次化查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$的变量顺序$\omega$构建视图树的算法。该算法自顶向下遍历变量顺序$\omega$，并保持节点所有祖先均为自由变量（或将被重度值绑定的连接变量视作自由变量）的不变式。

<!-- Media -->

INDICATORVTS(variable order $\omega$ ) : triple of view trees

INDICATORVTS(变量顺序$\omega$)：视图树三元组

---

							let $X =$ root of $\omega$

							设$X =$为$\omega$的根

							let $\operatorname{keys} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$

							设$\operatorname{keys} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$

								let alltree $=$ BUILDVT("All", $\omega$ ,keys)

								设alltree = BUILDVT("All", $\omega$, keys)

							let ltree $=$ BUILDVT("L", keys, keys)

							设ltree = BUILDVT("L", keys, keys)

							let allroot $=$ root of alltree

							设allroot = alltree的根

								let lroot $=$ root of ltree

								设lroot = ltree的根

let htree = NEWVT("H_" + X.name,keys,\{allroot,#lroot\})

设htree = NEWVT("H_" + X.name, keys, \{allroot,#lroot\})

							return (alltree, ltree, htree)

							返回(alltree, ltree, htree)

---

FIGURE 10. Construction of the heavy and light indicator view trees for a canonical variable order $\omega$ of a hierarchical query. The variable order ${\omega }^{\text{keys }}$ has the structure of $\omega$ but each atom $R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$ is replaced with the light part ${R}^{\text{keys }}\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$ of relation $R$ partitioned on keys. The view #Iroot maps all tuples contained in lroot to 0 and all other tuples to 1 .

图10. 为层次化查询的规范变量序$\omega$构建重/轻指示符视图树。该变量序${\omega }^{\text{keys }}$保持$\omega$的结构，但每个原子$R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$被替换为基于键值分区的关系$R$的轻量部分${R}^{\text{keys }}\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$。视图#Iroot将lroot包含的所有元组映射为0，其余元组映射为1。

<!-- Media -->

The free variables at node $X$ are the ancestors of $X$ and the free variables in the subtree rooted at $X$ (Line 3). If the residual query ${Q}_{X}$ at node $X$ (Line 4) is free-connex in the static case or ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical in the dynamic case,we return a view tree for ${Q}_{X}$ (Lines 5-7). If $X$ is free,we recursively compute a set of view trees for each child of $X$ . We may extend the root of each child tree with an auxiliary view in the dynamic mode to support constant-time propagation of updates coming via the siblings of $X$ . For each combination of the child view trees, we form a new view joining the roots of the child view trees and using $X$ and its ancestors as free variables (Lines 8-11). If $X$ is bound,we create two evaluation strategies for the residual query ${Q}_{X}$ based on the degree of values of $X$ and its ancestors in the relations of ${Q}_{X}$ . We construct the indicator view trees for $X$ and its ancestors (Line 12). The heavy indicator restricts the joins of the child views to only heavy values for the tuple of $X$ and its ancestors (Lines 13-15). We also construct a view tree over the light parts of the relations in $\omega$ (Line 16).

节点$X$的自由变量包括其祖先节点及以$X$为根的子树中的自由变量（第3行）。若该节点处的剩余查询${Q}_{X}$在静态情况下满足自由连接性，或在动态情况下满足${\delta }_{0}$-层次性（第4行），则返回${Q}_{X}$的视图树（第5-7行）。当$X$自由时，递归计算其每个子节点的视图树集合。动态模式下可通过辅助视图扩展子树根节点，以支持经由$X$兄弟节点更新的常数时间传播（第8-11行）。若$X$受限，则根据该节点及其祖先在${Q}_{X}$关系中的取值度，为剩余查询${Q}_{X}$创建两种评估策略，并构建$X$及其祖先的指示符视图树（第12行）。重指示符将子视图连接限制为仅针对$X$及其祖先元组的重值（第13-15行），同时基于$\omega$中各关系的轻量部分构建视图树（第16行）。

The algorithm from Figure 11 uses different criteria for the static and dynamic cases (Lines 5-6) to decide on whether to stop recursively traversing the variable order. Since the class of ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical queries is a proper subset of the class of free-connex queries,the algorithm may partition input relations on more attributes and create more view trees in the dynamic case than in the static case for the same variable order and free variables.

图11算法对静态和动态情形采用不同判断标准（第5-6行）来决定是否终止变量序的递归遍历。由于${\delta }_{0}$-层次化查询类是自由连接查询类的真子集，对于相同变量序和自由变量，动态情形下算法可能基于更多属性划分输入关系，并创建比静态情形更多的视图树。

We next showcase our approach on a non-free-connex query. Section 9 provides additional examples with ${\delta }_{1}$ -hierarchical queries.

接下来我们通过非自由连接查询演示本方法，第9节将提供更多${\delta }_{1}$-层次化查询案例。

Example 5.2. Figure 12 shows the view trees for the query

示例5.2. 图12展示了该查询的视图树

$$
Q\left( {C,D,E,F}\right)  = R\left( {A,B,D}\right) ,S\left( {A,B,E}\right) ,T\left( {A,C,F}\right) ,U\left( {A,C,G}\right) \text{.}
$$

We start from the root $A$ in the variable order. Since $Q$ is not free-connex (and also not ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical) and $A$ is bound,we create the view trees for the indicators ${H}_{A}\left( A\right)$ and ${L}_{A}\left( A\right)$ . Materializing the views in these view trees takes linear time.

从变量序的根节点$A$出发。由于$Q$不满足自由连接性（也不满足${\delta }_{0}$-层次性）且$A$受限，我们为指示符${H}_{A}\left( A\right)$和${L}_{A}\left( A\right)$创建视图树，这些视图树的物化操作仅需线性时间。

FIGURE 11. Construction of skew-aware view trees for a canonical variable order $\omega$ of a hierarchical query with free variables $\mathcal{F}$ . The global parameter mode $\in  \{$ ’static’,’dynamic’ $\}$ specifies the evaluation mode. The variable order ${\omega }^{\text{keys }}$ has the structure of $\omega$ but each atom $R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$ is replaced by the light part ${R}^{\text{keys }}\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$ of relation $R$ partitioned on keys. The view $\exists {H}_{X}$ maps all tuples contained in ${H}_{X}$ to 1 and all other tuples to 0 .

图11. 针对具有自由变量$\mathcal{F}$的层次化查询的规范变量序$\omega$，构建偏斜感知视图树。全局参数模式$\in  \{$（'静态'、'动态'$\}$）指定评估模式。该变量序${\omega }^{\text{keys }}$保持$\omega$的结构，但每个原子$R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$被替换为基于键分区的关系$R$的轻量部分${R}^{\text{keys }}\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$。视图$\exists {H}_{X}$将${H}_{X}$包含的所有元组映射为1，其余元组映射为0。

In the light case for $A$ ,we create a view tree with the root ${V}_{A}\left( {C,D,E,F}\right)$ and the leaves being the light parts of the input relations partitioned on $A$ (bottom-left). Computing ${V}_{G}\left( {A,C}\right)$ and ${V}_{C}\left( {A,C,F}\right)$ takes linear time. We compute the view ${V}_{B}\left( {A,D,E}\right)$ in time $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \epsilon }\right)$ : For each(a,b,d)tuple in ${R}^{A}$ ,we iterate over at most ${N}^{\epsilon }\left( {a,b,e}\right)$ values in ${S}^{A}$ . The view ${V}_{B}\left( {A,D,E}\right)$ contains at most ${N}^{1 + \epsilon }$ tuples. Similarly,we compute ${V}_{A}\left( {C,D,E,F}\right)$ in time $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + {2\epsilon }}\right)$ : For each(a,d,e)tuple in ${V}_{B}$ ,we iterate over at most ${N}^{\epsilon }\left( {a,c,f}\right)$ values in ${V}_{C}$ . The view ${V}_{A}\left( {C,D,E,F}\right)$ allows constant delay enumeration of its result.

对于$A$的轻量情况，我们创建以${V}_{A}\left( {C,D,E,F}\right)$为根节点、以基于$A$分区的输入关系轻量部分为叶节点的视图树（左下）。计算${V}_{G}\left( {A,C}\right)$和${V}_{C}\left( {A,C,F}\right)$需线性时间。视图${V}_{B}\left( {A,D,E}\right)$的计算时间为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \epsilon }\right)$：对于${R}^{A}$中的每个(a,b,d)元组，我们最多迭代${S}^{A}$中的${N}^{\epsilon }\left( {a,b,e}\right)$个值。视图${V}_{B}\left( {A,D,E}\right)$最多包含${N}^{1 + \epsilon }$个元组。类似地，${V}_{A}\left( {C,D,E,F}\right)$的计算时间为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + {2\epsilon }}\right)$：对于<b11></b1>中的每个(a,d,e)元组，最多迭代${V}_{C}$中的${N}^{\epsilon }\left( {a,c,f}\right)$个值。视图${V}_{A}\left( {C,D,E,F}\right)$支持对其结果进行恒定延迟枚举。

In the heavy case for $A$ ,we recursively process the subtrees of $A$ in $\omega$ and treat $A$ as free. The right subquery, ${Q}_{C}\left( {A,C,F}\right)  = T\left( {A,C,F}\right) ,U\left( {A,C,G}\right)$ is free-connex and ${\delta }_{0}$ - hierarchical,thus we compute its view tree with the root ${V}_{C}\left( {A,C}\right)$ in the static case and the root ${V}_{C}^{\prime }\left( A\right)$ in the dynamic case (view trees in the second row) in linear time. The left subquery ${Q}_{B}\left( {A,D,E}\right)  = R\left( {A,B,D}\right) ,S\left( {A,B,E}\right)$ ,however,is neither free-connex nor ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical. Since $B$ is bound,we create the indicator relations ${H}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$ and ${L}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$ in linear time. We distinguish two new cases: In the light case for(A,B),we construct a view tree with the root ${V}_{B}\left( {A,D,E}\right)  = {R}^{AB}\left( {A,B,D}\right) ,{S}^{AB}\left( {A,B,E}\right)$ (second row left) and compute ${V}_{B}\left( {A,D,E}\right)$ in time $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \epsilon }\right)$ by iterating over ${R}^{AB}$ and,for each(a,b,d),iterating over at most ${N}^{\epsilon }E$ -values in ${S}^{AB}$ . In the heavy case for(A,B),we process the subtrees of $B$ considering $B$ as free variable. The two subqueries, ${Q}_{D}\left( {A,B,D}\right)  = R\left( {A,B,D}\right)$ and ${Q}_{E}\left( {A,B,E}\right)  = S\left( {A,B,E}\right)$ ,are ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical.

在$A$的重度情形中，我们递归处理$\omega$内$A$的子树，并将$A$视为自由变量。右侧子查询${Q}_{C}\left( {A,C,F}\right)  = T\left( {A,C,F}\right) ,U\left( {A,C,G}\right)$是自由连接且${\delta }_{0}$层次化的，因此我们在线性时间内分别计算静态情形下根为${V}_{C}\left( {A,C}\right)$与动态情形下根为${V}_{C}^{\prime }\left( A\right)$的视图树（第二行视图树）。而左侧子查询${Q}_{B}\left( {A,D,E}\right)  = R\left( {A,B,D}\right) ,S\left( {A,B,E}\right)$既非自由连接也非${\delta }_{0}$层次化。由于$B$是约束变量，我们在线性时间内创建指示关系${H}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$和${L}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$。我们区分两种新情形：在(A,B)的轻度情形中，构建以${V}_{B}\left( {A,D,E}\right)  = {R}^{AB}\left( {A,B,D}\right) ,{S}^{AB}\left( {A,B,E}\right)$为根的视图树（第二行左），并通过遍历${R}^{AB}$并对每个(a,b,d)迭代${S}^{AB}$中最多${N}^{\epsilon }E$个值，在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \epsilon }\right)$时间内计算${V}_{B}\left( {A,D,E}\right)$；在(A,B)的重度情形中，将$B$视为自由变量处理其子树。两个子查询${Q}_{D}\left( {A,B,D}\right)  = R\left( {A,B,D}\right)$和${Q}_{E}\left( {A,B,E}\right)  = S\left( {A,B,E}\right)$均为${\delta }_{0}$层次化。

Figure 12. Canonical variable order for the query $Q\left( {C,D,E,F}\right)  =$ $R\left( {A,B,D}\right) ,S\left( {A,B,E}\right) ,T\left( {A,C,F}\right) ,U\left( {A,C,G}\right)$ (top left). The three view trees constructed for the query (top right and second row). The indicator view trees for computing ${H}_{A}$ and ${H}_{B}$ (third and fourth row). The views with a dashed box are only needed for dynamic query evaluation.

图12. 查询$Q\left( {C,D,E,F}\right)  =$$R\left( {A,B,D}\right) ,S\left( {A,B,E}\right) ,T\left( {A,C,F}\right) ,U\left( {A,C,G}\right)$的规范变量序（左上）。为该查询构建的三个视图树（右上及第二行）。用于计算${H}_{A}$和${H}_{B}$的指示器视图树（第三、四行）。虚线框标注的视图仅用于动态查询求值。

<!-- Media -->

Overall,we create three view trees for $Q$ and two sets of view trees for the indicator relations at $A$ and $B$ . The time needed to compute these view trees is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + {2\epsilon }}\right)$ .

总体而言，我们为$Q$创建了三棵视图树，并为$A$和$B$处的指示器关系构建了两组视图树。计算这些视图树所需时间为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + {2\epsilon }}\right)$。

Given a hierarchical query, our algorithm effectively rewrites it into an equivalent union of queries, with one query defined by the join of the leaves of a view tree.

给定层次化查询时，我们的算法能将其高效重写为查询的等价并集，其中每个查询由视图树叶节点的连接定义。

Proposition 5.3. Let $\left\{  {{T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{k}}\right\}   = \tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ be the set of view trees constructed by the algorithm in Figure 11 for a given hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ and a canonical variable order $\omega$ for $Q$ . Let ${Q}^{\left( i\right) }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ be the query defined by the conjunction of the leaf atoms in ${T}_{i},\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . Then, $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  \equiv  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{Q}^{\left( i\right) }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ .

命题5.3。设$\left\{  {{T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{k}}\right\}   = \tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$为图11中算法针对给定层次化查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$和规范变量序$\omega$所构建的视图树集合。令${Q}^{\left( i\right) }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$表示由${T}_{i},\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$中叶原子合取定义的查询，则有$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  \equiv  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{Q}^{\left( i\right) }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$。

The preprocessing time of our approach is given by the time to materialize the views in the view trees.

本方法的预处理时间取决于视图树中视图物化的耗时。

Proposition 5.4. Given a hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ with static width $\mathrm{w}$ ,a canonical variable order $\omega$ for $Q$ ,a database of size $N$ ,and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,the views in the set of view trees $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ time.

命题5.4. 给定一个静态宽度为$\mathrm{w}$的层次化查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$、针对$Q$的规范变量序$\omega$、规模为$N$的数据库以及$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，视图树集合$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$中的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$时间内物化。

## 6. ENUMERATION

## 6. 枚举算法

For any hierarchical query, Section 5 constructs a set of view trees that together represent the query result. We now show how to enumerate the distinct tuples in the query result with their multiplicity using the open/next/close iterator model for such view trees.

对于任意层次化查询，第5节构建了一组共同表示查询结果的视图树。现在我们将展示如何利用此类视图树的open/next/close迭代器模型，枚举查询结果中具有重复次数的不同元组。

Each view in a view tree follows the iterator model. The function V.open(ctx) initializes the iterator on view $V$ using the tuple ${ctx}$ as context,setting the range of the iterator to those tuples that are consistent with ${ctx}$ in $V$ ,that is, ${ctx}$ is part of each such tuple in $V$ . The function $V$ .next(   ) returns a tuple consistent with ${ctx}$ in $V$ ; or it returns EOF if the tuples in the range of the iterator are exhausted. The tuples returned by V.next(   ) are distinct. Both functions operate in constant time, as per our computational model.

视图树中的每个视图都遵循迭代器模型。函数V.open(ctx)使用元组${ctx}$作为上下文初始化视图$V$上的迭代器，将迭代范围设置为与$V$中${ctx}$一致的元组，即这些元组都包含${ctx}$。函数$V$.next()返回$V$中与${ctx}$一致的元组；若迭代范围内元组耗尽则返回EOF。V.next()返回的元组互不相同。根据我们的计算模型，这两个函数都在常数时间内运行。

Given a subtree $T$ of a view tree and the current tuple ${ctx}$ in its parent view,the call $T$ .open(ctx) described in Figure 13 sets the range of the iterator of $T$ to those tuples in its root view that agree with ${ctx}$ and positions the iterator at the first tuple in this range. The open call is recursively propagated down the view tree with an increasingly more specific context tuple. A T.close(   ) call resets the iterators of tree $T$ . Each subtree $T$ has an attribute $T$ .tuple storing the next tuple to be reported. The open method ends with a call to $T$ .next(   ) to set $T$ .tuple to the first tuple to be reported.

给定视图树的子树$T$及其父视图中的当前元组${ctx}$，图13描述的$T$.open(ctx)调用会将$T$的迭代器范围设置为根视图中与${ctx}$匹配的元组，并将迭代器定位在此范围内的首个元组。open调用会随着上下文元组的逐步具体化，递归地向视图树下方传播。T.close()调用会重置树$T$的迭代器。每个子树$T$都有存储待返回元组的属性$T$.tuple。open方法最后会调用$T$.next()，将$T$.tuple设置为首个待返回元组。

There are two cases that need special attention. If the schema of a view $V$ includes all free variables in the subtree rooted at $V$ ,then there is no need to open the views in this subtree since $V$ already has the tuples over these free variables; e.g.,this is the case of the view ${V}_{A}\left( {C,D,E,F}\right)$ in Figure 12. The views with heavy indicators,e.g.,the views ${V}_{A}\left( A\right)$ and ${V}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$ in Figure 12,also require special treatment. If $V$ has as child a heavy indicator $\exists H$ ,the tree $T$ rooted at $V$ represents possibly overlapping relations in the contexts given by the different tuples $h \in  \exists H$ . We ground the heavy indicator by creating an iterator for each heavy tuple agreeing with the current tuple ${ctx}$ at the parent view of $V$ and keep this iterator in a shallow copy of $T$ . Creating a shallow copy of $T$ means creating a tree of iterators of the same structure as $T$ but without copying the content of views under $T$ .

有两种情况需要特别注意。若视图$V$的模式包含其根子树中所有自由变量，则无需打开该子树中的视图，因为$V$已拥有这些自由变量的元组（例如图12中的视图${V}_{A}\left( {C,D,E,F}\right)$）。带有heavy指标的视图（如图12中的${V}_{A}\left( A\right)$和${V}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$）也需特殊处理。若$V$的子节点是heavy指标$\exists H$，则以$V$为根的树$T$表示在不同元组$h \in  \exists H$上下文中可能重叠的关系。我们通过为每个与$V$父视图中当前元组${ctx}$匹配的heavy元组创建迭代器来固定该heavy指标，并将该迭代器保存在$T$的浅拷贝中。创建$T$的浅拷贝意味着构建与$T$结构相同的迭代器树，但不复制$T$下视图的内容。

FIGURE 14. Find the next tuple in a view tree.

图14. 在视图树中查找下一元组

<!-- Media -->

After the first open call for a view tree $T$ ,we can enumerate the distinct tuples from $T$ with their multiplicity by calling $T$ .next(   ),see Figure 14. The next call propagates recursively down $T$ and observes the same cases as the open call. If a view $V$ in $T$ already covers all free variables in $T$ ,then it suffices to enumerate from $V$ . If $T$ has as child a heavy indicator, we return the next tuple and its multiplicity from the union of all its groundings using the UNION algorithm (Section 6.1). Otherwise, we synthesize the returning tuple out of the tuples at the iterators of $T$ ’s children. Given the current context at $T$ ’s view,we return the next tuple and its multiplicity from the Cartesian product of the tuples produced by $T$ ’s children using the PRODUCT algorithm (Section 6.2).

首次对视图树$T$调用open后，可通过$T$.next()枚举带重复计数的唯一元组（见图14）。next调用会递归向下传播至$T$，其处理逻辑与open调用相同。若视图树$T$中的视图$V$已覆盖所有自由变量，则直接从$V$枚举即可。若$T$含有重型指示器，则使用UNION算法（第6.1节）从其所有接地结果的并集中返回下一元组及其计数。否则，我们根据$T$子节点迭代器的元组合成返回元组。基于当前视图上下文，使用PRODUCT算法（第6.2节）从子节点元组的笛卡尔积中返回下一元组及其计数。

For a view tree with no heavy indicators, calling open and next on the view tree translates to calling open and next on its views, where each such call on a view takes constant time and the number of such calls is independent of the size of the database. Thus, calling open and next on a view tree with no heavy indicators takes constant time.

对于无重型指示器的视图树，调用open和next操作会转化为对其视图的调用，每次视图调用耗时恒定且与数据库规模无关。因此，这类视图树的open和next操作可在常数时间内完成。

In the presence of heavy indicators, the time to initialize a view tree and produce the next tuple is dominated by the number of shallow view trees created in the grounding step. The delay of the UNION algorithm is the sum of the delays of the grounded view trees. Their number is determined by the size of the heavy indicators in the view tree,which is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ . Thus,calling open and next on a view tree with heavy indicators takes $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ time.

当存在重型指示器时，初始化视图树和生成下一元组的时间主要消耗在接地阶段创建的浅层视图树数量上。UNION算法的延迟等于所有接地视图树延迟之和，其数量由视图树中重型指示器的规模$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$决定。因此，含重型指示器的视图树调用open和next操作需要$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$时间。

So far we discussed the case of enumerating from one view tree. In case of a set of view trees we again use the UNION algorithm. In case the query has several connected

前文讨论了单个视图树的枚举场景。对于视图树集合，我们再次采用UNION算法。若查询包含多个相连的

$\operatorname{UNION}\left( {\text{view trees}{T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{n}}\right)$ : tuple components, i.e., it is a Cartesian product of hierarchical queries, we use the Product algorithm with an empty context.

$\operatorname{UNION}\left( {\text{view trees}{T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{n}}\right)$：元组组件（即层次化查询的笛卡尔积），则使用空上下文的Product算法处理。

FIGURE 15. Find the next tuple in a union of view trees.

图15. 在视图树联合中查找下一个元组

<!-- Media -->

The multiplicity for a tuple returned by the UNION algorithm is the sum of the multiplicities of its occurrences across the buckets, while for a tuple returned by the Product algorithm it is the multiplication of the multiplicities of the constituent tuples. Since all tuples in the database have positive multiplicities, the derived multiplicities are always strictly positive and therefore the returned tuple is part of the result.

UNION算法返回的元组重数是其在各桶中出现重数的总和，而Product算法返回的元组重数是组成元组重数的乘积。由于数据库中所有元组都具有正重数，导出重数始终严格为正，因此返回的元组属于结果集。

We next explain the UNION and PRODUCT algorithms used by T.next(   ) in Figure 14.

接下来我们将解释图14中T.next()使用的UNION和PRODUCT算法。

6.1. The Union Algorithm. The UNION algorithm is given in Figure 15. It is an adaptation of prior work [DS11]. It takes as input $n$ view trees that represent possibly overlapping sets of tuples over the same relation and returns a tuple and its multiplicity in the union of these sets, where the tuple is distinct from all tuples returned before.

6.1 联合算法。图15给出了UNION算法，该算法是对前人工作[DS11]的改进。它接收$n$个表示同一关系上可能重叠的元组集合的视图树作为输入，返回这些集合联合中一个元组及其重数，该元组与之前返回的所有元组都不同。

We first explain the algorithm on two views ${T}_{1}$ and ${T}_{2}$ that have been already open and with their iterators positioned at the first respective tuples. On each call, we return one tuple together with its multiplicity or $\mathbf{{EOF}}$ . We check whether the next tuple ${t}_{1}$ in ${T}_{1}$ is also present in ${T}_{2}$ . If so,we return the next tuple in ${T}_{2}$ and its total multiplicity from ${T}_{1}$ and ${T}_{2}$ ; otherwise,we return ${t}_{1}$ and its multiplicity in ${T}_{1}$ . If ${T}_{1}$ is exhausted,we return the next tuple in ${T}_{2}$ and its total multiplicity from ${T}_{1}$ and ${T}_{2}$ ,or EOF if ${T}_{2}$ is also exhausted.

我们首先解释针对两个视图${T}_{1}$和${T}_{2}$的算法，这两个视图已打开且其迭代器定位在各自第一个元组。每次调用时，我们返回一个元组及其重数或$\mathbf{{EOF}}$。我们检查${T}_{1}$中下一个元组${t}_{1}$是否也存在于${T}_{2}$中。若存在，则返回${T}_{2}$中的下一个元组及其在${T}_{1}$和${T}_{2}$中的总重数；否则返回${t}_{1}$及其在${T}_{1}$中的重数。若${T}_{1}$已耗尽，则返回${T}_{2}$中的下一个元组及其在${T}_{1}$和${T}_{2}$中的总重数，若${T}_{2}$也耗尽则返回EOF。

In case of $n > 2$ views,we consider one view defined by the union of the first $n - 1$ views and a second view defined by ${T}_{n}$ ,and we then reduce the general case to the previous case of two views.

对于$n > 2$个视图的情况，我们将前$n - 1$个视图的联合视为第一个视图，${T}_{n}$视为第二个视图，从而将一般情况归约为前述两个视图的情形。

The delay of this algorithm is given by the delay of iterating over each view, the cost of lookups into the views, and the cost of computing output multiplicities. The lookup costs are constant when using a hierarchy of materialized views for representing the query result [OZ15]. Given $n$ views,computing an output multiplicity takes $\mathcal{O}\left( n\right)$ time. The overall delay is the sum of the delays of the $n$ views,which is $\mathcal{O}\left( n\right)$ .

该算法的延迟由遍历每个视图的延迟、视图查找成本和计算输出重数的成本决定。当使用物化视图层次结构表示查询结果[OZ15]时，查找成本为常数。给定$n$个视图，计算输出重数需要$\mathcal{O}\left( n\right)$时间。总延迟是$n$个视图延迟之和，即$\mathcal{O}\left( n\right)$。

In our paper, we employ the UNION algorithm in two cases: (1) on the set of view trees obtained after grounding the heavy indicators; and (2) on the set of view trees obtained by using skew-aware indicators in the preprocessing stage. In the first case, the number of the view trees is in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ ,since the number of heavy tuples in any heavy indicator view is at most ${N}^{1 - \epsilon }$ . In the second case,the number of view trees does not depend on the database size $N$ ,but it may depend exponentially on the number of bound join variables in the input hierarchical query.

本文在两种情况下使用UNION算法：(1)处理重指标物化后获得的视图树集合；(2)预处理阶段使用偏斜感知指标获得的视图树集合。第一种情况的视图树数量属于$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$，因为任何重指标视图中的重元组数量最多为${N}^{1 - \epsilon }$。第二种情况的视图树数量不依赖于数据库大小$N$，但可能随输入层次查询中绑定连接变量的数量呈指数级增长。

FIGURE 16. Find the next tuple in a product of view trees. In case $k = 1$ , the innermost loop is executed.

图16. 在视图树乘积中查找下一个元组。当 $k = 1$ 时，执行最内层循环。

<!-- Media -->

6.2. The Product Algorithm. The Product algorithm is given in Figure 16. It takes as input a set of view trees ${T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{k}$ and a context,which is the current tuple in the parent view, and outputs the next tuple and its multiplicity in the Cartesian product of the tuples returned by the $k$ view trees given the context. By construction,the parent view joins the roots of the $k$ view trees and thus yields only contexts for which each of the view trees produces a non-empty result.

6.2 乘积算法。乘积算法如图16所示，其输入为一组视图树 ${T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{k}$ 和上下文(即父视图中的当前元组)，输出给定上下文下 $k$ 视图树返回元组的笛卡尔积中的下一个元组及其重数。根据构造，父视图会连接 $k$ 视图树的根节点，因此仅生成能使每个视图树产生非空结果的上下文。

In case $k = 1$ ,we execute the innermost loop for ${T}_{k}$ : On a call,we take the current tuple in ${T}_{k}$ and project away the variables that are in common with the context tuple,retaining only the free variables in ${T}_{k}$ . We concatenate this projection with the context tuple. The concatenation operator is $\circ$ . Before we return this concatenated tuple and its multiplicity, we advance the iterator to the next tuple-multiplicity pair in ${T}_{k}$ . Eventually,we reach the end of the iterator for ${T}_{k}$ ,in which case we return $\mathbf{{EOF}}$ .

当 $k = 1$ 时，对 ${T}_{k}$ 执行最内层循环：调用时获取 ${T}_{k}$ 的当前元组，投影掉与上下文元组共有的变量，仅保留 ${T}_{k}$ 中的自由变量。将此投影与上下文元组连接，连接运算符为 $\circ$ 。在返回连接后的元组及其重数前，将迭代器推进到 ${T}_{k}$ 中的下一个元组-重数对。最终当到达 ${T}_{k}$ 迭代器末尾时，返回 $\mathbf{{EOF}}$ 。

In case $k > 1$ ,we hold the current tuple-multiplicity pairs for ${T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{k - 1}$ and iterate over ${T}_{k}$ . Whenever ${T}_{k}$ reaches $\mathbf{{EOF}}$ ,we reset it and advance the iterator for ${T}_{k - 1}$ . We concatenate the context tuple and the current tuples of all iterators, projected onto the variables that are not in the schema of the context tuple (since those fields are already in the context). We multiply the current multiplicities of all iterators and advance the iterator for ${T}_{k}$ before returning the concatenated tuple and its multiplicity.

当 $k > 1$ 时，保持 ${T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{k - 1}$ 的当前元组-重数对，并迭代 ${T}_{k}$ 。每当 ${T}_{k}$ 到达 $\mathbf{{EOF}}$ 时重置之，并推进 ${T}_{k - 1}$ 的迭代器。将上下文元组与所有迭代器的当前元组连接(投影到不在上下文元组模式中的变量上，因为这些字段已在上下文中)，将所有迭代器的当前重数相乘，在返回连接元组及其重数前推进 ${T}_{k}$ 的迭代器。

The delay for a Product call is given by the sum of the delays of the $k$ input view trees. In the worst case,the algorithms makes $k - 1$ open calls and $k$ next calls before returning the next tuple. We use this algorithm in two cases: (1) enumerating from a view with several children in a tree (in which case the context is given as the current tuple in the view); (2) a collection of view trees, one per connected component of the input query (in which case the context is the empty tuple). In both cases, the number of parameters to the PRODUCT call is independent of the size of the database and only dependent on the number of atoms and respectively of connected components in the input query. This means that the delay (in data complexity) is the maximum delay of any of its parameter view trees,which is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ .

乘积调用的延迟由 $k$ 个输入视图树的延迟之和决定。最坏情况下，算法在返回下一个元组前会执行 $k - 1$ 次打开调用和 $k$ 次下一个调用。该算法用于两种场景：(1)从具有多个子节点的树状视图枚举(此时上下文作为视图中的当前元组)；(2)视图树集合(输入查询的每个连通分量对应一个视图树，此时上下文为空元组)。两种情况下，乘积调用的参数数量均与数据库规模无关，仅取决于输入查询的原子数或连通分量数。这意味着(数据复杂度下的)延迟是其参数视图树的最大延迟，即 $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ 。

We next state the complexity of enumeration in our approach.

接下来阐述我们方法中枚举操作的复杂度。

Proposition 6.1. The tuples in the result of a hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ over a database of size $N$ can be enumerated with $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ delay using the view trees constructed by $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ for a canonical variable order $\omega$ for $Q$ .

命题6.1. 对于规模为$N$的数据库，层次化查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$结果中的元组可通过$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$为$Q$构建的规范变量序$\omega$所构造的视图树，以$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$延迟进行枚举。

## 7. UPDATES

## 7. 更新机制

We present our strategy for maintaining the views in the set of view trees $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ constructed for a canonical variable order $\omega$ of a hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ under updates to input relations. We specify here the procedure for processing a single-tuple update to any input relation. Processing a sequence of such updates builds upon this procedure and occasional rebalancing steps (Section 7.2).

我们提出在输入关系更新时，维护为层次化查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$的规范变量序$\omega$构建的视图树集合$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$的策略。此处详述处理单条元组更新的流程，连续更新的处理基于此流程及定期再平衡操作（第7.2节）。

We write ${\delta R} = \{ \mathbf{x} \rightarrow  m\}$ to denote a single-tuple update ${\delta R}$ mapping the tuple $\mathbf{x}$ to the non-zero multiplicity $m \in  \mathbb{Z}$ and any other tuple to 0; i.e., $\left| {\delta R}\right|  = 1$ . Inserts and deletes are updates represented as relations in which tuples have positive and negative multiplicities. We assume that after applying an update to the database, all relations and views contain no tuples with negative multiplicities.

用${\delta R} = \{ \mathbf{x} \rightarrow  m\}$表示将元组$\mathbf{x}$映射为非零重数$m \in  \mathbb{Z}$、其他元组映射为0的单条更新${\delta R}$，即$\left| {\delta R}\right|  = 1$。插入和删除操作被表示为含正负重数元组的关系。假定应用更新后，所有关系和视图不含负重数元组。

Compared to static evaluation, our strategy for dynamic evaluation may construct additional views to support efficient updates to all input relations. In Figure 12, the view tree created for the case of heavy(A,B)-values (second row right) has five such additional views, marked with dashed boxes. These views enable an update to any leaf view to be propagated to the root view in constant time. For instance,the views ${R}^{\prime }$ and ${S}^{\prime }$ eliminate the need to iterate over the $D$ -values in relation $R$ for updates to relation $S$ and $\exists {H}_{B}$ and respectively over the $E$ -values in $S$ for updates to $R$ and $\exists {H}_{B}$ . Figure 8 gives the rule for creating such views: If node $Z$ has a sibling in the variable order,then we create an auxiliary view that aggregates away $Z$ to avoid iterating over the $Z$ -values for updates coming via the (auxiliary) views constructed for the siblings of $Z$ .

相比静态评估，动态评估策略会构建额外视图以支持高效更新所有输入关系。图12中，针对heavy(A,B)值情况构建的视图树（第二行右）包含五个此类附加视图（虚线框标记）。这些视图使得任意叶视图的更新可在常数时间内传播至根视图。例如视图${R}^{\prime }$和${S}^{\prime }$消除了更新$S$和$\exists {H}_{B}$时遍历$R$中$D$值的需求，同理避免更新$R$和$\exists {H}_{B}$时遍历$S$中$E$值。图8给出创建此类视图的规则：若节点$Z$在变量序中有兄弟节点，则创建聚合掉$Z$的辅助视图，避免通过$Z$兄弟节点的（辅助）视图更新时遍历$Z$值。

7.1. Processing a Single-Tuple Update. An update ${\delta R}$ to a relation $R$ may affect multiple view trees in the set of view trees constructed by our algorithm from Figure 11. ${}^{2}$ We apply ${\delta R}$ to each such view tree in sequence,by propagating changes along the path from the leaf $R$ to the root of the view tree. For each view on this path,we update the view result with the change computed using the standard delta rules [CY12] (see Example 9.1). To simplify the reasoning about the maintenance task, we assume that each view tree has a

7.1. 单条更新处理。关系$R$的更新${\delta R}$可能影响图11算法构建的视图树集合中多个视图树。${}^{2}$我们通过沿叶节点$R$到根节点的路径传播变更，依次对每个视图树应用${\delta R}$。对路径上每个视图，使用标准增量规则[CY12]计算变更更新视图结果（见例9.1）。为简化维护任务推理，假定每个视图树具有

Updates to indicator views, however, may trigger further changes in the views constructed over them. Consider,for instance,the heavy indicator ${H}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$ constructed over the view ${Al}{l}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$ and the light indicator $\nexists {L}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$ in Figure 12. An insert ${\delta R} = \{ \left( {a,b,d}\right)  \rightarrow  1\}$ into $R$ may change the multiplicity ${H}_{B}\left( {a,b}\right)$ from 0 to non-zero,thus changing $\exists {H}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$ and its dependent views: ${V}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right) ,{V}_{B}^{\prime }\left( A\right)$ ,and ${V}_{A}\left( A\right)$ . But if the multiplicity ${H}_{B}\left( {a,b}\right)$ stays 0 or non-zero after applying ${\delta R}$ ,then $\exists {H}_{B}$ also stays unchanged.

但指示器视图的更新可能触发其上层视图的级联变更。例如图12中基于视图${Al}{l}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$构建的重指示器${H}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$和轻指示器$\nexists {L}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$。向$R$插入${\delta R} = \{ \left( {a,b,d}\right)  \rightarrow  1\}$可能使多重性${H}_{B}\left( {a,b}\right)$从0变为非零，从而改变$\exists {H}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$及其依赖视图：${V}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right) ,{V}_{B}^{\prime }\left( A\right)$和${V}_{A}\left( A\right)$。但若应用${\delta R}$后多重性${H}_{B}\left( {a,b}\right)$保持0或非零，则$\exists {H}_{B}$亦保持不变。

Figure 18 shows the function UPDATEINDTREE that applies an update ${\delta R}$ to an indicator tree ${T}_{\text{Ind }}$ with a root view $I\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ . The function returns the change $\delta \left( {\exists I}\right)$ in the support of the indicator view $I$ ,to be further propagated to other views. The free variables $\mathcal{S}$ of $I$ appear in each input relation from ${T}_{Ind}$ ,and ${\delta R}$ fixes their values to constants; thus, $\left| {\delta \left( {\exists I}\right) }\right|  \leq  1$ .

图18展示的函数UPDATEINDTREE将更新${\delta R}$应用于根视图为$I\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$的指示树${T}_{\text{Ind }}$，返回指示器视图$I$支持集的变更$\delta \left( {\exists I}\right)$以供后续传播。视图$I$的自由变量$\mathcal{S}$出现在${T}_{Ind}$的每个输入关系中，而${\delta R}$将其值固定为常量，故$\left| {\delta \left( {\exists I}\right) }\right|  \leq  1$。

Figure 19. Updating a set $\mathcal{T}$ of view trees and a set ${\mathcal{T}}_{\text{Ind }}$ of triples of indicator trees for a single-tuple update ${\delta R}$ to relation $R$ .

图19. 为关系 $R$ 的单元组更新 ${\delta R}$ 更新视图树集合 $\mathcal{T}$ 和指示器树三元组集合 ${\mathcal{T}}_{\text{Ind }}$ 。

<!-- Media -->

Figure 19 gives our algorithm for maintaining a set of view trees $\mathcal{T}$ and a set of indicator tress ${T}_{\text{Ind }}$ under an update ${\delta R}$ . We first apply ${\delta R}$ to the view trees from $\mathcal{T}$ (Line 1). Then, we consider the triples $\left( {{T}_{All},{T}_{L},{T}_{H}}\right)$ of indicator trees from ${\mathcal{T}}_{Ind}$ that are affected by ${\delta R}$ . We maintain the heavy indicator tree ${T}_{H}$ with the root $H\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \operatorname{All}\left( \mathcal{S}\right) ,\nexists L\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ for changes in both ${All}$ and $\nexists L$ . We apply ${\delta R}$ to ${T}_{All}$ (Line 6) and subsequently ${\delta All}$ to ${T}_{H}$ (Line 8). The latter may trigger a change $\delta \left( {\exists H}\right)$ in the support of $H$ ,which we apply to the view trees from $\mathcal{T}$ (Line 9). If the update ${\delta R}$ belongs to the light part ${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$ (Line 10),we apply $\delta {R}^{\mathcal{S}}$ to the view trees from $\mathcal{T}$ and to the light indicator tree ${T}_{L}$ (Lines 11-12). We then propagate the opposite change $\delta \left( {\nexists L}\right)$ in the support of the root $L$ of ${T}_{L}$ ,if any,to ${T}_{H}$ and further to the view trees from $\mathcal{T}$ (Lines 13-14).

图19展示了在更新${\delta R}$时维护视图树集合$\mathcal{T}$和指示符树集合${T}_{\text{Ind }}$的算法。我们首先将${\delta R}$应用于$\mathcal{T}$中的视图树（第1行）。接着，考虑${\mathcal{T}}_{Ind}$中受${\delta R}$影响的指示符树三元组$\left( {{T}_{All},{T}_{L},{T}_{H}}\right)$。针对${All}$和$\nexists L$的变更，我们维护以$H\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \operatorname{All}\left( \mathcal{S}\right) ,\nexists L\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$为根的重指示符树${T}_{H}$。将${\delta R}$应用于${T}_{All}$（第6行），随后将${\delta All}$应用于${T}_{H}$（第8行）。后者可能引发$H$支持度的变化$\delta \left( {\exists H}\right)$，我们将其作用于$\mathcal{T}$中的视图树（第9行）。若更新${\delta R}$属于轻量部分${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$（第10行），则对$\mathcal{T}$中的视图树及轻指示符树${T}_{L}$实施$\delta {R}^{\mathcal{S}}$（第11-12行）。随后将${T}_{L}$根节点$L$支持度的反向变化$\delta \left( {\nexists L}\right)$（如有）传递至${T}_{H}$，并进一步作用于$\mathcal{T}$中的视图树（第13-14行）。

Example 7.1. We analyze the time needed to maintain the views from Figure 12 under a single-tuple update to any input relation. For the view tree constructed for the case of heavy(A,B)-values (second row right),propagating an update from any relation to the root view takes constant time. For instance,an update ${\delta R}$ to $R$ changes the view ${R}^{\prime }\left( {A,B}\right)$ with $\delta {R}^{\prime }\left( {a,b}\right)  = {\delta R}\left( {a,b,d}\right)$ ; the view ${V}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$ with $\delta {V}_{B}\left( {a,b}\right)  = \exists {H}_{B}\left( {a,b}\right) ,\delta {R}^{\prime }\left( {a,b}\right) ,{S}^{\prime }\left( {a,b}\right)$ ; changes to the views ${V}_{B}^{\prime }\left( A\right)$ and ${V}_{A}\left( A\right)$ are similar. The auxiliary views ${S}^{\prime }\left( {A,B}\right)$ and ${V}_{C}^{\prime }\left( A\right)$ enable the constant-time updates in this case by aggregating away the $E$ -values in $S\left( {A,B,E}\right)$ and the $C$ -values in ${V}_{C}\left( {A,B}\right)$ .

示例7.1. 我们分析在单条元组更新任意输入关系时，维护图12视图所需的时间。对于针对heavy(A,B)值情况构建的视图树（第二行右侧），从任意关系传播更新至根视图仅需常数时间。例如，对$R$的更新${\delta R}$会改变含$\delta {R}^{\prime }\left( {a,b}\right)  = {\delta R}\left( {a,b,d}\right)$的视图${R}^{\prime }\left( {A,B}\right)$；含$\delta {V}_{B}\left( {a,b}\right)  = \exists {H}_{B}\left( {a,b}\right) ,\delta {R}^{\prime }\left( {a,b}\right) ,{S}^{\prime }\left( {a,b}\right)$的视图${V}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$；视图${V}_{B}^{\prime }\left( A\right)$和${V}_{A}\left( A\right)$的变更类似。辅助视图${S}^{\prime }\left( {A,B}\right)$和${V}_{C}^{\prime }\left( A\right)$通过聚合掉$S\left( {A,B,E}\right)$中的$E$值和${V}_{C}\left( {A,B}\right)$中的$C$值，实现了该场景下的常数时间更新。

Consider now the view tree defined over the light parts of input relations (bottom-left). The update ${\delta R} = \{ \left( {a,b,d}\right)  \rightarrow  m\}$ affects the light part ${R}^{A}$ of $R$ when $\left( {a,b,d}\right)  \notin  R$ or $a \in  {\pi }_{A}{R}^{A}$ . If so,computing $\delta {V}_{B}\left( {a,d,E}\right)  = \delta {R}^{A}\left( {a,b,d}\right) ,{S}^{A}\left( {a,b,E}\right)$ takes $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$ time since $a$ is light in ${S}^{A}$ . The size of $\delta {V}_{B}$ is also $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$ . Computing $\delta {V}_{A}$ at the root requires pairing each $E$ -value from $\delta {V}_{B}$ with the(C,F)-values in ${V}_{C}$ for the given $a$ . Since $a$ is light in ${T}^{A}$ , the number of such(C,F)-values in ${T}^{A}$ is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$ . Thus,computing $\delta {V}_{A}$ takes $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{2\epsilon }\right)$ time. A similar analysis shows that updates to ${S}^{A}$ and ${T}^{A}$ also take $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{2\epsilon }\right)$ time,while updates to ${U}^{A}$ take $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{3\epsilon }\right)$ time.

现在考虑定义在输入关系轻量部分上的视图树（左下）。当$\left( {a,b,d}\right)  \notin  R$或$a \in  {\pi }_{A}{R}^{A}$时，更新${\delta R} = \{ \left( {a,b,d}\right)  \rightarrow  m\}$会影响$R$的轻量部分${R}^{A}$。若成立，由于$a$在${S}^{A}$中属于轻量级，计算$\delta {V}_{B}\left( {a,d,E}\right)  = \delta {R}^{A}\left( {a,b,d}\right) ,{S}^{A}\left( {a,b,E}\right)$仅需$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$时间。$\delta {V}_{B}$的大小同样为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$。在根节点计算$\delta {V}_{A}$需要将$\delta {V}_{B}$中的每个$E$值与给定$a$对应的${V}_{C}$内(C,F)值配对。由于$a$在${T}^{A}$中为轻量级，该关系中的(C,F)值数量为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$。因此，计算$\delta {V}_{A}$耗时$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{2\epsilon }\right)$。类似分析表明，对${S}^{A}$和${T}^{A}$的更新也仅需$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{2\epsilon }\right)$时间，而对${U}^{A}$的更新则需$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{3\epsilon }\right)$时间。

FIGURE 20. Recomputing the light parts of base relations and affected views.

图20. 重新计算基础关系及受影响视图的轻量部分。

<!-- Media -->

For the view tree constructed for the case of heavy $A$ -values (bottom-middle),updates to ${R}^{AB}$ and ${S}^{AB}$ take $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$ time,while updates to $T$ and $U$ take constant time. The indicator view trees (top and middle row) encode the results of ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical queries,thus maintaining their views takes constant time per update.

针对$A$值重型情况构建的视图树（中下），对${R}^{AB}$和${S}^{AB}$的更新需$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$时间，而对$T$和$U$的更新仅需常数时间。指示符视图树（顶部及中间行）编码了${\delta }_{0}$层次查询结果，因此其视图维护每次更新仅需常数时间。

The indicator views $\exists {H}_{A}\left( A\right)$ and $\exists {H}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$ may change under updates to any relation and respectively under updates to $R$ and $S$ . For instance,the update ${\delta R}$ can trigger a new single-tuple change in $\exists {H}_{A}$ when the multiplicity ${H}_{A}\left( a\right)$ increases from 0 to non-zero or vice versa. Applying this change $\delta \left( {\exists {H}_{A}}\right)$ to the view trees containing $\exists {H}_{A}$ takes constant time; the same holds for propagating a change $\delta \left( {\exists {H}_{B}}\right)$ to the view trees containing $\exists {H}_{B}$ .

指示视图$\exists {H}_{A}\left( A\right)$和$\exists {H}_{B}\left( {A,B}\right)$可能随任意关系更新而变化，或分别随$R$和$S$的更新而变化。例如，当重数${H}_{A}\left( a\right)$从0增至非零或反之，更新${\delta R}$可能触发$\exists {H}_{A}$中新的单元组变更。将此变更$\delta \left( {\exists {H}_{A}}\right)$应用到包含$\exists {H}_{A}$的视图树仅需常数时间；同理，将变更$\delta \left( {\exists {H}_{B}}\right)$传播至包含$\exists {H}_{B}$的视图树亦然。

In conclusion, maintaining the views from Figure 12 under a single-tuple update to any relation takes $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{3\epsilon }\right)$ overall time.

综上所述，在任意关系的单元组更新下，维护图12所示视图总体耗时$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{3\epsilon }\right)$。

We next state the complexity of updates in our approach.

接下来阐述本方法中更新操作的复杂度。

Proposition 7.2. Given a hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ with dynamic width $\delta$ ,a canonical variable order $\omega$ for $Q$ ,a database of size $N$ ,and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,maintaining the views in the set of view trees $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ under a single-tuple update to any input relation takes $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ time.

命题7.2。给定一个具有动态宽度$\delta$的层次化查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$，针对$Q$的规范变量序$\omega$，规模为$N$的数据库，以及$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，在单次元组更新任意输入关系时，维护视图树集$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$中的视图需要$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$时间。

7.2. Rebalancing Partitions. As the database evolves under updates, we periodically rebalance the relation partitions and views to account for a new database size and updated degrees of data values. The cost of rebalancing is amortized over a sequence of updates.

7.2. 分区再平衡。随着数据库在更新过程中演进，我们会定期对关系分区和视图进行再平衡，以适应新的数据库规模和数据值的更新程度。再平衡的成本被分摊到一系列更新操作中。

Major Rebalancing. We loosen the partition threshold to amortize the cost of rebalancing over multiple updates. Instead of the actual database size $N$ ,the threshold now depends on a number $M$ for which the invariant $\left\lfloor  {\frac{1}{4}M}\right\rfloor   \leq  N < M$ always holds. If the database size falls below $\left\lfloor  {\frac{1}{4}M}\right\rfloor$ or reaches $M$ ,we perform major rebalancing,where we halve or respectively double $\bar{M}$ ,followed by strictly repartitioning the light parts of input relations with the new threshold ${M}^{\epsilon }$ and recomputing the views. Figure 20 shows the major rebalancing procedure.

主再平衡。我们放宽分区阈值，将再平衡成本分摊到多次更新中。阈值不再基于实际数据库规模$N$，而是取决于满足不变式$\left\lfloor  {\frac{1}{4}M}\right\rfloor   \leq  N < M$的数值$M$。若数据库规模低于$\left\lfloor  {\frac{1}{4}M}\right\rfloor$或达到$M$，则执行主再平衡：将$\bar{M}$减半或翻倍，随后用新阈值${M}^{\epsilon }$严格重分区输入关系的轻量部分并重新计算视图。图20展示了主再平衡流程。

Proposition 7.3. Given a hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ with static width $\mathrm{w}$ ,a canonical variable order $\omega$ for $Q$ ,a database of size $N$ ,and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,major rebalancing of the views in the set of view trees $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ takes $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {w - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ time.

命题7.3。给定一个具有静态宽度$\mathrm{w}$的层次化查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$，针对$Q$的规范变量序$\omega$，规模为$N$的数据库，以及$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，对视图树集$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$中的视图进行主再平衡需要$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {w - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$时间。

The cost of major rebalancing is amortized over $\Omega \left( M\right)$ updates. After a major rebalancing step,it holds that $N = \frac{1}{2}M$ (after doubling),or $N = \frac{1}{2}M - \frac{1}{2}$ or $N = \frac{1}{2}M - 1$ (after halving). To violate the size invariant $\left\lfloor  {\frac{1}{4}M}\right\rfloor   \leq  N < M$ and trigger another major rebalancing,the number of required updates is at least $\frac{1}{4}M$ . In the extended technical report,we prove the amortized $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ time of major rebalancing [KNOZ19]. By Proposition 4.8,we have $\delta  = \mathrm{w}$ or $\delta  = \mathrm{w} - 1$ ; hence,the amortized major rebalancing time is $\mathcal{O}\left( {M}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ .

主再平衡成本被分摊到$\Omega \left( M\right)$次更新中。主再平衡步骤后满足$N = \frac{1}{2}M$（翻倍后），或$N = \frac{1}{2}M - \frac{1}{2}$或$N = \frac{1}{2}M - 1$（减半后）。要违反规模不变式$\left\lfloor  {\frac{1}{4}M}\right\rfloor   \leq  N < M$并触发另一次主再平衡，至少需要$\frac{1}{4}M$次更新。在扩展技术报告中，我们证明了主再平衡的摊还$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$时间[KNOZ19]。根据命题4.8，可得$\delta  = \mathrm{w}$或$\delta  = \mathrm{w} - 1$；因此摊还主再平衡时间为$\mathcal{O}\left( {M}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$。

	对每个$T \in  \mathcal{T}$执行$\operatorname{AppLY}\left( {T,\delta \left( {\exists H}\right) }\right)$

FIGURE 21. Deleting heavy tuples from or inserting light tuples into the light part of relation $R$ .

图21. 从关系$R$的轻量部分删除重型元组或插入轻型元组

<!-- Media -->

Minor Rebalancing. After an update ${\delta R} = \{ \mathbf{x} \rightarrow  m\}$ to relation $R$ ,we check the light part and heavy part conditions of each partition of $R$ . Consider the light part ${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$ of $R$ partitioned on a schema $\mathcal{S}$ . If the number of tuples in ${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$ that agree with $\mathbf{x}$ on $\mathcal{S}$ exceeds $\frac{3}{2}{M}^{\epsilon }$ ,then we delete those tuples from ${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$ . If the number of tuples that agree with $\mathbf{x}$ on $\mathcal{S}$ in ${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$ is zero and in $R$ is below $\frac{1}{2}{M}^{\epsilon }$ ,then we insert those tuples into ${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$ . Figure 21 shows this minor rebalancing procedure.

次要再平衡。对关系$R$进行更新${\delta R} = \{ \mathbf{x} \rightarrow  m\}$后，我们检查$R$每个分区的轻量部分和重型部分条件。考虑按模式$\mathcal{S}$分区的$R$的轻量部分${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$。若${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$中与$\mathbf{x}$在$\mathcal{S}$上匹配的元组数量超过$\frac{3}{2}{M}^{\epsilon }$，则从${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$删除这些元组。若${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$中与$\mathbf{x}$在$\mathcal{S}$上匹配的元组数量为零，且$R$中该数量低于$\frac{1}{2}{M}^{\epsilon }$，则将这些元组插入${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$。图21展示了该次要再平衡过程。

Proposition 7.4. Given a hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ with dynamic width $\delta$ ,a canonical variable order $\omega$ for $Q$ ,a database of size $N$ ,and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,minor rebalancing of the views in the set of view trees $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ takes $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\left( {\delta  + 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ time.

命题7.4. 给定动态宽度为$\delta$的层次化查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$、$Q$的规范变量序$\omega$、规模为$N$的数据库及$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，对视图树集$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$中视图进行次要再平衡耗时$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\left( {\delta  + 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$。

The cost of minor rebalancing is amortized over $\Omega \left( {M}^{\epsilon }\right)$ updates. This lower bound on the number of updates is due to the gap between the two thresholds in the heavy and light part conditions. The extended technical report proves the amortized $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ time of minor rebalancing [KNOZ19].

次要再平衡的成本分摊在$\Omega \left( {M}^{\epsilon }\right)$次更新中。该更新次数的下限源于轻重部分条件中两个阈值间的差距。扩展技术报告[KNOZ19]证明了次要再平衡的分摊时间复杂度为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$。

Figure 22 gives the trigger procedure ONUPDATE that maintains a set of view trees $\mathcal{T}$ and a set of indicator trees ${\mathcal{T}}_{\text{Ind }}$ under a sequence of single-tuple updates to input relations. We first apply an update ${\delta R}$ to the view trees from $\mathcal{T}$ and indicator trees from ${\mathcal{T}}_{\text{Ind }}$ using UPDATETREES from Figure 19. If this update leads to a violation of the size invariant $\left\lfloor  {\frac{1}{4}M}\right\rfloor   \leq  N < M$ ,we invoke MAJORREBALANCING to recompute the light parts of the input relations and affected views. Otherwise,for each triple of indicator trees from ${\mathcal{T}}_{\text{Ind }}$ with the light part ${R}^{\mathcal{F}}$ partitioned on $\mathcal{F}$ ,we check if the heavy or light condition is violated; if so,we invoke MinorREBALANCING to move the $R$ -tuples having the $\mathcal{F}$ -values of the update ${\delta R}$ either into or from the light part ${R}^{\mathcal{F}}$ of relation $R$ .

图22展示了触发器过程ONUPDATE，该过程在输入关系的一系列单元组更新下维护视图树集$\mathcal{T}$和指示符树集${\mathcal{T}}_{\text{Ind }}$。我们首先使用图19中的UPDATETREES方法，将更新${\delta R}$应用于来自$\mathcal{T}$的视图树和来自${\mathcal{T}}_{\text{Ind }}$的指示符树。若此更新导致规模不变量$\left\lfloor  {\frac{1}{4}M}\right\rfloor   \leq  N < M$被违反，则调用MAJORREBALANCING重新计算输入关系及受影响视图的轻量部分。否则，对于来自${\mathcal{T}}_{\text{Ind }}$且按$\mathcal{F}$分区轻量部分${R}^{\mathcal{F}}$的每组指示符树三元组，检查是否违反重/轻条件；若违反，则调用MinorREBALANCING将具有更新${\delta R}$的$\mathcal{F}$值的$R$-元组移入或移出关系$R$的轻量部分${R}^{\mathcal{F}}$。

We state the amortized maintenance time of our approach under a sequence of single-tuple updates.

我们阐述了在单元组更新序列下本方法的摊还维护时间。

Proposition 7.5. Given a hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ with dynamic width $\delta$ ,a canonical variable order $\omega$ for $Q$ ,a database of size $N$ ,and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,maintaining the views in the set of view trees $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ under a sequence of single-tuple updates takes $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ amortized time per single-tuple update.

命题7.5。给定动态宽度为$\delta$的层次化查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$、$Q$的规范变量序$\omega$、规模为$N$的数据库及$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，在单元组更新序列下维护视图树集$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$中的视图，每次单元组更新需消耗$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$摊还时间。

若$\left( {N = M}\right)$

	$M = {2M}$

	MAJORREBALANCING $\left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{T}}_{\text{Ind }},{M}^{\epsilon }}\right)$

	主重平衡$\left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{T}}_{\text{Ind }},{M}^{\epsilon }}\right)$

else if $\left( {N < \left\lfloor  {\frac{1}{4}M}\right\rfloor  }\right)$

否则若$\left( {N < \left\lfloor  {\frac{1}{4}M}\right\rfloor  }\right)$

	$M = \left\lfloor  {\frac{1}{2}M}\right\rfloor   - 1$

	MAJORREBALANCING $\left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{T}}_{\text{Ind }},{M}^{\epsilon }}\right)$

	主重平衡$\left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{T}}_{\text{Ind }},{M}^{\epsilon }}\right)$

else

否则

	foreach $\left( {{T}_{All},{T}_{L},{T}_{H}}\right)  \in  {\mathcal{T}}_{\text{Ind }}$ such that $R \in  {T}_{All}$ do

	对每个满足$R \in  {T}_{All}$的$\left( {{T}_{All},{T}_{L},{T}_{H}}\right)  \in  {\mathcal{T}}_{\text{Ind }}$执行

		let ${R}^{\mathcal{F}} \in  {T}_{L}$ be light part of $R$ partitioned on $\mathcal{F}$

		设${R}^{\mathcal{F}} \in  {T}_{L}$为按$\mathcal{F}$分区的$R$轻量部分

		let $\operatorname{key} = \mathbf{x}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{F}\right\rbrack$ ,where ${\delta R} = \{ \mathbf{x} \rightarrow  m\}$

		设$\operatorname{key} = \mathbf{x}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{F}\right\rbrack$，其中${\delta R} = \{ \mathbf{x} \rightarrow  m\}$

		if $\left( {\left| {{\sigma }_{\mathcal{F} = {key}}{R}^{\mathcal{F}}}\right|  = 0 \land  \left| {{\sigma }_{\mathcal{F} = {key}}R}\right|  < \frac{1}{2}{M}^{\epsilon }}\right)$

		若$\left( {\left| {{\sigma }_{\mathcal{F} = {key}}{R}^{\mathcal{F}}}\right|  = 0 \land  \left| {{\sigma }_{\mathcal{F} = {key}}R}\right|  < \frac{1}{2}{M}^{\epsilon }}\right)$

			MinorRebalancing $\left( {\mathcal{T},{T}_{L},{T}_{H},R,\text{key,true}}\right)$

			次重平衡$\left( {\mathcal{T},{T}_{L},{T}_{H},R,\text{key,true}}\right)$

		else if $\left( {\left| {{\sigma }_{\mathcal{F} = {key}}{R}^{\mathcal{F}}}\right|  \geq  \frac{3}{2}{M}^{\epsilon }}\right)$

		否则若$\left( {\left| {{\sigma }_{\mathcal{F} = {key}}{R}^{\mathcal{F}}}\right|  \geq  \frac{3}{2}{M}^{\epsilon }}\right)$

			MinorRebalancing $\left( {\mathcal{T},{T}_{L},{T}_{H},R,\text{key,false}}\right)$

			小幅平衡调整 $\left( {\mathcal{T},{T}_{L},{T}_{H},R,\text{key,false}}\right)$

The proof of Proposition 7.5 is based on prior work (Section 4.1 in $\left\lbrack  {{\mathrm{{KNN}}}^{ + }{19}\mathrm{a}}\right\rbrack$ ). The adapted proof is given in the technical report (Section F.4 in [KNOZ19]).

命题7.5的证明基于前人工作（$\left\lbrack  {{\mathrm{{KNN}}}^{ + }{19}\mathrm{a}}\right\rbrack$ 第4.1节）。改编后的证明见技术报告（[KNOZ19]第F.4节）。

## 8. Matching Lower Bound for ${\delta }_{1}$ -Hierarchical Queries

## 8. ${\delta }_{1}$层次化查询的匹配下界

Corollary 4.14 says that,given a database of size $N$ and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,any ${\delta }_{i}$ -hierarchical query with $i \in  \mathbb{N}$ can be evaluated with $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{i\epsilon }\right)$ amortized update time and $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ enumeration delay. For ${\delta }_{1}$ -hierarchical queries,this upper bound is matched by a lower bound conditioned on the Online Matrix-Vector Multiplication Conjecture [HKNS15]. The following proposition extends the lower bound result from prior work [BKS18] to amortized update time. The adapted proof can be found in the technical report (Proposition 10 in [KNOZ19]).

推论4.14指出：给定大小为$N$和$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$的数据库，任何具有$i \in  \mathbb{N}$的${\delta }_{i}$层次化查询可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{i\epsilon }\right)$摊销更新时间和$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$枚举延迟下求值。对于${\delta }_{1}$层次化查询，该上界与基于在线矩阵向量乘法猜想[HKNS15]的下界相匹配。以下命题将前人工作[BKS18]的下界结果扩展至摊销更新时间。改编证明见技术报告（[KNOZ19]命题10）。

Proposition 8.1. Given a ${\delta }_{1}$ -hierarchical query without repeating relation symbols, $\gamma  > 0$ , and a database of size $N$ ,there is no algorithm that maintains the query with arbitrary preprocessing time, $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{1}{2} - \gamma }\right)$ amortized update time,and $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{1}{2} - \gamma }\right)$ enumeration delay, unless the Online Matrix-Vector Multiplication conjecture fails.

命题8.1. 给定无重复关系符号的${\delta }_{1}$层次化查询$\gamma  > 0$，及大小为$N$的数据库，不存在能在任意预处理时间下，以$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{1}{2} - \gamma }\right)$摊销更新时间和$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{1}{2} - \gamma }\right)$枚举延迟维护该查询的算法，除非在线矩阵向量乘法猜想不成立。

<!-- figureText: $\left( {\frac{3}{2},\frac{1}{2},\frac{1}{2}}\right)$ ${\log }_{N}$ update time (2,1,0) ${\log }_{N}$ preprocessing time weakly Pareto optimal -->

<img src="https://cdn.noedgeai.com/019653eb-0067-7ccd-b18a-6871af36c114_29.jpg?x=269&y=1816&w=450&h=297&r=0"/>

<!-- Media -->

The blue line connecting the points(1,0,1)and (2,1,0)in the left figure visualizes the trade-offs of our approach for ${\delta }_{1}$ -hierarchical queries. The gray cuboid is infinite in the dimension of preprocessing time. Each point strictly included in the gray cuboid corresponds to a combination of some preprocessing time and $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{1}{2} - \gamma }\right)$ amortized update time and delay for $\gamma  > 0$ . Following Proposition 8.1, this is not attainable, unless the Online Matrix-Vector Multiplication conjecture fails. Each point on the surface of the cuboid corresponds to Pareto worst-case optimality in the update-delay trade-off space. For $\epsilon  = \frac{1}{2}$ ,our approach needs $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)$ amortized update time and delay, which is weakly Pareto worst-case optimal: there can be no tighter upper bounds for both the update time and delay. Since $w \in  \{ 1,2\}$ for ${\delta }_{1}$ -hierarchical queries,the preprocessing time is $O\left( {N}^{\frac{3}{2}}\right)$ .

左图中连接点(1,0,1)与(2,1,0)的蓝线展示了${\delta }_{1}$层次化查询方法的权衡空间。灰色长方体在预处理时间维度无限延伸。严格包含于长方体内的每个点对应某种预处理时间与$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{1}{2} - \gamma }\right)$摊销更新时间及$\gamma  > 0$延迟的组合。根据命题8.1，除非在线矩阵向量乘法猜想不成立，否则无法实现。长方体表面各点对应更新-延迟权衡空间中的帕累托最坏情况最优解。对于$\epsilon  = \frac{1}{2}$，本方法需要$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)$摊销更新时间和延迟，属弱帕累托最坏情况最优：更新时间和延迟不存在更紧上界。因${\delta }_{1}$层次化查询中$w \in  \{ 1,2\}$，预处理时间为$O\left( {N}^{\frac{3}{2}}\right)$。

## 9. Examples Showcasing Our Approach

## 9. 方法示例

We exemplify our approach for the static and dynamic evaluation of two ${\delta }_{1}$ -hierarchical queries. We start with the query from Example 2.2.

我们通过两个${\delta }_{1}$层次化查询的静态与动态求值示例演示本方法。首先分析示例2.2的查询。

Example 9.1. Consider the ${\delta }_{1}$ -hierarchical and non-free-connex query $Q\left( {A,C}\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right)$ , $S\left( {B,C}\right)$ from Example 2.2 whose relations have size at most $N$ . We partition $R$ and $S$ on $B$ : A $B$ -value $b$ is light in $R$ if $\left| {\{ a \mid  \left( {a,b}\right)  \in  R\} }\right|  \leq  {N}^{\epsilon }$ and heavy otherwise (similar for $S$ ). Since each heavy $B$ -value is paired with at least ${N}^{\epsilon }A$ -values in $R$ ,there are at most ${N}^{1 - \epsilon }$ heavy $B$ -values. There are four cases to consider: $B$ is either light or heavy in each of $R$ and $S$ . We can reduce them to two cases: either $B$ is light in both relations,or $B$ is heavy in at least one of them. We keep the light/heavy information in two indicator views: ${L}_{B}\left( B\right)  = {R}^{B}\left( {A,B}\right) ,{S}^{B}\left( {B,C}\right)$ ,where ${R}^{B}$ and ${S}^{B}$ are the light parts of $R$ and respectively $S$ ; and ${H}_{B}\left( B\right)  = {Al}{l}_{B}\left( B\right) ,\nexists {L}_{B}\left( B\right)$ ,where ${Al}{l}_{B}\left( B\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( {B,C}\right)$ . The $\exists$ operator before indicators denotes their use with set semantics, i.e., the tuple multiplicities are 0 or 1 . The $\nexists$ operator flips the multiplicity.

示例9.1 考虑例2.2中具有${\delta }_{1}$层次结构且非自由连接的查询$Q\left( {A,C}\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right)$和$S\left( {B,C}\right)$，其关系大小最多为$N$。我们对$R$和$S$按$B$进行划分：若$\left| {\{ a \mid  \left( {a,b}\right)  \in  R\} }\right|  \leq  {N}^{\epsilon }$，则称$B$值$b$在$R$中为轻量值，否则为重量值（$S$同理）。由于每个重量级$B$值在$R$中至少对应${N}^{\epsilon }A$个值，故重量级$B$值最多有${N}^{1 - \epsilon }$个。需考虑四种情况：$B$在$R$和$S$中分别为轻量或重量值。可简化为两种情况：$B$在两个关系中均为轻量值，或至少在一个关系中为重量值。我们通过两个指示视图${L}_{B}\left( B\right)  = {R}^{B}\left( {A,B}\right) ,{S}^{B}\left( {B,C}\right)$（其中${R}^{B}$和${S}^{B}$分别是$R$和$S$的轻量部分）及${H}_{B}\left( B\right)  = {Al}{l}_{B}\left( B\right) ,\nexists {L}_{B}\left( B\right)$（其中${Al}{l}_{B}\left( B\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( {B,C}\right)$）来记录轻重状态。指示视图前的$\exists$运算符表示采用集合语义（即元组重复度为0或1），$\nexists$运算符则用于翻转重复度。

Figure 23 gives the evaluation and maintenance strategies for our query. A strategy is depicted by a view tree, with one view per node such that the head of the view is depicted at the node and its body is the join of its children.

图23展示了本查询的求值与维护策略。策略通过视图树呈现，每个节点对应一个视图，视图头部显示于节点处，其主体为子节点的连接结果。

To support light/heavy partitions,we need to keep the degree information of the $B$ - values in the two relations. The light/heavy indicators can be computed in linear time, e.g., for ${L}_{B}$ we start with the light parts of $R$ and $S$ ,aggregate away $A$ and respectively $C$ and then join them on $B$ .

为支持轻重划分，需要维护两个关系中$B$值的度数信息。轻重指示器可在线性时间内计算，例如对${L}_{B}$，我们从$R$和$S$的轻量部分出发，先聚合移除$A$和$C$，再按$B$进行连接。

If $B$ is light,we compute the view ${V}_{B}\left( {A,C}\right)$ in time $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \epsilon }\right)$ : We iterate over ${S}^{B}$ and for each of its tuples(b,c),we fetch the $A$ -values in ${R}^{B}$ paired with $b$ in $R$ . The iteration over ${S}^{B}$ takes linear time and for each $b$ there are at most ${N}^{\epsilon }A$ -values in $R$ . The view ${V}_{B}\left( {A,C}\right)$ is a subset of $Q$ ’s result.

若$B$为轻量值，可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \epsilon }\right)$时间内计算视图${V}_{B}\left( {A,C}\right)$：遍历${S}^{B}$时，对每个元组(b,c)获取${R}^{B}$中与$R$里$b$配对的$A$值。${S}^{B}$的遍历耗时线性，且每个$b$在$R$中最多对应${N}^{\epsilon }A$个值。视图${V}_{B}\left( {A,C}\right)$是$Q$结果的子集。

If $B$ is heavy,we construct the view ${V}_{B}\left( B\right)$ with up to ${N}^{1 - \epsilon }$ heavy $B$ -values. For each such value $b$ ,we can enumerate the distinct tuples(a,c)such that $R\left( {a,b}\right)$ and $S\left( {b,c}\right)$ hold. Distinct $B$ -values may,however,have the same tuple(a,c). Therefore,if we were to enumerate such tuples for one $B$ -value after those for another $B$ -value,the same tuple(a,c) may be output several times, which violates the enumeration constraint. To address this challenge,we use the union algorithm [DS11]. We use the ${N}^{1 - \epsilon }$ buckets of(a,c)tuples,one for each heavy $B$ -value,and an extra bucket ${V}_{B}\left( {A,C}\right)$ constructed in the light case. From each bucket of a $B$ -value,we can enumerate the distinct(a,c)tuples with constant delay by looking up into $R$ and $S$ . The tuples in the materialized view ${V}_{B}\left( {A,C}\right)$ can be enumerated with constant delay. We then use the union algorithm to enumerate the distinct(a,c)tuples with delay given by the sum of the delays of the buckets. For each such tuple, we sum up the positive multiplicities of its occurrences in the buckets. This yields an overall $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ delay for the enumeration of the distinct tuples in the result of $Q$ .

若$B$值频次较高，则构建最多包含${N}^{1 - \epsilon }$个高频$B$值的视图${V}_{B}\left( B\right)$。对于每个此类值$b$，可枚举满足$R\left( {a,b}\right)$与$S\left( {b,c}\right)$条件的唯一元组(a,c)。但不同$B$值可能对应相同元组(a,c)。因此若按$B$值顺序枚举这些元组，会导致同一元组(a,c)被多次输出，违反枚举约束。为解决此问题，我们采用联合算法[DS11]。使用${N}^{1 - \epsilon }$个(a,c)元组桶（每个高频$B$值对应一个桶）及轻量情况下构建的额外桶${V}_{B}\left( {A,C}\right)$。通过查询$R$和$S$，可以恒定延迟从每个$B$值对应的桶中枚举唯一(a,c)元组。物化视图${V}_{B}\left( {A,C}\right)$中的元组同样支持恒定延迟枚举。随后运用联合算法，以各桶延迟之和为总延迟来枚举唯一(a,c)元组。对每个枚举出的元组，累加其在各桶中的正重复次数。最终实现$Q$结果中唯一元组枚举的总体$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$延迟。

<!-- Media -->

<!-- figureText: ${Al}{l}_{B}\left( B\right)$ ${L}_{B}\left( B\right)$ ${H}_{B}\left( B\right)$ ${L}_{C}\left( B\right)$ ${S}^{B}\left( {B,\underline{C}}\right)$ ${Al}{l}_{B}\left( B\right)$ $\nexists {L}_{B}\left( B\right)$ ${V}_{B}\left( B\right)$ $R\left( {\underline{A},B}\right) S\left( {B,\underline{C}}\right)$ ${Al}{l}_{A}\left( B\right)$ ${Al}{l}_{C}\left( B\right)$ ${L}_{A}\left( B\right)$ $R\left( {\underline{A},B}\right)$ $S\left( {B,\underline{C}}\right)$ ${R}^{B}\left( {\underline{A},B}\right)$ ${V}_{B}\left( {\underline{A},\underline{C}}\right)$ ${R}^{B}\left( {\underline{A},B}\right)$ ${S}^{B}\left( {B,\underline{C}}\right)$ -->

<img src="https://cdn.noedgeai.com/019653eb-0067-7ccd-b18a-6871af36c114_31.jpg?x=344&y=327&w=1105&h=417&r=0"/>

FIGURE 23. The view trees for $Q\left( {A,C}\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( {B,C}\right)$ in Example 9.1. The dashed boxes enclose views that are only needed in the dynamic case.

图23. 示例9.1中$Q\left( {A,C}\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( {B,C}\right)$的视图树结构。虚线框标注的视图仅在动态场景下需要。

<!-- Media -->

We now turn to the dynamic case. The preprocessing time and delay remain the same as in the static case,while each single-tuple update can be processed in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$ amortized time. To support updates, we need to maintain tuple multiplicities in addition to the degree information of the $B$ -values in the two relations. The multiplicity of a result tuple is the sum of the multiplicities of its duplicates across the $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ buckets. We also need two views to support efficient updates to $R$ and $S$ ; these are marked with the dashed boxes in Figure 23. For simplicity, we assume that each view tree maintains copies of its base relations.

现转向动态场景分析。预处理时间与延迟保持静态场景相同，而单元组更新可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$摊还时间内完成。为支持更新操作，除维护两个关系中$B$值的度信息外，还需保持元组重复计数。结果元组的重复度是其跨$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$个桶中所有重复项重复次数之和。另需两个视图支持$R$和$S$的高效更新，这些视图在图23中用虚线框标注。为简化起见，假定每个视图树均维护其基关系副本。

Consider a single-tuple update ${\delta R} = \{ \left( {a,b}\right)  \rightarrow  m\}$ to relation $R$ . We maintain each view affected by ${\delta R}$ using the hierarchy of materialized views from Figure 23. The changes in those views are expressed using the classical delta rules [CY12]. We update the views ${R}^{\prime }\left( B\right)$ and ${V}_{B}\left( B\right)$ in the bottom-right tree with $\delta {R}^{\prime }\left( b\right)  = {\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right)$ and $\delta {V}_{B}\left( b\right)  = \exists {H}_{B}\left( b\right) ,\delta {R}^{\prime }\left( b\right) ,{S}^{\prime }\left( b\right)$ in constant time; the same holds for updating the views ${Al}{l}_{A}\left( B\right) ,{Al}{l}_{B}\left( B\right)$ ,and ${H}_{B}\left( B\right)$ .

考虑对关系$R$的单元组更新${\delta R} = \{ \left( {a,b}\right)  \rightarrow  m\}$。通过图23的物化视图层级结构维护受${\delta R}$影响的每个视图。这些视图的变更通过经典增量规则[CY12]实现。以恒定时间更新右下角树中的视图${R}^{\prime }\left( B\right)$和${V}_{B}\left( B\right)$（分别对应$\delta {R}^{\prime }\left( b\right)  = {\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right)$和$\delta {V}_{B}\left( b\right)  = \exists {H}_{B}\left( b\right) ,\delta {R}^{\prime }\left( b\right) ,{S}^{\prime }\left( b\right)$），更新视图${Al}{l}_{A}\left( B\right) ,{Al}{l}_{B}\left( B\right)$和${H}_{B}\left( B\right)$同理。

The update ${\delta R}$ affects the light part ${R}^{B}$ of $R$ if the $B$ -value $b$ already exists among the $B$ -values in ${R}^{B}$ or does not exist in $R$ . For such change $\delta {R}^{B}$ ,we update ${V}_{B}\left( {A,C}\right)$ with $\delta {V}_{B}\left( {a,C}\right)  = \delta {R}^{B}\left( {a,b}\right) ,{S}^{B}\left( {b,C}\right)$ in time $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$ since $b$ is light in ${S}^{B}$ ; updating ${L}_{B}\left( B\right)$ and ${H}_{B}\left( B\right)$ takes constant time.

当$B$值在${R}^{B}$中已存在或在$R$中不存在时，更新${\delta R}$会影响$R$的轻量部分${R}^{B}$。针对此类变更$\delta {R}^{B}$，由于$b$在${S}^{B}$中属于轻量级，我们及时用$\delta {V}_{B}\left( {a,C}\right)  = \delta {R}^{B}\left( {a,b}\right) ,{S}^{B}\left( {b,C}\right)$更新${V}_{B}\left( {A,C}\right)$；而更新${L}_{B}\left( B\right)$和${H}_{B}\left( B\right)$仅需常量时间。

The update ${\delta R}$ may trigger a new single-tuple change in $\exists {H}_{B}$ ,affecting ${V}_{B}\left( B\right)$ . The change $\delta \left( {\exists {H}_{B}}\right)$ is non-empty only when the multiplicity ${H}_{B}\left( b\right)$ changes from 0 to non-zero or vice versa. For such change $\delta \left( {\exists {H}_{B}}\right)$ ,we update ${V}_{B}\left( B\right)$ via constant-time lookups in ${R}^{\prime }\left( b\right)$ and ${S}^{\prime }\left( b\right)$ .

更新${\delta R}$可能触发$\exists {H}_{B}$中的新单元素变更，进而影响${V}_{B}\left( B\right)$。仅当多重性${H}_{B}\left( b\right)$从0变为非零或相反时，变更$\delta \left( {\exists {H}_{B}}\right)$才非空。对此类变更$\delta \left( {\exists {H}_{B}}\right)$，我们通过在${R}^{\prime }\left( b\right)$和${S}^{\prime }\left( b\right)$中进行常量时间查找来更新${V}_{B}\left( B\right)$。

The update ${\delta R}$ may change the degree of $b$ in $R$ from light to heavy or vice versa. In such cases,we need to rebalance the partitioning of $R$ and possibly recompute some of the views. Although such rebalancing steps may take time more than $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$ ,they happen periodically and their amortized cost remains the same as for a single-tuple update (Section 7).

更新${\delta R}$可能导致$R$中$b$的度从轻量级转为重量级或反之。此时需要重新平衡$R$的分区划分，并可能重新计算部分视图。虽然此类再平衡操作耗时可能超过$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$，但其周期性发生且摊还成本与单元素更新相同（第7节）。

Next,we demonstrate our approach for the ${\delta }_{1}$ -hierarchical query from Example 3.2.

接下来，我们将演示针对示例3.2中${\delta }_{1}$层次化查询的解决方案。

Example 9.2. Consider the ${\delta }_{1}$ -hierarchical free-connex query $Q\left( A\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( B\right)$ from Example 3.2 whose relations have size at most $N$ . Figure 24 shows the single view tree (bottom-left) that our approach constructs in the static case, and the other five view trees needed in the dynamic case. In the static case,since $Q$ is free-connex,its result can be computed in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time and then its tuples can be enumerated with $\mathcal{O}\left( 1\right)$ delay. Our approach does not partition the relations in the static case. We compute the view ${V}_{B}\left( A\right)$ in time $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ by iterating over the tuples in $R$ and looking up for each tuple(a,b)in $R$ the multiplicity of $b$ in $S$ in $\mathcal{O}\left( 1\right)$ time. The result can be enumerated from the view ${V}_{B}\left( A\right)$ with $\mathcal{O}\left( 1\right)$ delay.

示例9.2. 考虑示例3.2中关系规模最大为$N$的${\delta }_{1}$层次化自由连接查询$Q\left( A\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( B\right)$。图24展示了静态场景下构建的单一视图树（左下），以及动态场景所需的另外五棵视图树。静态场景中，由于$Q$具有自由连接性，其结果可在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内计算完成，并以$\mathcal{O}\left( 1\right)$延迟枚举元组。本方案在静态场景下不对关系进行分区，通过遍历$R$中的元组并在$\mathcal{O}\left( 1\right)$时间内查找$S$中$b$的多重性，耗时$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$构建视图${V}_{B}\left( A\right)$，最终可从该视图以$\mathcal{O}\left( 1\right)$延迟枚举结果。

FIGURE 24. The view trees for $Q\left( A\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( B\right)$ in Example 9.2. The bottom-left view tree is the only one needed in the static case, all others are needed in the dynamic case.

图24. 示例9.2中$Q\left( A\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( B\right)$的视图树集合。左下角视图树为静态场景唯一所需，其余均为动态场景必备。

<!-- Media -->

In the dynamic case,we partition relations $R$ and $S$ on the bound join variable $B$ and create the indicators ${L}_{B}\left( B\right)$ and ${H}_{B}\left( B\right)$ as in Figure 24. In the light case,we compute the view ${V}_{B}\left( A\right)$ in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time: For each(a,b)in the light part ${R}^{B}$ of $R$ ,we check the multiplicity of $b$ in the light part ${S}^{B}$ of $S$ using a constant-time lookup. In the heavy case,we compute the view ${V}_{B}\left( B\right)$ in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time using the heavy indicator $\exists {H}_{B}$ ,the input relation $S$ ,and the projection ${R}^{\prime }\left( B\right)$ of $R$ on $B$ .

在动态场景下，我们对绑定连接变量$B$上的关系$R$和$S$进行分区，并如图24所示创建指示器${L}_{B}\left( B\right)$和${H}_{B}\left( B\right)$。在轻量场景中，我们以$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间复杂度计算视图${V}_{B}\left( A\right)$：对于$R$的轻量部分${R}^{B}$中的每个(a,b)对，通过常数时间查找验证$S$的轻量部分${S}^{B}$中$b$的多重性。在重量场景中，利用重量指示器$\exists {H}_{B}$、输入关系$S$以及$R$在$B$上的投影${R}^{\prime }\left( B\right)$，以$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间复杂度计算视图${V}_{B}\left( B\right)$。

We can enumerate the tuples in the query result with $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ delay: Since there are at most ${N}^{1 - \epsilon }$ heavy $B$ -values in ${V}_{B}\left( B\right)$ ,each with its own list of $A$ -values in $R$ ,we need $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ delay to enumerate the distinct $A$ -values paired with the heavy $B$ -values. In addition,we can enumerate from the view ${V}_{B}\left( A\right)$ created for the light $B$ -values with constant delay. To obtain the multiplicity of each output tuple, we sum up the positive multiplicities of the duplicates of the tuple across the $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ buckets.

我们可以用$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$延迟枚举查询结果中的元组：由于${V}_{B}\left( B\right)$中最多存在${N}^{1 - \epsilon }$个重量级$B$值，每个值在$R$中有对应的$A$值列表，因此需要$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$延迟来枚举与重量级$B$值配对的唯一$A$值。此外，我们还能以恒定延迟从为轻量级$B$值创建的视图${V}_{B}\left( A\right)$中进行枚举。为获取每个输出元组的多重性，我们汇总该元组在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$个桶中所有副本的正数多重性。

A single-tuple update to $R$ triggers constant-time updates to all views. A single-tuple update to $S$ triggers constant-time updates to the indicators and ${V}_{B}\left( B\right)$ . In the light case, the update to ${V}_{B}\left( A\right)$ is given by $\delta {V}_{B}\left( A\right)  = {R}^{B}\left( {A,b}\right) ,\delta {S}^{B}\left( b\right)$ ,which requires $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$ time since $b$ is light in ${R}^{B}$ . We may need to rebalance the partitions,which gives an amortized update time of $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$ .

对$R$的单元组更新会触发所有视图的常数时间更新。对$S$的单元组更新会触发指标和${V}_{B}\left( B\right)$的常数时间更新。在轻量情况下，${V}_{B}\left( A\right)$的更新由$\delta {V}_{B}\left( A\right)  = {R}^{B}\left( {A,b}\right) ,\delta {S}^{B}\left( b\right)$给出，这需要$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$时间，因为$b$在${R}^{B}$中是轻量的。我们可能需要重新平衡分区，这将产生$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$的摊还更新时间。

Since both queries in Examples 9.1 and 9.2 are ${\delta }_{1}$ -hierarchical and do not have repeating relation symbols, there is no algorithm that can maintain them under single-tuple updates with $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{1}{2} - \gamma }\right)$ amortized update time and $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{1}{2} - \gamma }\right)$ delay for $\gamma  > 0$ unless the Online Matrix-Vector Multiplication conjecture fails (Proposition 8.1). Our approach meets this lower bound for $\epsilon  = \frac{1}{2}$ .

由于示例9.1和9.2中的查询都是${\delta }_{1}$层次化的且不包含重复关系符号，除非在线矩阵向量乘法猜想不成立（命题8.1），否则不存在能在单次元组更新下以$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{1}{2} - \gamma }\right)$摊还更新时间和$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{1}{2} - \gamma }\right)$延迟维护$\gamma  > 0$的算法。我们的方法达到了$\epsilon  = \frac{1}{2}$的这个下界。

## 10. Conclusion and Future Work

## 10. 结论与未来工作

This paper investigates the preprocessing-update-delay trade-off for hierarchical queries and introduces an approach that recovers a number of prior results when restricted to hierarchical queries. There are several lines of future work. Of paramount importance is the generalization of our trade-off from hierarchical to conjunctive queries. The results of this paper can be immediately extended to hierarchical queries with group-by aggregates and order-by clauses. In particular, this extension would capture the prior result on constant-delay enumeration for such queries in the context of factorized databases [OS16]. An open problem is to find lower bounds for ${\delta }_{i}$ -hierarchical queries for $i > 1$ . We conjecture our update/delay upper bounds $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{i\epsilon }\right) /\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ are worst-case optimal,as it is the case for $i = 0$ with $\epsilon  = 1$ [BKS17a] and $i = 1$ with $\epsilon  = \frac{1}{2}$ (Proposition 8.1).

本文研究了层次化查询的预处理-更新-延迟权衡关系，并提出了一种在限定于层次化查询时可复现多项先前结果的方法。未来工作有多个方向，最重要的是将我们的权衡关系从层次化查询推广到合取查询。本文结果可直接扩展到包含分组聚合和排序子句的层次化查询，特别是这将涵盖因子化数据库[OS16]中关于此类查询常数延迟枚举的先前结果。一个开放性问题是为$i > 1$的${\delta }_{i}$层次化查询寻找下界。我们推测更新/延迟上界$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{i\epsilon }\right) /\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$是最坏情况最优的，正如$\epsilon  = 1$[BKS17a]中的$i = 0$和$\epsilon  = \frac{1}{2}$（命题8.1）中的$i = 1$那样。

## Appendix A. Proofs of the Results in Section 2

## 附录A. 第2节结果的证明

THEOREM 2.1. Given a hierarchical query with static width $\mathrm{w}$ ,a database of size $N$ , and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,the query result can be enumerated with $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ delay after $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ preprocessing time.

定理2.1. 给定一个静态宽度为$\mathrm{w}$的层次查询、大小为$N$的数据库及$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，查询结果可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$预处理时间后以$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$延迟进行枚举。

The theorem follows from Propositions 5.3,5.4,and 6.1. Let $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ be a hierarchical query and $\omega$ an arbitrary canonical variable order for $Q$ . Without loss of generality,assume that $\omega$ consists of a single tree. The preprocessing stage materializes the views in the view trees $\left\{  {{T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{k}}\right\}$ returned by $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ from Figure 11 in the static mode. By Proposition 5.4, these views can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ . By Proposition 5.3, $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ is equivalent to $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{Q}_{i}\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ ,where ${Q}_{i}\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ is the query defined by the join of the leaves in ${T}_{i}$ . By Proposition 6.1,the result of $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ can be enumerated from these materialized views with delay $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ .
