该定理由命题5.3、5.4和6.1推导得出。设$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$为分层查询，$\omega$为$Q$的任意规范变量序。不失一般性，假设$\omega$由单一树结构组成。预处理阶段将图11中$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$在静态模式下返回的视图树$\left\{  {{T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{k}}\right\}$物化。根据命题5.4，这些视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$时间内完成物化。命题5.3表明$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$等价于$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{Q}_{i}\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$，其中${Q}_{i}\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$是由${T}_{i}$中叶节点连接定义的查询。根据命题6.1，$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$的结果可从这些物化视图中以$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$延迟枚举得出。

If the canonical variable order for $Q$ consists of several trees ${\omega }_{1},\ldots ,{\omega }_{m}$ ,we construct a set ${\mathcal{T}}_{i}$ of view trees for each ${\omega }_{i}$ ,where $i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$ . The result of the query is the Cartesian product of the tuple sets obtained from each ${\mathcal{T}}_{i}$ . Given that each set ${\mathcal{T}}_{i}$ of view trees admits $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ enumeration delay,the tuples in the Cartesian product can be enumerated with the same delay using the ProDUCT algorithm (Figure 16),since $m$ is independent of the database size $N$ .

若$Q$的规范变量序包含多棵树${\omega }_{1},\ldots ,{\omega }_{m}$，则为每个${\omega }_{i}$构建视图树集合${\mathcal{T}}_{i}$，其中$i \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$。查询结果是各${\mathcal{T}}_{i}$所获元组集的笛卡尔积。鉴于每个视图树集合${\mathcal{T}}_{i}$允许$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$枚举延迟，使用PRODUCT算法（图16）可在相同延迟下枚举笛卡尔积中的元组，因为$m$与数据库规模$N$无关。

## Appendix B. Proofs of the Results in Section 3

## 附录B. 第3节结果的证明

THEOREM 3.1. Given a hierarchical query with static width $\mathbf{w}$ and dynamic width $\delta$ ,a database of size $N$ ,and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,the query result can be enumerated with $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ delay after $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {w - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ preprocessing time and $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ amortized update time for single-tuple updates.

定理3.1. 给定静态宽度$\mathbf{w}$和动态宽度$\delta$的分层查询、规模为$N$的数据库及$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {w - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$预处理时间和$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$单元组更新的均摊更新时间后，该查询结果能以$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$延迟进行枚举。

The theorem follows from Propositions 5.3,5.4,6.1,7.2,and 7.5. Let $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ be a hierarchical query and $\omega$ an arbitrary canonical variable order for $Q$ . Without loss of generality,assume that $\omega$ consists of a single tree. The preprocessing stage materializes the views in the set of view trees returned by $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ from Figure 11 in the dynamic mode. The preprocessing time $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ follows from Proposition 5.4,which captures both the static and dynamic modes (see the proof). The equivalence between the constructed view trees and the query follows from Proposition 5.3. The delay $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ needed when enumerating the query result from these materialized views follows from Proposition 6.1. The time $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ to maintain these materialized views under a single-tuple update follows from Proposition 7.2. By Proposition 7.5, the amortized maintenance time under a sequence of single-tuple updates is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ .

该定理由命题5.3、5.4、6.1、7.2和7.5推导得出。设$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$为层次化查询，$\omega$为$Q$的任意规范变量序。不失一般性，假设$\omega$由单一树结构组成。预处理阶段将图11中$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$返回的视图树集合在动态模式下物化。预处理时间$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$由命题5.4确定（涵盖静态与动态模式，参见证明）。构造视图树与查询的等价性由命题5.3保证。从物化视图中枚举查询结果所需的延迟$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$遵循命题6.1。单元组更新下维护这些物化视图的时间$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$由命题7.2给出。根据命题7.5，连续单元组更新的摊还维护时间为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$。

If the canonical variable order consists of several view trees, the reasoning is analogous to the proof of Theorem 2.1.

若规范变量序包含多个视图树，其论证过程与定理2.1的证明类似。

Appendix C. Proofs of the Results in Section 5

附录C. 第5节结果的证明

### C.1. Proof of Proposition 5.3.

### C.1. 命题5.3的证明

Proposition 5.3. Let $\left\{  {{T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{k}}\right\}   = \tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ be the set of view trees constructed by the algorithm in Figure 11 for a given hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ and a canonical variable order $\omega$ for $Q$ . Let ${Q}^{\left( i\right) }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ be the query defined by the conjunction of the leaf atoms in ${T}_{i},\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . Then, $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  \equiv  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{Q}^{\left( i\right) }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ .

命题5.3. 设$\left\{  {{T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{k}}\right\}   = \tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$为图11算法针对给定层次化查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$和规范变量序$\omega$构造的视图树集合，${Q}^{\left( i\right) }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$为由${T}_{i},\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$中叶原子合取定义的查询，则$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  \equiv  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{Q}^{\left( i\right) }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$成立。

We use two observations. (1) The procedure BUILDVT constructs a view tree whose leaf atoms are exactly the same as the leaf atoms of the input variable order. (2) Each of the procedures NEWVT and AUXVIEW constructs a view tree whose set of leaf atoms is the union of the sets of leaf atoms of the input trees. For a variable order or view tree $T$ and schema a set $\mathcal{S}$ of variables occurring in $T$ ,we define ${Q}_{T}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = { \boxtimes  }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( T\right) }R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$ .

基于两点观察：(1) BUILDVT过程构建的视图树，其叶原子与输入变量序的叶原子完全一致；(2) NEWVT和AUXVIEW过程构建的视图树，其叶原子集合均为输入树对应集合的并集。对于变量序或视图树$T$及其模式变量集$\mathcal{S}$，我们定义${Q}_{T}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = { \boxtimes  }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( T\right) }R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$。

The proof is by induction over the structure of $\omega$ . We show that for any subtree ${\omega }^{\prime }$ of $\omega$ , it holds:

通过对$\omega$结构的归纳法证明。我们证明对于$\omega$的任意子树${\omega }^{\prime }$，均满足：

$$
{Q}_{{\omega }^{\prime }}\left( {\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right) }\right)  \equiv  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{T \in  \tau \left( {{\omega }^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right) }}{Q}_{T}\left( {\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right) }\right) . \tag{C.1}
$$

Base case: If ${\omega }^{\prime }$ is an atom,the procedure $\tau$ returns it and the base case holds trivially. Inductive step: Assume that ${\omega }^{\prime }$ has root variable $X$ and subtrees ${\omega }_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{\omega }_{k}^{\prime }$ . Let $\operatorname{keys} =$ $\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\} ,{\mathcal{F}}_{X} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \left( {\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right) }\right)$ ,and ${Q}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)  = { \bowtie  }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right) \text{ atoms }\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right) }R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$ . The procedure $\tau$ distinguishes the following cases:

基本情况：若${\omega }^{\prime }$为原子元素，过程$\tau$直接返回该元素，显然满足基本情况。归纳步骤：假设${\omega }^{\prime }$具有根变量$X$及子树${\omega }_{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{\omega }_{k}^{\prime }$。令$\operatorname{keys} =$$\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\} ,{\mathcal{F}}_{X} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \left( {\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right) }\right)$，且${Q}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)  = { \bowtie  }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right) \text{ atoms }\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right) }R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$。过程$\tau$将区分以下情形：

Case 1: (mode $=$ ’static’ $\land  {Q}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$ is free-connex) $\vee$ (mode $=$ ’dynamic’ $\land  {Q}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$ is ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical). The procedure $\tau$ returns a view tree $T$ constructed by the procedure BUILDVT $\left( {\cdot ,{\omega }^{\prime },{\mathcal{F}}_{X}}\right)$ . The leaves of $T$ are the atoms of ${\omega }^{\prime }$ . This implies Equivalence C.1.

情形1：（模式$=$为静态时$\land  {Q}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$是自由连接的）$\vee$（模式$=$为动态时$\land  {Q}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$是${\delta }_{0}$层次化的）。过程$\tau$返回由BUILDVT$\left( {\cdot ,{\omega }^{\prime },{\mathcal{F}}_{X}}\right)$构建的视图树$T$，其叶节点为${\omega }^{\prime }$的原子元素。由此可得等价性C.1。

Case 1 does not hold and $X \in  \mathcal{F}$ : The set of view trees $\tau \left( {{\omega }^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right)$ is defined as follows: for each set ${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ with ${T}_{i} \in  \tau \left( {{\omega }_{i}^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right)$ ,the set $\tau \left( {{\omega }^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right)$ contains the view tree $\operatorname{NEWVT}\left( {\cdot ,\text{ keys },{\left\{  {\widehat{T}}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\right)$ where ${\widehat{T}}_{i} = \operatorname{AuxVIEW}\left( \right.$ root of $\left. {{\omega }_{i}^{\prime },{T}_{i}}\right)$ for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ .

若情形1不成立且$X \in  \mathcal{F}$：视图树集合$\tau \left( {{\omega }^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right)$定义如下：对于每个满足${T}_{i} \in  \tau \left( {{\omega }_{i}^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right)$的集合${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$，集合$\tau \left( {{\omega }^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right)$包含以$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$的$\left. {{\omega }_{i}^{\prime },{T}_{i}}\right)$为根节点的视图树$\operatorname{NEWVT}\left( {\cdot ,\text{ keys },{\left\{  {\widehat{T}}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\right)$，其中${\widehat{T}}_{i} = \operatorname{AuxVIEW}\left( \right.$。

Using the induction hypothesis, we rewrite as follows:

根据归纳假设，我们进行如下重写：

$$
{Q}_{{\omega }^{\prime }}\left( {\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right) }\right)  = { \bowtie  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }{Q}_{{\omega }_{i}^{\prime }}\left( {\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{i}^{\prime }\right) }\right) \overset{\text{ IH }}{ \equiv  }{ \bowtie  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }\left( {\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{T \in  \tau \left( {{\omega }_{i}^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right) }}{Q}_{T}\left( {\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{i}^{\prime }\right) }\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
 \equiv  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   : {T}_{i} \in  \tau \left( {{\omega }_{i}^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right) }}{Q}_{{T}_{i}}\left( {\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{i}^{\prime }\right) }\right)  \equiv  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   : {T}_{i} \in  \tau \left( {{\omega }_{i}^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right) }}{Q}_{\operatorname{NewVT}\left( {\cdot ,\operatorname{keys},{\left\{  {\widehat{T}}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\right) }\left( {\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right) }\right) 
$$

$= \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{T \in  \tau \left( {{\omega }^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right) }}{Q}_{T}\left( {\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right) }\right) .$

Case 1 does not hold and $X \notin  \mathcal{F}$ : The procedure $\tau$ creates the views ${Al}{l}_{X}\left( \text{keys}\right)  =$ ${ \boxtimes  }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right) }R\left( \mathcal{X}\right) ,{L}_{X}\left( \text{ keys }\right)  = { \boxtimes  }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right) }{R}^{\text{keys }}\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$ ,and ${H}_{X}\left( \text{ keys }\right)  = {\operatorname{All}}_{X}\left( \text{ keys }\right)$ - $\nexists {L}_{X}\left( \text{keys}\right)$ . It then returns the view trees $\{$ ltree $\}  \cup$ htrees defined as follows:

情形1不成立且$X \notin  \mathcal{F}$：该过程$\tau$创建视图${Al}{l}_{X}\left( \text{keys}\right)  =$${ \boxtimes  }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right) }R\left( \mathcal{X}\right) ,{L}_{X}\left( \text{ keys }\right)  = { \boxtimes  }_{R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right) }{R}^{\text{keys }}\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$及${H}_{X}\left( \text{ keys }\right)  = {\operatorname{All}}_{X}\left( \text{ keys }\right)$-$\nexists {L}_{X}\left( \text{keys}\right)$，随后返回按以下方式定义的视图树$\{$ltree$\}  \cup$htrees：

- ltree $= \operatorname{BUILDVT}\left( {\cdot ,{\omega }^{\text{keys }},\mathcal{F}}\right)$ ,where ${\omega }^{\text{keys }}$ has the same structure as ${\omega }^{\prime }$ but each atom is replaced by its light part;

- ltree$= \operatorname{BUILDVT}\left( {\cdot ,{\omega }^{\text{keys }},\mathcal{F}}\right)$，其中${\omega }^{\text{keys }}$与${\omega }^{\prime }$结构相同，但每个原子被替换为其轻量部分；

- for each set ${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ with ${T}_{i} \in  \tau \left( {{\omega }_{i}^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right)$ ,htrees contains the view tree NEWVT $( \cdot  ,$ keys, $\left. {{\left\{  \exists {H}_{X}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }{\left. {\left\{  {\widehat{T}}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }\right\}  }_{\text{an ere }}{\widehat{T}}_{i} = \operatorname{AuxVIEW}\left( {\text{ root of }{\omega }_{i}^{\prime },{T}_{i}}\right) \text{ for }i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  .}\right)$

- 对于每个满足${T}_{i} \in  \tau \left( {{\omega }_{i}^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right)$的集合${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$，htrees包含视图树NEWVT$( \cdot  ,$键值，$\left. {{\left\{  \exists {H}_{X}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }{\left. {\left\{  {\widehat{T}}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }\right\}  }_{\text{an ere }}{\widehat{T}}_{i} = \operatorname{AuxVIEW}\left( {\text{ root of }{\omega }_{i}^{\prime },{T}_{i}}\right) \text{ for }i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  .}\right)$

From ${AL}{L}_{X}\left( \text{keys}\right)  = {L}_{X}\left( \text{keys}\right)  \cup  {H}_{X}\left( \text{keys}\right)$ ,we derive the following equivalence. For simplicity, we skip the schemas of queries:

由${AL}{L}_{X}\left( \text{keys}\right)  = {L}_{X}\left( \text{keys}\right)  \cup  {H}_{X}\left( \text{keys}\right)$可推导出以下等价关系。为简化表述，我们省略查询的模式：

$$
\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{k\rbrack  : {T}_{i} \in  \tau \left( {{\omega }_{i}^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right) }}{Q}_{{T}_{i}} \equiv  {Q}_{\text{ltree }} \cup  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   : {T}_{i} \in  \tau \left( {{\omega }_{i}^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right) }}{Q}_{\text{NEWVT }\left( {\cdot ,\text{ keys },\left\{  {\exists {H}_{X}}\right\}   \cup  {\left\{  {\widehat{T}}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\right) } \tag{C.2}
$$

Using Equivalence (C.2) and the induction hypothesis, we obtain:

利用等价关系(C.2)及归纳假设，可得：

$$
{Q}_{{\omega }^{\prime }} = { \bowtie  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }{Q}_{{\omega }_{i}^{\prime }}\overset{\mathrm{{IH}}}{ \equiv  }{ \bowtie  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }\left( {\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{T \in  \tau \left( {{\omega }_{i}^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right) }}{Q}_{T}}\right)  \equiv  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   : {T}_{i} \in  \tau \left( {{\omega }_{i}^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right) }}{ \bowtie  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }{Q}_{{T}_{i}}
$$

$$
\overset{\left( \mathrm{C}{.2}\right) }{ \equiv  }{Q}_{\text{ltree }} \cup  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   : {T}_{i} \in  \tau \left( {{\omega }_{i}^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right) }}{Q}_{\text{NEWVT }\left( {\cdot ,\text{ keys },\left\{  {\exists {H}_{X}}\right\}   \cup  {\left\{  {\widehat{T}}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\right) }
$$

$$
 = {Q}_{\text{ltree }} \cup  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{T \in  \text{htrees }}}{Q}_{T} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{T \in  \tau \left( {{\omega }^{\prime },\mathcal{F}}\right) }}{Q}_{T}
$$

### C.2. Proof of Proposition 5.4.

### C.2. 命题5.4的证明

Proposition 5.4. Given a hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ with static width $\mathrm{w}$ ,a canonical variable order $\omega$ for $Q$ ,a database of size $N$ ,and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,the views in the set of view trees $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ time.

命题5.4. 给定一个静态宽度为$\mathrm{w}$的层次化查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$、$Q$的规范变量序$\omega$、规模为$N$的数据库及$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，视图树集合$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$中的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$时间内物化。

We analyze the procedure $\tau$ from Figure 11 for both of the cases mode $=$ ’static’ and mode $=$ ’dynamic’. We show that in both cases the time to materialize the set of view trees $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {w - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ .

我们分别分析图11中模式$=$'静态'与模式$=$'动态'下的过程$\tau$，证明两种情形下物化视图树集合$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$的时间均为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {w - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$。

We explain the intuition behind the complexity analysis. If the procedure $\tau$ runs in ’static’ mode and $Q$ is free-connex,or it runs in ’dynamic’ mode and $Q$ is ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical, the procedure constructs a view tree that can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time. Otherwise, there must be at least one bound variable $X$ in $\omega$ such that the subtree ${\omega }_{X}$ rooted at $X$ contains free variables. In this case, the algorithm partitions the relations at the leaves of ${\omega }_{X}$ into heavy and light parts and creates view trees for computing parts of the query. The time to materialize the views of the view trees where at least one leaf relation is heavy is $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ . The overall time to materialize the view trees $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ is dominated by the time to materialize the views of the view trees where all leaf relations are light. In the worst case, the root variable of $\omega$ is bound and we need to materialize a view that joins the light parts of all leaf relations in $\omega$ and has the entire set $\mathcal{F}$ as free variables. We can compute such a view $V\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ as follows. We first aggregate away all bound variables that are not ancestors of free variables in $\omega$ . By using the algorithm InsideOut [AKNR16],this can be done in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time. Then,we choose one atom to iterate over the tuples of its relation (outer loop of the evaluation). For each such tuple, we iterate over the matching tuples in the relations of the other atoms (inner loops of the evaluation). To decide which atom to take for the outer loop and which ones for the inner loops of our evaluation strategy, we use an optimal integral edge cover $\mathbf{\lambda }$ of $\mathcal{F}$ . The schema of each atom that is mapped to 0 by $\mathbf{\lambda }$ must be subsumed by the schema of an atom mapped to 1 . Hence, we can take one of the atoms mapped to 1 to do the outer loop. The other atoms that are mapped to 1 are used for the inner loops. For the atoms that are mapped to 0 , it suffices to do constant-time lookups during the iteration over the tuples of the other atoms. By exploiting the degree constraints on light relation parts,the view $V\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\rho \left( \mathcal{F}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ time. By Proposition 4.5, $\rho \left( \mathcal{F}\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ . Considering the time needed to aggregate away bound variables before computing $V\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ ,we get $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\max \left\{  {1,1 + \left( {{\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right\}  }\right.$ overall time complexity. We show that $\max \left\{  {1,1 + \left( {{\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right\}$ is upper-bounded by $1 + \left( {\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon$ .

我们阐述复杂度分析背后的直观原理。若过程$\tau$以'静态'模式运行且$Q$是自由连接（free-connex）的，或以'动态'模式运行且$Q$具有${\delta }_{0}$层次结构（-hierarchical），则该过程构建的视图树可在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内物化。否则，$\omega$中至少存在一个绑定变量$X$，使得以$X$为根的子树${\omega }_{X}$包含自由变量。此时，算法将${\omega }_{X}$叶节点上的关系划分为重部分和轻部分，并创建用于计算部分查询的视图树。物化至少含一个重叶关系的视图树所需时间为$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$。整体物化时间$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$主要由全轻叶关系的视图树物化时间决定。最坏情况下，$\omega$的根变量被绑定，需物化一个连接$\omega$中所有轻叶关系、且以整个集合$\mathcal{F}$作为自由变量的视图。该视图$V\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$的计算步骤如下：首先聚合$\omega$中非自由变量祖先的所有绑定变量，借助InsideOut算法[AKNR16]，此步骤耗时$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$；随后选取一个原子，遍历其关系元组（评估的外循环），对每个元组遍历其他原子关系的匹配元组（评估的内循环）。为确定外循环与内循环的原子分配策略，我们采用$\mathcal{F}$的最优整数边覆盖$\mathbf{\lambda }$。被$\mathbf{\lambda }$映射为0的原子的模式必须被映射为1的原子的模式包含，故选取映射为1的原子执行外循环，其余映射为1的原子用于内循环。对于映射为0的原子，在遍历其他原子元组时仅需常量时间查找。利用轻关系部分的度数约束，视图$V\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\rho \left( \mathcal{F}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$时间内物化。根据命题4.5，$\rho \left( \mathcal{F}\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$。综合计算$V\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$前聚合绑定变量的时间，总时间复杂度为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\max \left\{  {1,1 + \left( {{\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right\}  }\right.$。我们证明$\max \left\{  {1,1 + \left( {{\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right\}$的上界是$1 + \left( {\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon$。

The proof is structured following the basic building blocks of the procedure $\tau$ . Lemmas C.2-C.6 give upper bounds on the times to materialize the views in the view trees returned by the procedures NEWVT (Figure 7), AuxVIEW (Figure 8) BUILDVT (Figure 6), and INDICATORVTS (Figure 10). Lemma C. 7 states the complexity of the procedure $\tau$ based on a measure $\xi$ defined over canonical variable orders. The section closes with the proof of Proposition 5.4 that bridges the measure $\xi$ and the static width of hierarchical queries.

该证明遵循过程$\tau$的基本构建模块进行组织。引理C.2至C.6给出了由NEWVT（图7）、AuxVIEW（图8）、BUILDVT（图6）和INDICATORVTS（图10）过程返回的视图树中物化视图所需时间的上界。引理C.7基于在规范变量序上定义的度量$\xi$，阐述了过程$\tau$的复杂度。本节最后通过命题5.4的证明，将度量$\xi$与层次化查询的静态宽度联系起来。

We introduce the measure $\xi$ . Let $\omega$ be a canonical variable order, $\mathcal{F} \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( \omega \right)$ ,and $X$ a variable or atom in $\omega$ . We denote by ${\omega }_{X}$ the subtree of $\omega$ rooted at $X$ and by ${Q}_{X}$ a query that joins the atoms at the leaves of ${\omega }_{X}$ . We define

我们引入度量$\xi$。设$\omega$为规范变量序，$\mathcal{F} \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( \omega \right)$，且$X$为$\omega$中的变量或原子。用${\omega }_{X}$表示以$X$为根的$\omega$子树，${Q}_{X}$表示连接${\omega }_{X}$叶节点原子的查询。我们定义

$$
\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{\substack{{Y \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right) } \\  {\left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( Y\right) \cup \{ Y\} }\right)  \nsubseteq  \mathcal{F}} }}\left\{  {{\rho }_{{Q}_{X}}^{ * }\left( {\operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{Y}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{F}}\right) }\right\}  .
$$

If ${\omega }_{X}$ does not contain a variable $Y$ with $\left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( Y\right) \cup \{ Y\} }\right)  \nsubseteq  \mathcal{F}$ ,then $\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  = 0$ . If $X$ has children ${X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{k}$ ,then

若${\omega }_{X}$不包含满足$\left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( Y\right) \cup \{ Y\} }\right)  \nsubseteq  \mathcal{F}$的变量$Y$，则$\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  = 0$。若$X$含有子节点${X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{k}$，则

$$
\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  \geq  \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\left\{  {\xi \left( {\omega ,{X}_{i},\mathcal{F}}\right) }\right\}  . \tag{C.3}
$$

We start with an observation that each view $V$ constructed by the procedures BUILDVT (Figure 6), NEWVT (Figure 7), AuxVIEW (Figure 8), INDICATORVTS (Figure 10), and $\tau$ (Figure 11) at some node $X$ of a variable order $\omega$ contains in its schema all variables in the root path of $X$ and no variables which are not in ${\omega }_{X}$ . Moreover, $V$ results from the join of its child views. This can be shown by a straightforward induction over the structure of $\omega$ .

我们首先观察到：由BUILDVT（图6）、NEWVT（图7）、AuxVIEW（图8）、INDICATORVTS（图10）和$\tau$（图11）过程在变量序$\omega$的某个节点$X$处构建的每个视图$V$，其模式包含$X$根路径上的所有变量，且不包含${\omega }_{X}$之外的变量。此外，$V$是其子视图连接的结果。这可通过$\omega$结构的直接归纳得到证明。

Observation C.1. Let $\omega$ be a canonical variable order and $V\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ a view constructed at some node $X$ of $\omega$ by one of the procedures BUILDVT,NEWVT,AUXVIEW,INDICATORVTS, and $\tau$ . It holds

观察结论C.1。设$\omega$为规范变量序，$V\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$为BUILDVT、NEWVT、AUXVIEW、INDICATORVTS或$\tau$过程在$\omega$某节点$X$处构建的视图。成立条件如下

(1) $\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \subseteq  \mathcal{F} \subseteq  \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \operatorname{vars}\left( \omega \right)$ .

(2) If ${V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}\right)$ are the child views of $V\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ ,then $V\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  = {V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}\right)$ .

(2) 若${V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}\right)$是$V\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$的子视图，则$V\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  = {V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}\right)$。

The next lemma gives a bound on the time to materialize the views in a view tree returned by the procedure NEWVT in Figure 7.

下个引理给出了图7中NEWVT过程返回的视图树内物化视图所需时间的界。

Lemma C.2. Given a set ${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ of view trees with root views ${\left\{  {V}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{i}\right) \right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ ,let ${M}_{i}$ be the time to materialize the views in ${T}_{i}$ ,for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . If the query $V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = {V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{k}\right)$ with $\mathcal{S} \subseteq  \mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{\mathcal{S}}_{i}$ is ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical,the views in the view tree $\operatorname{NEWVT}\left( {\cdot ,\mathcal{S},{\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\right)$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\left\{  {M}_{i}\right\}  }\right)$ time.

引理C.2。给定一组根视图为${\left\{  {V}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{i}\right) \right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$的视图树集合${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$，设${M}_{i}$为物化${T}_{i}$中视图所需时间，其中$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$。若查询$V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = {V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{k}\right)$（带条件$\mathcal{S} \subseteq  \mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{\mathcal{S}}_{i}$）是${\delta }_{0}$层次结构的，则视图树$\operatorname{NEWVT}\left( {\cdot ,\mathcal{S},{\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\right)$中的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\left\{  {M}_{i}\right\}  }\right)$时间内完成物化。

Proof. The procedure NEWVT defines the view $V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = {V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{k}\right)$ (Line 2). The view tree $T$ returned by NEWVT is defined as follows (Line 4): If $k = 1$ and $\mathcal{S} = {\mathcal{S}}_{1}$ ,then $T = {T}_{1}$ ; otherwise, $T$ is the view tree that has root $V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ and subtrees ${T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{k}$ . By assumption,the time to materialize the views in the trees ${T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{k}$ is $\mathcal{O}\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\left\{  {M}_{i}\right\}  }\right)$ . Hence,the sizes of the materialized root views ${V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{k}\right)$ must be $\mathcal{O}\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\left\{  {M}_{i}\right\}  }\right)$ . Assume that the query defining $V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ is ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical. Hence,we can construct a free-top canonical variable order for the query. We materialize $V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ as follows. Traversing the variable order bottom-up, we aggregate away all bound variables using the InsideOut algorithm [AKNR16]. Since the query defining $V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ is $\alpha$ -acyclic,this aggregation phase can be done in time linear in the size of the views ${V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{k}\right)$ . Thus,the aggregation phase requires $\mathcal{O}\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\left\{  {M}_{i}\right\}  }\right)$ time. It follows that the time to materialize the views in the tree returned by NEWVT is $\mathcal{O}\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\left\{  {M}_{i}\right\}  }\right)$ .

证明。过程NEWVT定义了视图$V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = {V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{k}\right)$（第2行）。NEWVT返回的视图树$T$定义如下（第4行）：若$k = 1$且$\mathcal{S} = {\mathcal{S}}_{1}$，则$T = {T}_{1}$；否则，$T$是以$V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$为根节点、${T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{k}$为子树的视图树。根据假设，实例化子树${T}_{1},\ldots ,{T}_{k}$中视图的时间为$\mathcal{O}\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\left\{  {M}_{i}\right\}  }\right)$。因此，实例化根视图${V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{k}\right)$的大小必须为$\mathcal{O}\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\left\{  {M}_{i}\right\}  }\right)$。假设定义$V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$的查询是${\delta }_{0}$分层的，故可为该查询构造自由拓扑规范变量序。我们按以下方式实例化$V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$：自底向上遍历变量序时，使用InsideOut算法[AKNR16]聚合所有约束变量。由于定义$V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$的查询是$\alpha$非循环的，此聚合阶段耗时与视图${V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{k}\right)$的大小成线性关系，因此需要$\mathcal{O}\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\left\{  {M}_{i}\right\}  }\right)$时间。综上，实例化NEWVT返回的视图树中所有视图的总时间为$\mathcal{O}\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\left\{  {M}_{i}\right\}  }\right)$。

We proceed with a lemma that gives a bound on the time to materialize the views in the view tree returned by the procedure AuxVIEW in Figure 8.

我们继续给出一个引理，该引理界定了图8中AuxVIEW过程返回的视图树内实例化视图所需的时间上限。

Lemma C.3. Let $T$ be a view tree and $M$ the time to materialize the views in $T$ . The views in the view tree $\operatorname{AuxView}\left( {\cdot ,T}\right)$ can be materialized in time $\mathcal{O}\left( M\right)$ .

引理C.3。设$T$为视图树，$M$为实例化$T$中视图所需时间。视图树$\operatorname{AuxView}\left( {\cdot ,T}\right)$中的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( M\right)$时间内完成实例化。

Proof. Assume that the parameters of the procedure AuxVIEW are $\mathcal{Z}$ and $T$ . Let $V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ be the root of $T$ . If the condition in Line 3 of the procedure AuxVIEW does not hold,the procedure returns $T$ (Line 5). Otherwise,it returns a view tree ${T}^{\prime }$ that results from $T$ by adding a view ${V}^{\prime }\left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( Z\right) }\right)$ on top of $V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ (Line 4). Since $\operatorname{anc}\left( Z\right)  \subset  \mathcal{S},{V}^{\prime }\left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( Z\right) }\right)$ results from $V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ by aggregating away the variables in $\mathcal{S} - \operatorname{anc}\left( Z\right)$ . Since the size of $V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ must be $\mathcal{O}\left( M\right)$ and the variables can be aggregated away in time linear in the size of $V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ ,the overall time to materialize the views in the output tree ${T}^{\prime }$ is $\mathcal{O}\left( M\right)$ .

证明。假设过程AuxVIEW的参数为$\mathcal{Z}$和$T$。令$V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$为$T$的根节点。若过程AuxVIEW第3行的条件不成立，则返回$T$（第5行）。否则，返回通过在$V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$上添加视图${V}^{\prime }\left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( Z\right) }\right)$而生成的视图树${T}^{\prime }$（第4行）。由于$\operatorname{anc}\left( Z\right)  \subset  \mathcal{S},{V}^{\prime }\left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( Z\right) }\right)$是通过聚合掉$\mathcal{S} - \operatorname{anc}\left( Z\right)$中的变量从$V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$生成的。由于$V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$的大小必须为$\mathcal{O}\left( M\right)$，且变量可在与$V\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$大小线性相关的时间内被聚合，物化输出树${T}^{\prime }$中视图的总时间为$\mathcal{O}\left( M\right)$。

The following lemma says that if the input to the procedure BUILDVT in Figure 6 represents a free-connex query, the procedure outputs a view tree whose views can be materialized in time linear in the database size.

下述引理表明，若图6中过程BUILDVT的输入表示一个自由连接查询，则该过程输出的视图树其视图可在与数据库大小线性相关的时间内被物化。

Lemma C.4. Let $\omega$ be a canonical variable order, $X$ a node in $\omega ,N$ the size of the leaf relations of $\omega$ ,and $\mathcal{F}$ a set of variables. If the query ${Q}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }\right)  =$ join of atoms $\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$ with ${\mathcal{F}}^{\prime } = \mathcal{F} \cap  \left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right) }\right)$ is free-connex,the views in the view tree $\operatorname{BulLDVT}\left( {\cdot ,{\omega }_{X},\mathcal{F}}\right)$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time.

引理C.4。设$\omega$为规范变量序，$X$为$\omega ,N$中的节点，$\omega$的叶关系大小为$\mathcal{F}$的变量集合。若原子式$\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$与${\mathcal{F}}^{\prime } = \mathcal{F} \cap  \left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right) }\right)$的连接查询${Q}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }\right)  =$是自由连接的，则视图树$\operatorname{BulLDVT}\left( {\cdot ,{\omega }_{X},\mathcal{F}}\right)$中的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内被物化。

Proof. The proof is by induction over the structure of the variable order ${\omega }_{X}$ .

证明。通过对变量序${\omega }_{X}$的结构进行归纳证明。

Base case: Assume that $X$ is a single atom $R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$ . In this case,the procedure BUILDVT returns this atom,which can obviously be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time.

基础情形：假设$X$是单个原子式$R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$。此时过程BUILDVT返回该原子式，显然可在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内被物化。

Inductive step: Assume that $X$ is a variable with child nodes ${X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{k}$ and ${Q}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }\right)  =$ join of atoms $\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$ a free-connex query. Let ${\mathcal{F}}_{i} = \mathcal{F} \cap  \left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( {X}_{i}\right)  \cup  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{{X}_{i}}\right) }\right)$ ,for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ .

归纳步骤：假设$X$是具有子节点${X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{k}$和${Q}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }\right)  =$的变量，原子式$\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$的连接为自由连接查询。令${\mathcal{F}}_{i} = \mathcal{F} \cap  \left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( {X}_{i}\right)  \cup  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{{X}_{i}}\right) }\right)$，其中$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$。

We first show that for each $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ :

我们首先证明对于每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$：

$$
{Q}_{{X}_{i}}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)  = \text{join of atoms}\left( {\omega }_{{X}_{i}}\right) \text{is free-connex.} \tag{C.4}
$$

An $\alpha$ -acyclic query is free-connex if and only if after adding an atom $R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$ ,where $\mathcal{X}$ is the set of free variables,the query remains $\alpha$ -acyclic [BB13]. A query is $\alpha$ -acyclic if it has a (not necessarily free-top) variable order with static width 1 [OZ15,BFMY83]. Let ${Q}_{X}^{\prime }$ be the query that results from ${Q}_{X}$ by adding a new atom $R\left( {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }\right)$ . Likewise,let ${Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }$ be the query that we obtain from ${Q}_{{X}_{i}}$ by adding a new atom ${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$ ,for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . Since ${Q}_{X}$ is free-connex,there must be a variable order ${\omega }^{\prime } = \left( {{T}_{{\omega }^{\prime }},{\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }^{\prime }}}\right)$ for ${Q}_{X}^{\prime }$ ,such that $\mathrm{w}\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right)  = 1$ . In the following,we turn ${\omega }^{\prime }$ into a variable order ${\omega }_{i}^{\prime } = \left( {{T}_{{\omega }_{i}^{\prime }},{\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }_{i}^{\prime }}}\right)$ for ${Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }$ with $\mathrm{w}\left( {\omega }_{i}^{\prime }\right)  = 1$ , for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . From this,it follows that ${Q}_{{X}_{i}}$ is free-connex,for each $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . To obtain ${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$ ,we traverse ${\omega }^{\prime }$ bottom-up and eliminate all variables and atoms (including $R\left( {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }\right)$ ) that do not occur in ${Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }$ . When eliminating a node $Y$ with a parent node $Z$ ,we append the children of $Y$ to $Z$ . If $Y$ does not have any parent node,the subtrees rooted at its children nodes become independent. Finally,we append ${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$ under the lowest variable $Y$ in the obtained variable order such that $Y$ is included in ${\mathcal{F}}_{i}$ . In the following we show that for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ :

当且仅当添加原子$R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$后（其中$\mathcal{X}$为自由变量集合），查询仍保持$\alpha$-无环性[BB13]，则该$\alpha$-无环查询是自由连接的。若查询存在静态宽度为1的变量序（不必是自由顶序）[OZ15,BFMY83]，则该查询具有$\alpha$-无环性。设${Q}_{X}^{\prime }$为通过向${Q}_{X}$添加新原子$R\left( {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }\right)$生成的查询。同理，对于$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$，设${Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }$为通过向${Q}_{{X}_{i}}$添加新原子${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$获得的查询。由于${Q}_{X}$是自由连接的，必然存在${Q}_{X}^{\prime }$的变量序${\omega }^{\prime } = \left( {{T}_{{\omega }^{\prime }},{\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }^{\prime }}}\right)$使得$\mathrm{w}\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right)  = 1$成立。下文将把${\omega }^{\prime }$转化为${Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }$的变量序${\omega }_{i}^{\prime } = \left( {{T}_{{\omega }_{i}^{\prime }},{\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }_{i}^{\prime }}}\right)$，并满足$\mathrm{w}\left( {\omega }_{i}^{\prime }\right)  = 1$条件（对于$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$）。由此可证，每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$对应的${Q}_{{X}_{i}}$都是自由连接的。为构建${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$，我们自底向上遍历${\omega }^{\prime }$，剔除所有未出现在${Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }$中的变量和原子（包括$R\left( {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }\right)$）。当剔除含有父节点$Z$的子节点$Y$时，将$Y$的子节点追加至$Z$。若$Y$无父节点，则以其子节点为根的子树将独立存在。最后，在所得变量序的最低变量$Y$下追加${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$，且需满足$Y$包含于${\mathcal{F}}_{i}$。下文将证明对于$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$：

(1) ${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$ is a valid variable order for ${Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }$ .

(1) ${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$是${Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }$的有效变量序

(2) $\mathrm{w}\left( {\omega }_{i}^{\prime }\right)  = 1$ .

(1) ${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$ is a valid variable order for ${Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }$ ,for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ : The following property follows from the construction of the variable order ${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$ :

(1) 对于$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$而言，${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$是${Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }$的有效变量序：该性质源自变量序${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$的构建过程

$\left( *\right)$ : Any two variables in $\operatorname{vars}\left( {Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }\right)$ that are on the same root-to-leaf path in ${\omega }^{\prime }$ remain on the same root-to-leaf path in ${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$ .

$\left( *\right)$：在${\omega }^{\prime }$中处于同一条根到叶路径的任意两个$\operatorname{vars}\left( {Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }\right)$内变量，在${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$中仍保持同路径关系

Each atom $K\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$ in atoms $\left( {Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }\right)  - \left\{  {{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right) }\right\}$ is also an atom in atoms $\left( {Q}_{X}^{\prime }\right)$ . Hence,the variables in $\mathcal{X}$ must be on the same root-to-leaf path in ${\omega }^{\prime }$ . Due to $\left( *\right)$ ,they also must be on the same root-to-leaf path in ${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$ . It remains to show that all variables in ${\mathcal{F}}_{i}$ are on the same root-to-leaf path in ${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$ . In the canonical variable order $\omega$ ,each variable in ${\mathcal{F}}^{\prime } - \{ X\}$ is either above or below $X$ . Hence, $X$ depends on all variables in ${\mathcal{F}}^{\prime } - \{ X\}$ ,which means that all variables in ${\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }$ must be on the same root-to-leaf path in ${\omega }^{\prime }$ . Due to ${\mathcal{F}}_{i} \subseteq  \{ X\}  \cup  {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }$ and Property (*),all variables in ${\mathcal{F}}_{i}$ must be on the same root-to-leaf path in ${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$ .

原子集合$\left( {Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }\right)  - \left\{  {{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right) }\right\}$中的每个原子$K\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$同时也是原子集合$\left( {Q}_{X}^{\prime }\right)$中的原子。因此，$\mathcal{X}$中的变量在${\omega }^{\prime }$中必须位于同一条根到叶的路径上。由于$\left( *\right)$的存在，这些变量在${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$中也必须位于同一条根到叶的路径上。接下来需要证明${\mathcal{F}}_{i}$中的所有变量在${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$中都位于同一条根到叶的路径上。在规范变量序$\omega$中，${\mathcal{F}}^{\prime } - \{ X\}$中的每个变量要么位于$X$之上，要么位于其下。因此，$X$依赖于${\mathcal{F}}^{\prime } - \{ X\}$中的所有变量，这意味着${\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }$中的所有变量在${\omega }^{\prime }$中必须位于同一条根到叶的路径上。根据${\mathcal{F}}_{i} \subseteq  \{ X\}  \cup  {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }$和性质(*)，${\mathcal{F}}_{i}$中的所有变量在${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$中必须位于同一条根到叶的路径上。

(2) $\mathrm{w}\left( {\omega }_{i}^{\prime }\right)  = 1$ ,for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   :$ Let $Y \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{i}^{\prime }\right)$ for some $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . We need to show that ${\rho }_{{Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }}^{ * }\left( {\{ Y\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }_{i}^{\prime }}\left( Y\right) }\right)  = 1$ . By Proposition 4.5,it suffices to show that ${Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }$ contains an atom that covers $\{ Y\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }_{i}^{\prime }}\left( Y\right)$ ,i.e.,whose schema includes the latter set. By construction, $Y$ must be included in ${\omega }^{\prime }$ . First,observe that any two variables that are not dependent in ${Q}_{X}^{\prime }$ cannot be dependent in ${Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }$ . Moreover,each variable $Z$ included in the root path of $Y$ in ${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$ ,is included in the root path of $Y$ in ${\omega }^{\prime }$ . Hence:

(2) $\mathrm{w}\left( {\omega }_{i}^{\prime }\right)  = 1$，对于$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   :$，设$Y \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{i}^{\prime }\right)$对于某个$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$成立。我们需要证明${\rho }_{{Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }}^{ * }\left( {\{ Y\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }_{i}^{\prime }}\left( Y\right) }\right)  = 1$。根据命题4.5，只需证明${Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }$包含一个覆盖$\{ Y\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }_{i}^{\prime }}\left( Y\right)$的原子，即其模式包含后者集合。根据构造，$Y$必须被包含在${\omega }^{\prime }$中。首先注意到，任何在${Q}_{X}^{\prime }$中不依赖的两个变量在${Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }$中也不可能依赖。此外，包含在${\omega }_{i}^{\prime }$中$Y$的根路径上的每个变量$Z$，也都包含在${\omega }^{\prime }$中$Y$的根路径上。因此：

$\left( {* * }\right) \{ Y\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }_{i}^{\prime }}\left( Y\right)  \subseteq  \left( {\{ Y\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }^{\prime }}\left( Y\right) }\right)  \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }\right) .$

Due to $\mathrm{w}\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right)  = 1$ and Proposition 4.5,there must be an atom $K\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( {Q}_{X}^{\prime }\right)$ such that $\{ Y\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }^{\prime }}\left( Y\right)  \subseteq  \mathcal{X}$ . First,assume that $K\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \neq  R\left( {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }\right)$ . Since $\mathcal{X}$ includes $Y$ , the atom $K\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$ must be under the variable $Y$ in ${\omega }_{{X}_{i}}$ ,which means that atoms $\left( {Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }\right)$ includes $K\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$ . Due to Property $\left( {* * }\right) ,K\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$ covers $\{ Y\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }_{i}^{\prime }}\left( Y\right)$ . Now assume that $K\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  = R\left( {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }\right)$ . This means that $\{ Y\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }^{\prime }}\left( Y\right)  \subseteq  {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }$ . By Property $\left( {* * }\right) ,\{ Y\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }_{s}^{\prime }}\left( Y\right)  \subseteq$ $\left( {\{ Y\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }^{\prime }}\left( Y\right) }\right)  \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }\right)  \subseteq  {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime } \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }\right)$ . Since ${\mathcal{F}}_{i} = {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime } \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }\right) ,{R}_{i}{\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right) }^{\prime }$ covers $\{ Y\}  \cup  {de}{p}_{{\omega }_{i}^{\prime }}\left( Y\right)$ .

根据$\mathrm{w}\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right)  = 1$及命题4.5(Proposition 4.5)，必然存在一个原子$K\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( {Q}_{X}^{\prime }\right)$使得$\{ Y\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }^{\prime }}\left( Y\right)  \subseteq  \mathcal{X}$。首先假设$K\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  \neq  R\left( {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }\right)$。由于$\mathcal{X}$包含$Y$，该原子$K\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$在${\omega }_{{X}_{i}}$中必须位于变量$Y$之下，这意味着原子集合$\left( {Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }\right)$包含$K\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$。根据性质$\left( {* * }\right) ,K\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$可覆盖$\{ Y\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }_{i}^{\prime }}\left( Y\right)$。现假设$K\left( \mathcal{X}\right)  = R\left( {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }\right)$，即表明$\{ Y\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }^{\prime }}\left( Y\right)  \subseteq  {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime }$。由性质$\left( {* * }\right) ,\{ Y\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }_{s}^{\prime }}\left( Y\right)  \subseteq$可得$\left( {\{ Y\}  \cup  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }^{\prime }}\left( Y\right) }\right)  \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }\right)  \subseteq  {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime } \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }\right)$。因${\mathcal{F}}_{i} = {\mathcal{F}}^{\prime } \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {Q}_{{X}_{i}}^{\prime }\right) ,{R}_{i}{\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right) }^{\prime }$覆盖$\{ Y\}  \cup  {de}{p}_{{\omega }_{i}^{\prime }}\left( Y\right)$。

This completes the proof of (C.4).

由此完成(C.4)的证明。

Let $T = \operatorname{BUILDVT}\left( {\cdot ,{\omega }_{X},\mathcal{F}}\right)$ . To construct the view tree $T$ ,the procedure BUILDVT first constructs the view trees ${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ with ${T}_{i} = \operatorname{BulLDVT}\left( {\cdot ,{\omega }_{{X}_{i}},\mathcal{F}}\right)$ for each $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ (Line 2). By Property (C.4) and the induction hypothesis, the views in each view tree ${T}_{i}$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time. In the following we show that all views in $T$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time. We distinguish whether $\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$ is included in $\mathcal{F}$ (Lines 4-7) or not (Lines 8-10):

设$T = \operatorname{BUILDVT}\left( {\cdot ,{\omega }_{X},\mathcal{F}}\right)$。为构建视图树$T$，BUILDVT过程首先为每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$构造带${T}_{i} = \operatorname{BulLDVT}\left( {\cdot ,{\omega }_{{X}_{i}},\mathcal{F}}\right)$的视图树${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$(第2行)。根据性质(C.4)及归纳假设，各视图树${T}_{i}$中的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内物化。以下证明$T$中所有视图均可在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内物化。我们将区分$\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$是否包含于$\mathcal{F}$(第4-7行)两种情况(第8-10行)：

Case $\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}  \subseteq  \mathcal{F}$ : In this case,it holds $T = \operatorname{NEWVT}\left( {\cdot ,{\mathcal{F}}_{X}\text{,subtrees}}\right)$ ,where subtrees $= {\left\{  \operatorname{AuxV}\operatorname{IEW}\left( {X}_{i},{T}_{i}\right) \right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ and ${\mathcal{F}}_{X} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$ . The procedures NEWVT and AuxView are given in Figures 7 and 8, respectively. By the induction hypothesis and Lemma C.3,the views in subtrees can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time. Let ${V}_{1}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}^{\prime }\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}^{\prime }\right)$ be the roots of the trees in subtrees. The overall size of these root views must be $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ . Observation C.1.(1) implies that for any $i,j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ with $i \neq  j$ ,it holds ${\mathcal{F}}_{i}^{\prime } \cap  {\mathcal{F}}_{i}^{\prime } = {\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ . Hence, the query ${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)  = {V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}^{\prime }\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}^{\prime }\right)$ is ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical. Since ${\mathcal{F}}_{X} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}  \subseteq$ $\mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{\mathcal{F}}_{i}^{\prime }$ ,it follows from Lemma C. 2 that the views in $T$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time.

情形 $\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}  \subseteq  \mathcal{F}$：在此情形下，满足 $T = \operatorname{NEWVT}\left( {\cdot ,{\mathcal{F}}_{X}\text{,subtrees}}\right)$，其中子树 $= {\left\{  \operatorname{AuxV}\operatorname{IEW}\left( {X}_{i},{T}_{i}\right) \right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ 与 ${\mathcal{F}}_{X} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$。NEWVT和AuxView过程分别如图7和图8所示。根据归纳假设及引理C.3，子树中的视图可在 $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ 时间内物化。设 ${V}_{1}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}^{\prime }\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}^{\prime }\right)$ 为子树中各树的根节点，这些根视图的总大小必为 $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$。观察结论C.1.(1)表明：对于任意满足 $i \neq  j$ 的 $i,j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$，均有 ${\mathcal{F}}_{i}^{\prime } \cap  {\mathcal{F}}_{i}^{\prime } = {\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ 成立。因此，查询 ${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)  = {V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}^{\prime }\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}^{\prime }\right)$ 是 ${\delta }_{0}$ 层次化的。由于 ${\mathcal{F}}_{X} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}  \subseteq$ $\mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{\mathcal{F}}_{i}^{\prime }$，根据引理C.2可知，$T$ 中的视图可在 $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ 时间内完成物化。

Case $\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}  \nsubseteq  \mathcal{F}$ : n this case,we have $T = \operatorname{NEWVT}\left( {\cdot ,{\mathcal{F}}_{X}\text{,subtrees }}\right)$ ,where ${\mathcal{F}}_{X} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \left( {\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right) }\right)$ and subtrees $= {\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ . Let ${V}_{i}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}^{\prime }\right)$ be the root of ${T}_{i}$ , for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . By the definition of the procedure NEWVT,the tree $T$ results from the trees ${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ by adding a new root view defined by ${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)  = {V}_{1}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}^{\prime }\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}^{\prime }\right)$ . It follows from Observation C.1.(2),that ${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$ can be rewritten as ${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)  =$ join of atoms $\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$ . We show that the view ${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time. The set atoms $\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$ must contain an atom $R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$ with ${\mathcal{F}}_{X} \subseteq  \mathcal{Y}$ (Lemma 35 in [KNOZ19]). Hence,we can easily materialize the view ${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$ by using the InsideOut algorithm [AKNR16] to aggregate away all variables that are not included in ${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ . Since the query defining the view ${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$ is $\left( {\alpha \text{-}}\right)$ acyclic,the whole computation takes $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time.

情形$\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}  \nsubseteq  \mathcal{F}$：在此情形中，我们拥有$T = \operatorname{NEWVT}\left( {\cdot ,{\mathcal{F}}_{X}\text{,subtrees }}\right)$，其中${\mathcal{F}}_{X} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \left( {\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right) }\right)$及子树$= {\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$。设${V}_{i}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}^{\prime }\right)$为${T}_{i}$的根节点，对于$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$而言。根据NEWVT过程的定义，树$T$通过添加由${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)  = {V}_{1}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}^{\prime }\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}^{\prime }\right)$定义的新根视图，从树${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$生成。由观察结论C.1.(2)可知，${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$可重写为原子$\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$的连接${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)  =$。我们证明视图${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$可在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内实现物化。原子集合$\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$必须包含具有${\mathcal{F}}_{X} \subseteq  \mathcal{Y}$的原子$R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$（参见[KNOZ19]中引理35）。因此，通过使用InsideOut算法[AKNR16]聚合所有未包含在${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$中的变量，可以轻松实现视图${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$的物化。由于定义该视图的查询是$\left( {\alpha \text{-}}\right)$无环的，整个计算过程耗时$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$。

The next lemma upper bounds the time to materialize the views constructed by the procedure BUILDVT in Figure 6 for a variable order ${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$ . This variable order has the same structure as ${\omega }_{X}$ yet each atom $R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$ is replaced by the light part ${R}^{\text{keys }}\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$ of relation $R$ partitioned on the variable set keys (cf. Section 5.2).

下一个引理给出了针对变量序${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$，通过图6中BUILDVT过程构建视图物化时间的上界。该变量序与${\omega }_{X}$具有相同结构，但每个原子$R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$被替换为基于变量集键分区的关系$R$的轻量部分${R}^{\text{keys }}\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$（参见第5.2节）。

Lemma C.5. Given a canonical variable order $\omega$ ,a node $X$ in $\omega$ ,the size $N$ of the leaf relations in $\omega ,$ keys $= \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\} ,\mathcal{F} \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( \omega \right)$ ,and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ . The view tree BUILDVT $\left( {\cdot ,{\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }},\mathcal{F}}\right)$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\max \{ 1,1 + \left( {\xi \left( {{\omega }^{\text{keys }},X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon \} }\right)$ time.

引理C.5. 给定规范变量序$\omega$、节点$X$、叶关系大小$N$、键$= \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\} ,\mathcal{F} \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( \omega \right)$及$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，视图树BUILDVT$\left( {\cdot ,{\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }},\mathcal{F}}\right)$可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\max \{ 1,1 + \left( {\xi \left( {{\omega }^{\text{keys }},X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon \} }\right)$时间内物化。

Proof. For a node $X$ in ${\omega }^{\text{keys }}$ ,we set

证明. 对于${\omega }^{\text{keys }}$中的节点$X$，我们设定

$$
{m}_{X} = \max \{ 1,1 + \left( {\xi \left( {{\omega }^{\text{keys }},X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon \} .
$$

The proof is by induction on the structure of ${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$ .

该证明基于${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$结构的归纳法。

Base case: If ${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$ is a single atom $R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$ ,the procedure BUILDVT returns this atom, which can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time. Since ${m}_{X} \geq  1$ ,this completes the base case.

基础情形：若${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$是单原子$R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$，过程BUILDVT返回该原子，其物化时间为$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$。由于${m}_{X} \geq  1$，至此完成基础情形证明。

Inductive step: Assume $X \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }^{\text{keys }}\right)$ and has child nodes ${X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{k}$ . The procedure first calls BUILDVT $\left( {\cdot ,{\omega }_{{X}_{i}}^{\text{keys }},\mathcal{F}}\right)$ for each $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ and produces the view trees ${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ (Line 2). By induction hypothesis,we need $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{{X}_{i}}}\right)$ time to materialize the views in each view tree ${T}_{i}$ with $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . The procedure BUILDVT distinguishes whether $\left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right) \cup \{ X\} }\right)  \subseteq  \mathcal{F}$ (Lines 4-7) or not (Lines 8-10).

归纳步骤：假设$X \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }^{\text{keys }}\right)$存在子节点${X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{k}$。该过程首先为每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$调用BUILDVT$\left( {\cdot ,{\omega }_{{X}_{i}}^{\text{keys }},\mathcal{F}}\right)$并生成视图树${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$（第2行）。根据归纳假设，物化每个视图树${T}_{i}$中的视图需要$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{{X}_{i}}}\right)$时间。BUILDVT过程会区分$\left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right) \cup \{ X\} }\right)  \subseteq  \mathcal{F}$情形（第4-7行）与非该情形（第8-10行）。

Case $\left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right) \cup \{ X\} }\right)  \subseteq  \mathcal{F}$ : The view tree $T$ returned by the procedure BUILDVT is $\operatorname{NEWVT}\left( {\cdot ,{\mathcal{F}}_{X}}\right.$ ,subtrees),where subtrees is defined as ${\left\{  \operatorname{AuxView}\left( {X}_{i},{T}_{i}\right) \right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ and ${\mathcal{F}}_{X} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$ . By induction hypothesis and Inequality (C.3),the overall time to materialize the views in ${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$ . For each view tree ${T}_{i}$ ,AuxVIEW $\left( {{X}_{i},{T}_{i}}\right)$ adds at most one view with schema anc $\left( {X}_{i}\right)$ on top of the root view of ${T}_{i}$ . Then, $\operatorname{anc}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$ is a subset of the schema of the root view of ${T}_{i}$ . Since the size of the root view of ${T}_{i}$ must be bounded by $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$ ,the view added by AuxVIEW can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$ time. Assume that ${V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}\right)$ are the roots of the view trees in subtrees. In case $k = 1$ and $\mathcal{F} = {\mathcal{F}}_{i}$ ,NEWVT $\left( {{V}_{X},{\mathcal{F}}_{X}\text{,subtrees}}\right)$ returns ${V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right)$ ; otherwise,it returns a view tree that has ${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)  = {V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}\right)$ as root view and subtrees as subtrees. By the definition of AuxVIEW,it holds ${\mathcal{F}}_{i} \cap  {\mathcal{F}}_{j} = \left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right) \cup \{ X\} }\right)  = {\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ for any $i,j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . Hence, the view ${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$ can be computed by iterating over the tuples in a view ${V}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$ with $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ and filtering out those tuples that do not have matching tuples in all views ${V}_{j}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{j}\right)$ with $j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   - \{ i\}$ . Since the size of ${V}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$ is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$ and materialized views allow constant time lookups,the view ${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$ can be computed in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$ time. It follows that the view tree $T$ returned by BUILDVT can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$ time. This completes the inductive step for this case.

案例$\left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right) \cup \{ X\} }\right)  \subseteq  \mathcal{F}$：过程BUILDVT返回的视图树$T$由$\operatorname{NEWVT}\left( {\cdot ,{\mathcal{F}}_{X}}\right.$（子树）构成，其中子树定义为${\left\{  \operatorname{AuxView}\left( {X}_{i},{T}_{i}\right) \right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$和${\mathcal{F}}_{X} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$。根据归纳假设和不等式(C.3)，实例化${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$中视图的总时间为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$。对于每个视图树${T}_{i}$，AuxVIEW$\left( {{X}_{i},{T}_{i}}\right)$最多在${T}_{i}$的根视图之上添加一个模式为anc$\left( {X}_{i}\right)$的视图。因此，$\operatorname{anc}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$是${T}_{i}$根视图模式的子集。由于${T}_{i}$根视图的大小必然受限于$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$，AuxVIEW添加的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$时间内实例化。假设${V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}\right)$是子树中各视图树的根节点。在$k = 1$和$\mathcal{F} = {\mathcal{F}}_{i}$情况下，NEWVT$\left( {{V}_{X},{\mathcal{F}}_{X}\text{,subtrees}}\right)$返回${V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right)$；否则返回以${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)  = {V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}\right)$为根视图、子树为子树的视图树。根据AuxVIEW的定义，对于任意$i,j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$都满足${\mathcal{F}}_{i} \cap  {\mathcal{F}}_{j} = \left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right) \cup \{ X\} }\right)  = {\mathcal{F}}_{X}$。因此，视图${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$可通过迭代模式为$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$的视图${V}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$中的元组，并过滤掉那些在所有模式为$j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   - \{ i\}$的视图${V}_{j}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{j}\right)$中没有匹配项的元组来计算。由于${V}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$的大小为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$且物化视图支持常数时间查找，视图${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$时间内完成计算。由此可得，BUILDVT返回的视图树$T$可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$时间内实例化。至此完成本案例的归纳步骤。

Case $\left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right) \cup \{ X\} }\right)  \nsubseteq  \mathcal{F}$ : The procedure BUILDVT sets ${\mathcal{F}}_{X} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \left( {\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right) }\right)$ and subtrees $= {\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ . The view tree $T$ returned by the procedure BUILDVT is $\operatorname{NewVT}\left( {\cdot ,{\mathcal{F}}_{X}\text{,subtrees }}\right)$ . We show that all views in the view tree $T$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$ time. We analyze the steps in NEWVT. In case subtrees consists of a single tree ${T}^{\prime }$ such that the schema of the root view of ${T}^{\prime }$ is ${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ ,the procedure NEWVT returns the view tree ${T}^{\prime }$ . By induction hypothesis and Inequality (C.3),the views in $T = {T}^{\prime }$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$ time. Otherwise,let $V\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$ be the root view of ${T}_{i}$ ,for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . The tree $T$ returned by NEWVT consists of the root view

案例$\left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right) \cup \{ X\} }\right)  \nsubseteq  \mathcal{F}$：过程BUILDVT设置${\mathcal{F}}_{X} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \left( {\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right) }\right)$与子树$= {\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$。该过程返回的视图树$T$为$\operatorname{NewVT}\left( {\cdot ,{\mathcal{F}}_{X}\text{,subtrees }}\right)$。我们证明视图树$T$中的所有视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$时间内物化。分析NEWVT中的步骤：若子树由单一树${T}^{\prime }$组成，且${T}^{\prime }$根视图的模式为${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$，则过程NEWVT返回该视图树${T}^{\prime }$。根据归纳假设与不等式(C.3)，$T = {T}^{\prime }$中的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$时间内物化。否则，令$V\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$作为${T}_{i}$的根视图（其中$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$），NEWVT返回的树$T$由该根视图构成

$$
{V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)  = {V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}\right) 
$$

with subtrees ${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ . By induction hypothesis and Inequality (C.3),the views in the trees ${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$ time. It suffices to show that ${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$ time. Using Observation C.1.(2),we rewrite the view ${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$ using the leaf atoms of ${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$ :

附带子树${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$。根据归纳假设与不等式(C.3)，子树${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$中的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$时间内物化。只需证明${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$时间内物化。运用观察结论C.1.(2)，我们使用${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$的叶原子重写视图${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$：

$$
{V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)  = \text{ join of atoms }\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right) .
$$

We materialize the view ${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$ as follows. Using the InsideOut algorithm [AKNR16],we first aggregate away all variables in $\operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right)  - {\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ that are not above a variable from ${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ . Since the view ${V}_{X}$ is defined by an $\alpha$ -acyclic query,the time required by this step is $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ . Let ${V}_{X}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)  = {R}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}\right)$ be the resulting query. We distinguish between two subcases.

按如下步骤物化视图${V}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$：首先采用InsideOut算法[AKNR16]，聚合消除$\operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right)  - {\mathcal{F}}_{X}$中所有不位于${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$变量上方的变量。由于视图${V}_{X}$由$\alpha$非循环查询定义，此步骤耗时$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$。设${V}_{X}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)  = {R}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}\right)$为最终查询，我们区分两种子情况。

Subcase 1: For all ${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$ ,it holds ${\mathcal{F}}_{i} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right)  = \varnothing$

子情况1：对于所有${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$，均满足${\mathcal{F}}_{i} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right)  = \varnothing$

This means that ${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ and each ${\mathcal{F}}_{i}$ are contained in anc $\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$ . Since ${\omega }^{\text{keys }}$ is canonical, the inner nodes of each root-to-leaf path are the variables of an atom. Hence, there is an ${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$ with $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ such that ${\mathcal{F}}_{i}$ subsumes ${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ and each ${\mathcal{F}}_{j}$ with $j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . Thus,we can materialize the result of ${V}_{X}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$ in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time by iterating over the tuples in ${R}_{i}$ and doing constant-time lookups in the other relations.

这意味着${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$与每个${\mathcal{F}}_{i}$都包含在anc$\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$中。由于${\omega }^{\text{keys }}$是规范化的，每条根到叶路径的内部节点构成一个原子的变量集。因此，存在一个具有$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$特性的${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$，使得${\mathcal{F}}_{i}$能够包含${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$及所有具有$j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$特性的${\mathcal{F}}_{j}$。由此，我们可通过遍历${R}_{i}$中的元组并在其他关系中进行常数时间查找，在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内物化${V}_{X}^{\prime }\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$的结果。

Subcase 2: There is an ${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$ with ${\mathcal{F}}_{i} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right)  \neq  \varnothing$

子情况2：存在一个具有${\mathcal{F}}_{i} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right)  \neq  \varnothing$特性的${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$

Let $\mathbf{\lambda } = {\left( {\lambda }_{{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right) }\right) }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ be an edge cover of ${\mathcal{F}}_{X} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right)$ with $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{\lambda }_{{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right) } = {\rho }_{{V}_{X}^{\prime }}^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right.  \cap$ $\operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right)$ ). Since ${V}_{X}^{\prime }$ is hierarchical,we can assume that each ${\lambda }_{{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right) }$ is either 0 or 1 (Proposition 4.5). There must be at least one ${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$ with ${\lambda }_{{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right) } = 1$ ,otherwise there cannot be any variable from ${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ in ${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$ and we fall back to Subcase 1. Since ${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$ is canonical,for each atom ${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$ with ${\lambda }_{{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right) } = 0$ ,there must be a witness atom ${R}_{j}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{j}\right)$ such that ${\lambda }_{{R}_{j}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{j}\right) } = 1$ and ${\mathcal{F}}_{i} \subseteq  {\mathcal{F}}_{j}$ . The atoms ${R}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}\right)$ can still contain variables not included in ${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ . Each such variable appears above at least one variable from ${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ in ${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$ . We first compute the result of the view ${V}_{X}^{\prime \prime }\left( {\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{\mathcal{F}}_{k}}\right)  = {R}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}\right)$ as follows. We choose an arbitrary atom ${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$ with ${\lambda }_{{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right) } = 1$ and iterate over the tuples in ${R}_{i}$ . For each such tuple,we iterate over the matching tuples in the other atoms mapped to 1 by $\mathbf{\lambda }$ . For atoms that are not mapped to 1 , it suffices to do constant-time lookups while iterating over one of their witnesses. To obtain the result of ${V}_{X}^{\prime }$ from ${V}_{X}^{\prime \prime }$ ,we aggregate away all variables not included in ${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ . Recall that for each atom ${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$ ,there is an atom in atoms $\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right)$ that is the light part of a relation partitioned on keys $= \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$ . Hence,each tuple in the relation of an atom mapped to 1 by $\mathbf{\lambda }$ can be paired with $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$ tuples in the relation of any other atom mapped to 1 . This means that the time to materialize ${V}_{X}^{\prime \prime }$ and hence ${V}_{X}^{\prime }$ is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}^{\prime }}\right)$ where ${m}^{\prime } = 1 + \left( {{\rho }_{{V}_{X}^{\prime }}^{ * }\left( {{\mathcal{F}}_{X} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right) }\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon$ . Since ${V}_{X}^{\prime }$ results from ${V}_{X}$ by aggregating away variables in $\operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right)  - {\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ ,we have ${\rho }_{{V}_{X}^{\prime }}^{ * }\left( {{\mathcal{F}}_{X} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right) }\right)  = {\rho }_{{V}_{X}}^{ * }\left( {{\mathcal{F}}_{X} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right) }\right)$ . It follows from $\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}  \nsubseteq  \mathcal{F}$ that ${\rho }_{{V}_{X}}^{ * }\left( {{\mathcal{F}}_{X} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right) }\right)  = \xi \left( {{\omega }^{\text{keys }},X,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ . Hence,the view ${V}_{X}^{\prime }$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\xi \left( {{\omega }^{\text{keys }},X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ time.

设$\mathbf{\lambda } = {\left( {\lambda }_{{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right) }\right) }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$为${\mathcal{F}}_{X} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right)$的边覆盖，其中$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{\lambda }_{{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right) } = {\rho }_{{V}_{X}^{\prime }}^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right.  \cap$ $\operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right)$）。由于${V}_{X}^{\prime }$具有层次性，根据命题4.5，可假定每个${\lambda }_{{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right) }$取值仅为0或1。必须存在至少一个满足${\lambda }_{{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right) } = 1$的原子${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$，否则${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$中将不包含任何来自${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$的变量，此时需退回到子情况1处理。鉴于${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$是规范化的，对于每个满足${\lambda }_{{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right) } = 0$的原子${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$，必存在见证原子${R}_{j}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{j}\right)$使得${\lambda }_{{R}_{j}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{j}\right) } = 1$且${\mathcal{F}}_{i} \subseteq  {\mathcal{F}}_{j}$。原子${R}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}\right)$仍可能包含未纳入${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$的变量，这些变量在${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$中至少出现在${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$某个变量的上层。我们首先按如下方式计算视图${V}_{X}^{\prime \prime }\left( {\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{\mathcal{F}}_{k}}\right)  = {R}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}\right)$的结果：随机选取一个满足${\lambda }_{{R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right) } = 1$的原子${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$，遍历${R}_{i}$中的元组，对每个元组迭代处理被$\mathbf{\lambda }$映射为1的其他原子中的匹配元组。对于非1映射的原子，在遍历其任一见证原子时执行常数时间查找即可。为从${V}_{X}^{\prime \prime }$获取${V}_{X}^{\prime }$的结果，需聚合消除所有未包含在${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$中的变量。注意每个原子${R}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$在原子集$\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right)$中都存在对应原子，其作为基于键$= \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$划分关系的轻量部分。因此，被$\mathbf{\lambda }$映射为1的原子关系中，每个元组可与任何其他1映射原子关系中的$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$个元组配对。这意味着物化${V}_{X}^{\prime \prime }$及${V}_{X}^{\prime }$的时间复杂度为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}^{\prime }}\right)$，其中${m}^{\prime } = 1 + \left( {{\rho }_{{V}_{X}^{\prime }}^{ * }\left( {{\mathcal{F}}_{X} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right) }\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon$。由于${V}_{X}^{\prime }$是通过从${V}_{X}$聚合消除$\operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right)  - {\mathcal{F}}_{X}$中的变量所得，故有${\rho }_{{V}_{X}^{\prime }}^{ * }\left( {{\mathcal{F}}_{X} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right) }\right)  = {\rho }_{{V}_{X}}^{ * }\left( {{\mathcal{F}}_{X} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right) }\right)$。根据$\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}  \nsubseteq  \mathcal{F}$可推导出${\rho }_{{V}_{X}}^{ * }\left( {{\mathcal{F}}_{X} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}\right) }\right)  = \xi \left( {{\omega }^{\text{keys }},X,\mathcal{F}}\right)$。因此，视图${V}_{X}^{\prime }$可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\xi \left( {{\omega }^{\text{keys }},X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$时间内完成物化。

We sum up the analysis for the case $\left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right) \cup \{ X\} }\right)  \nsubseteq  \mathcal{F}$ : the initial aggregation step and the computation in Subcase 1 take $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time; the computation in Subcase 2 takes $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\xi \left( {{\omega }^{\text{keys }},X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ time. Thus,given ${m}_{X} = \max \{ 1,1 + \left( {\xi \left( {{\omega }^{\text{keys }},X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon \}$ ,the time to materialize the result of ${V}_{X}$ is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$ . This completes the inductive step in case $\left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right) \cup \{ X\} }\right)  \nsubseteq  \mathcal{F}$ .

我们对$\left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right) \cup \{ X\} }\right)  \nsubseteq  \mathcal{F}$情形下的分析进行总结：初始聚合步骤及子情形1中的计算耗时$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$；子情形2中的计算耗时$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {\xi \left( {{\omega }^{\text{keys }},X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$。因此，给定${m}_{X} = \max \{ 1,1 + \left( {\xi \left( {{\omega }^{\text{keys }},X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon \}$时，物化${V}_{X}$结果的时间为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}}\right)$。至此完成$\left( {\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right) \cup \{ X\} }\right)  \nsubseteq  \mathcal{F}$情形下的归纳步骤。

The next lemma states that the view trees returned by the procedure INDICATORVTS from Figure 10 can be materialized in time linear in the database size.

下一条引理表明，图10中INDICATORVTS过程返回的视图树可在与数据库大小呈线性关系的时间内完成物化。

Lemma C.6. Let $\omega$ be a canonical variable order, $X$ a variable in $\omega$ ,and $N$ the size of the leaf relations in the variable order $\omega$ . The views in the view trees returned by INDICATORVTS $\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time.

引理C.6。设$\omega$为规范变量序，$X$是$\omega$中的一个变量，$N$表示变量序$\omega$中叶关系的大小。INDICATORVTS$\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$返回的视图树中的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内完成物化。

Proof. In Lines 3 and 4, the procedure constructs the view tree alltree, which is defined by BUILDVT("All", ${\omega }_{X}$ ,keys) and the view tree ltree $=$ BUILDVT("L", ${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$ ,keys),where keys consists of the set $\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$ . The variable order ${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$ results from ${\omega }_{X}$ by replacing each atom $R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$ by the atom ${R}^{\text{keys }}\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$ ,which denotes the light part of relation $R$ partitioned on keys. These light parts can be computed in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time. The queries ${Q}_{X}\left( \text{keys}\right)  =$ join of atoms $\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$ and ${Q}_{X}^{\text{keys}}\left( \text{keys}\right)  =$ join of atoms $\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$ are free-connex. By using Lemma C.4, we derive that the views in alltree and ltree can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time. Hence,the roots allroot and lroot of alltree and ltree,respectively,can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time as well. It remains to analyze the time to materialize the views in the view tree htree $= \operatorname{NEWVT}\left( {\cdot ,\mathcal{F},\{ \text{allroot,}\neg \text{lroot}\} }\right)$ (Line 7). It follows from Observation C.1.(1) that $V\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  =$ allroot, $\nexists$ lroot is ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical. By using Lemma C.2,we derive that the views in htree can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time. Overall,all views in the view trees (alltree,ltree,htree) can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time.
