证明。在第3-4行中，该过程构建了由BUILDVT("All",${\omega }_{X}$,keys)定义的视图树alltree，以及视图树ltree$=$BUILDVT("L",${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$,keys)，其中keys由集合$\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$构成。变量序${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$是通过将${\omega }_{X}$中的每个原子$R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$替换为表示按keys分区后关系$R$轻量部分的原子${R}^{\text{keys }}\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$而得。这些轻量部分可在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内计算完成。查询${Q}_{X}\left( \text{keys}\right)  =$连接原子$\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$与${Q}_{X}^{\text{keys}}\left( \text{keys}\right)  =$连接原子$\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$是自由连接的。应用引理C.4可推导出alltree和ltree中的视图均可在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内物化。因此，alltree的根节点allroot和ltree的根节点lroot同样可在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内物化。最后需要分析视图树htree$= \operatorname{NEWVT}\left( {\cdot ,\mathcal{F},\{ \text{allroot,}\neg \text{lroot}\} }\right)$（第7行）中视图的物化时间。根据观察C.1.(1)可知，$V\left( \mathcal{F}\right)  =$allroot与$\nexists$lroot具有${\delta }_{0}$层次性。应用引理C.2可推导出htree中的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内物化。综上所述，视图树（alltree、ltree、htree）中的所有视图均可在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内完成物化。

We use Lemmas C.2-C.6 to show an upper bound on the time to materialize the views in any tree produced by the procedure $\tau$ in Figure 11.

我们利用引理C.2至C.6来证明，对于图11中过程$\tau$生成的任何树结构，其实例化视图所需时间的上界。

Lemma C.7. Let $\omega$ be a canonical variable order, $X$ a node in $\omega ,\mathcal{F} \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( \omega \right) ,N$ the size of the leaf relations in $\omega$ ,and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ . The views in the trees returned by $\tau \left( {{\omega }_{X},\mathcal{F}}\right)$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\max \{ 1,1 + \left( {\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon \} }\right)$ time.

引理C.7. 设$\omega$为规范变量序，$X$表示$\omega ,\mathcal{F} \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( \omega \right) ,N$中节点，$\omega$中叶关系的大小为$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$。通过$\tau \left( {{\omega }_{X},\mathcal{F}}\right)$返回的树结构中的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\max \{ 1,1 + \left( {\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon \} }\right)$时间内完成实例化。

Proof. For simplicity, we set

证明. 为简化推导，设定

$$
m = \max \{ 1,1 + \left( {\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon \} .
$$

The proof is by induction on the structure of ${\omega }_{X}$ .

该证明基于${\omega }_{X}$结构的归纳法。

Base case: Assume that ${\omega }_{X}$ is a single atom $R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$ . In this case,the procedure $\tau$ returns this atom (Line 1). The atom can obviously be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time. It holds $\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  = 0$ ,since ${\omega }_{X}$ does not contain any node which is a variable. This means that $m = 1$ . Then,the statement in the lemma holds for the base case.

基本情况：假设${\omega }_{X}$是单个原子$R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$。此时，过程$\tau$返回该原子（第1行）。显然该原子可在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内物化。由于${\omega }_{X}$不包含任何变量节点，故满足$\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  = 0$。这意味着$m = 1$。因此，引理中的陈述对基本情况成立。

Inductive step: Assume that $X$ is a variable with children nodes ${X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{k}$ . Let keys $= \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\} ,{\mathcal{F}}_{X} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \left( {\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right) }\right)$ ,and ${Q}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)  =$ join of atoms $\left( \omega \right)$ . Following the control flow in $\tau \left( {{\omega }_{X},\mathcal{F}}\right)$ ,we make a case distinction.

归纳步骤：假设$X$是具有子节点${X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{k}$的变量。设键值$= \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\} ,{\mathcal{F}}_{X} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \left( {\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right) }\right)$与原子$\left( \omega \right)$进行${Q}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)  =$连接。根据$\tau \left( {{\omega }_{X},\mathcal{F}}\right)$中的控制流，我们进行情形划分。

Case 1: mode $=$ ’static’ $\land  {Q}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$ is free-connex or mode $=$ ’dynamic’ $\land  {Q}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$ is ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical (Lines 5-7):

情形1：模式$=$为'静态'$\land  {Q}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$且满足自由连接，或模式$=$为'动态'$\land  {Q}_{X}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{X}\right)$且满足${\delta }_{0}$层次性（第5-7行）：

The procedure $\tau$ returns the view tree BUILDVT("V", ${\omega }_{X},{\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ ) (Line 7). Since ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical queries are in particular free-connex,it follows from Lemma C. 4 that BUILDVT("V", ${\omega }_{X},{\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ ) can be materialized in time $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ . This completes the inductive step for Case 1.

过程$\tau$返回视图树BUILDVT("V",${\omega }_{X},{\mathcal{F}}_{X}$)（第7行）。由于${\delta }_{0}$层次性查询特别属于自由连接类，根据引理C.4可知BUILDVT("V",${\omega }_{X},{\mathcal{F}}_{X}$)可在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内物化。至此完成情形1的归纳步骤。

Case 2: Case 1 does not hold and $X \in  \mathcal{F}$ (Lines 8-11):

情形2：情形1不成立且$X \in  \mathcal{F}$（第8-11行）：

The set of view trees $\tau \left( {{\omega }_{X},\mathcal{F}}\right)$ is defined as follows: for each set ${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ with ${T}_{i} \in$ $\tau \left( {{\omega }_{{X}_{i}},\mathcal{F}}\right)$ ,the set $\tau \left( {{\omega }_{X},\mathcal{F}}\right)$ contains the view tree NEWVT $\left( {\cdot ,\text{keys},{\left\{  {\widehat{T}}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\right)$ ,where ${\widehat{T}}_{i} =$ AuxVIEW $\left( {{X}_{i},{T}_{i}}\right)$ for each $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . We consider one such set ${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ of view trees. By induction hypothesis,the views in each ${T}_{i}$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\max \left\{  {1,1 + \left( {\xi \left( {\omega ,{X}_{i},\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right\}  }\right)$ time. It follows from Inequality (C.3), that the overall time to materialize the views in these view trees is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{m}\right)$ . By Lemma C.3,the views in each view tree ${\widehat{T}}_{i}$ with $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{m}\right)$ time. Let ${V}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$ be the root view of ${\widehat{T}}_{i}$ ,for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . It follows from Observation C.1.(1) that keys is included in each ${\mathcal{F}}_{i}$ and the query ${V}_{X}\left( \text{keys}\right)  =$ ${V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}\right)$ is ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical. Hence,it follows from Lemma C. 2 that the views in the view tree $\operatorname{NEWVT}\left( {\cdot ,\text{ keys },{\left\{  {\widehat{T}}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\right)$ can be materialized in time $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{m}\right)$ . This completes the inductive step in this case.

视图树集合$\tau \left( {{\omega }_{X},\mathcal{F}}\right)$定义如下：对于每个具有${T}_{i} \in$$\tau \left( {{\omega }_{{X}_{i}},\mathcal{F}}\right)$的集合${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$，该集合$\tau \left( {{\omega }_{X},\mathcal{F}}\right)$包含视图树NEWVT$\left( {\cdot ,\text{keys},{\left\{  {\widehat{T}}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\right)$，其中${\widehat{T}}_{i} =$AuxVIEW$\left( {{X}_{i},{T}_{i}}\right)$对应于每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$。我们考虑其中一个这样的视图树集合${\left\{  {T}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$。根据归纳假设，每个${T}_{i}$中的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\max \left\{  {1,1 + \left( {\xi \left( {\omega ,{X}_{i},\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right\}  }\right)$时间内物化。由不等式(C.3)可得，物化这些视图树中所有视图的总时间为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{m}\right)$。根据引理C.3，对于每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$，视图树${\widehat{T}}_{i}$中的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{m}\right)$时间内物化。设${V}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{i}\right)$为${\widehat{T}}_{i}$的根视图，其中$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$。由观察结论C.1.(1)可知，键值被包含在每个${\mathcal{F}}_{i}$中，且查询${V}_{X}\left( \text{keys}\right)  =$${V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{F}}_{k}\right)$是${\delta }_{0}$层次化的。因此根据引理C.2，视图树$\operatorname{NEWVT}\left( {\cdot ,\text{ keys },{\left\{  {\widehat{T}}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}\right)$中的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{m}\right)$时间内物化。至此完成该情况下的归纳步骤。

Case 3: Case 1 does not hold and $X \notin  \mathcal{F}$ (Lines 12-17):

情况3：若情况1不成立且$X \notin  \mathcal{F}$（第12-17行）：

The procedure $\tau$ first calls INDICATORVTS $\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$ (Line 12) given in Figure 10,which constructs the indicator view trees alltree, ltree, and htree. By Lemma C. 6, the views in these view trees can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time. Let ${H}_{X}$ be the root of htree. The only difference between the construction of the view trees returned in Case 2 above and the view trees in the set htrees defined in Lines 13-15 is that the roots of the view trees in the latter set have $\exists {H}_{X}$ as additional child view. By the same argumentation as in Case 2,it follows that the views in htrees can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{m}\right)$ time. Let ltree $=$ BUILDVT $\left( {\text{"V",}{\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }},{\mathcal{F}}_{X}}\right)$ as defined in Line 16,where ${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$ shares the same structure as ${\omega }_{X}$ ,but each atom $R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$ is replaced with ${R}^{\text{keys }}\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$ denoting the light part of relation $R$ partitioned on keys. It follows from Lemma C. 5 that the views in the view tree ltree can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{m}\right)$ time. Thus,all views of the views trees in the set ${htrees} \cup  \{ {ltree}\}$ can be materialized in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{m}\right)$ time. This completes the inductive step for Case 3.

该过程$\tau$首先调用图10中给出的INDICATORVTS$\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$（第12行），该函数构建了指示符视图树alltree、ltree和htree。根据引理C.6，这些视图树中的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内物化。令${H}_{X}$为htree的根节点。上述案例2返回的视图树构造与第13-15行定义的htrees集合中视图树的唯一区别在于：后者集合中视图树的根节点额外拥有$\exists {H}_{X}$作为子视图。通过案例2的相同论证可推知，htrees中的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{m}\right)$时间内物化。令ltree$=$BUILDVT$\left( {\text{"V",}{\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }},{\mathcal{F}}_{X}}\right)$如第16行定义，其中${\omega }_{X}^{\text{keys }}$与${\omega }_{X}$结构相同，但每个原子$R\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$被替换为表示关系$R$按键分区后轻量部分的${R}^{\text{keys }}\left( \mathcal{X}\right)$。根据引理C.5可推知，视图树ltree中的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{m}\right)$时间内物化。因此，集合${htrees} \cup  \{ {ltree}\}$中所有视图树的视图均可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{m}\right)$时间内物化。至此完成案例3的归纳步骤。

Using Lemma C.7,we prove Proposition 5.4. Without loss of generality,assume that $\omega$ consists of a single connected component. Otherwise, we apply the same reasoning for each connected component. We also assume that $Q$ contains at least one atom with non-empty schema. Otherwise, $\tau \left( {\omega ,\varnothing }\right)$ returns a single atom with empty schema,which can obviously be materialized in constant time.

运用引理C.7，我们证明命题5.4。不失一般性，假设$\omega$由单个连通分量组成。否则，我们对每个连通分量应用相同推理。同时假设$Q$至少包含一个具有非空模式的原子。否则，$\tau \left( {\omega ,\varnothing }\right)$将返回一个模式为空的单原子，显然该原子可在常数时间内物化。

By Lemma C.7,the view trees generated by $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ can be materialized in time $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\max \{ 1,1 + \left( {\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon \} }\right)$ ,where $X$ is the root variable of $\omega$ . It remains to show:

根据引理C.7，$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$生成的视图树可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\max \{ 1,1 + \left( {\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon \} }\right)$时间内物化，其中$X$是$\omega$的根变量。仍需证明：

$$
\max \{ 1,1 + \left( {\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon \}  \leq  1 + \left( {\mathrm{w} - 1}\right) \epsilon . \tag{C.5}
$$

First,assume that $\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  = 0$ . This means that $\max \{ 1,1 + \left( {\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon \}  = 1$ . Since $Q$ contains at least one atom with non-empty schema,we have $\mathrm{w} \geq  1$ . Thus,Inequality (C.5) holds. Now,let $\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  = \ell  \geq  1$ . We show that $\mathrm{w} \geq  \ell$ . It follows from $\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  = \ell$ that $\omega$ contains a bound variable $Y$ such that ${\rho }_{Q}^{ * }\left( B\right)  = \ell$ ,where $B = \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{Y}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{F}$ . The inner nodes of each root-to-leaf path of a canonical variable order are the variables of an atom. Hence,for each variable $Z \in  B$ ,there must be an atom in $Q$ that contains both $Y$ and $Z$ . This means that $Y$ and $Z$ depend on each other. Let ${\omega }^{\prime } = \left( {T,{de}{p}_{{\omega }^{\prime }}}\right)$ be an arbitrary free-top variable order for $Q$ . Since all variables in $B$ depend on $Y$ ,each of them must be on a root-to-leaf path with $Y$ . Since $Y$ is bound and the variables in $B$ are free,the set $B$ must be included in anc(Y). Hence, $B \subseteq  {de}{p}_{{\omega }^{\prime }}\left( Y\right)$ . This means ${\rho }_{Q}^{ * }\left( {\{ Y\}  \cup  {de}{p}_{{\omega }^{\prime }}\left( Y\right) }\right)  \geq  \ell$ , which implies $\mathrm{w}\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right)  \geq  \ell$ . It follows $\mathrm{w} \geq  \ell$ .

首先，假设$\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  = 0$。这意味着$\max \{ 1,1 + \left( {\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  - 1}\right) \epsilon \}  = 1$。由于$Q$至少包含一个具有非空模式的原子，因此有$\mathrm{w} \geq  1$。因此，不等式(C.5)成立。现在，设$\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  = \ell  \geq  1$。我们将证明$\mathrm{w} \geq  \ell$。由$\xi \left( {\omega ,X,\mathcal{F}}\right)  = \ell$可知，$\omega$包含一个约束变量$Y$，使得${\rho }_{Q}^{ * }\left( B\right)  = \ell$，其中$B = \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{Y}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{F}$。规范变量顺序中每条根到叶路径的内部节点都是某个原子的变量。因此，对于每个变量$Z \in  B$，在$Q$中必然存在一个同时包含$Y$和$Z$的原子。这意味着$Y$和$Z$相互依赖。设${\omega }^{\prime } = \left( {T,{de}{p}_{{\omega }^{\prime }}}\right)$为$Q$的任意自由顶变量顺序。由于$B$中所有变量都依赖于$Y$，它们必须与$Y$位于同一条根到叶路径上。由于$Y$是约束变量而$B$中的变量是自由变量，集合$B$必须包含在anc(Y)中。因此，$B \subseteq  {de}{p}_{{\omega }^{\prime }}\left( Y\right)$。这意味着${\rho }_{Q}^{ * }\left( {\{ Y\}  \cup  {de}{p}_{{\omega }^{\prime }}\left( Y\right) }\right)  \geq  \ell$，从而推出$\mathrm{w}\left( {\omega }^{\prime }\right)  \geq  \ell$。由此可得$\mathrm{w} \geq  \ell$。

## Appendix D. Proofs of the Results in Section 6

## 附录D. 第6节结果证明

Proposition 6.1. The tuples in the result of a hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ over a database of size $N$ can be enumerated with $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ delay using the view trees constructed by $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ for a canonical variable order $\omega$ for $Q$ .

命题6.1. 对于规模为$N$的数据库，分层查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$的结果元组可以通过$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$为规范变量顺序$\omega$构建的视图树，以$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$延迟进行枚举。

Following Proposition 5.3, the union of queries defined by the set of view trees constructed by $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ is equivalent $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ . We enumerate the tuples over $\mathcal{F}$ from this set of view trees using the next calls of these trees in the set.

根据命题5.3，由$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$构建的视图树集合所定义的查询并集等价于$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$。我们通过该视图树集合中这些树的next调用，枚举$\mathcal{F}$上的元组。

We first discuss the case of one view tree. In case there are no indicator views, then the view tree consisting of a hierarchy of views admits constant delay [OZ15]. In the static case, this holds for free-connex hierarchical queries; in the dynamic case, this holds for ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical queries (Section 5.1).

我们首先讨论单个视图树的情况。若不存在指示视图，则由视图层次结构组成的视图树允许常数延迟[OZ15]。静态情况下，这适用于自由连接分层查询；动态情况下，则适用于${\delta }_{0}$-分层查询（第5.1节）。

The view subtrees constructed over the light parts of input relations only do not bring additional difficulty. By construction (Section 5),the root view $V$ of such a subtree $T$ contains all the free variables that are present in $T$ . In this case,the open and next calls stop at $V$ and do not explore the children of $V$ . This means that for enumeration purposes, we can discard the descendants of $V$ .

仅基于输入关系轻量部分构建的视图子树不会带来额外困难。根据第5节的构造，此类子树$T$的根视图$V$包含$T$中存在的所有自由变量。此时，open和next调用在$V$处停止，不会探索$V$的子节点。这意味着出于枚举目的，我们可以丢弃$V$的后代节点。

By grounding the heavy indicators in $T$ ,we obtain instances of $T$ that may represent overlapping relations. We next analyze the enumeration delay in the presence of heavy indicators as a function of the view tree instances of a view tree created for $Q$ .

通过将重型指示器锚定在$T$中，我们获得了可能表征重叠关系的$T$实例。随后，我们将分析在存在重型指示器的情况下，枚举延迟如何随为$Q$创建的视图树实例而变化。

Consider one heavy indicator. Since its size is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ ,it may lead to that many view tree instances. From each instance, we can enumerate with constant delay, and we can also look up a tuple with schema $\mathcal{S}$ in constant time. Given there are $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ such tuples,we can enumerate from $T$ with $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ delay.

考察单个重型指示器。由于其尺寸为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$，可能导致生成等量视图树实例。每个实例均可实现恒定延迟枚举，并能在恒定时间内查询模式为$\mathcal{S}$的元组。假设存在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$个此类元组，我们便能以$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$延迟从$T$进行枚举。

Consider $p$ heavy indicators $\exists {H}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{X}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,\exists {H}_{p}\left( {\mathcal{X}}_{p}\right)$ whose parents ${V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{X}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{p}\left( {\mathcal{X}}_{p}\right)$ are along the same path in the view tree. Let us assume ${V}_{i}$ is an ancestor of ${V}_{j}$ for $i < j$ . By construction, there is a total strict inclusion order on their sets of variables, with the indicator above having less variables than at a lower depth: ${\mathcal{X}}_{1} \subset  \cdots  \subset  {\mathcal{X}}_{p}$ . Each indicator draws its tuples from the input relations whose schemas include that of the indicator. There is also an inclusion between the parent views: ${V}_{i} \subseteq  {\pi }_{{\mathcal{X}}_{i}}{V}_{i + 1},\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  {p - 1}\right\rbrack$ . This holds since ${V}_{i}$ is defined by the join of the leaves underneath,so the view ${V}_{j}$ that is a descendant of ${V}_{i}$ is used to define ${V}_{i}$ in joins with other views or relations. The size of ${V}_{i}$ is at most that of $\exists {H}_{i}$ since they both have the same schema and the former is defined by the join of the latter with other views. Since the size of $\exists {H}_{i}$ is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ ,it follows that the size of ${V}_{i}$ is also $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ . When grounding $\exists {H}_{i}$ ,we create an instance for each tuple $t$ that is in both $\exists {H}_{i}$ and ${V}_{i}$ : If $t$ were not in ${V}_{i}$ ,then there would be at least one sibling of $\exists {H}_{i}$ that does not have it. When opening the descendants of ${V}_{i}$ before enumeration,only these tuples in ${V}_{i}$ that also occur in $\exists {H}_{i}$ and in all its siblings can be extended at the descendant views,including all views ${V}_{j}$ for $j > i$ . The overall number of groundings for the $h$ heavy indicators is therefore $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ . Let ${n}_{i}$ be the number of instances of $\exists {H}_{i}$ . Then,the delay for enumerating from the union of $\exists {H}_{i}$ instances is $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \leq  j \leq  p}}{n}_{j}$ using the UNION algorithm, which also accounts for the delay incurred for enumeration from unions at instances of all $\exists {H}_{j}$ that are descendants of $\exists {H}_{i}$ . The overall delay is that for the union of instances for $\exists {H}_{1} : \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{1 \leq  j \leq  p}}{n}_{j} = \mathcal{O}\left( {p \times  {N}^{1 - \epsilon }}\right)  = \mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right) .$

考虑$p$那些在视图树中沿相同路径拥有父节点${V}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{X}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{V}_{p}\left( {\mathcal{X}}_{p}\right)$的heavy indicators（重度指标）$\exists {H}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{X}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,\exists {H}_{p}\left( {\mathcal{X}}_{p}\right)$。假设${V}_{i}$是$i < j$中${V}_{j}$的祖先节点。根据构造原理，它们的变量集之间存在严格的全包含序，位于上层的指标所含变量少于深层指标：${\mathcal{X}}_{1} \subset  \cdots  \subset  {\mathcal{X}}_{p}$。每个指标从其模式包含该指标模式的输入关系中抽取元组。父视图之间也存在包含关系：${V}_{i} \subseteq  {\pi }_{{\mathcal{X}}_{i}}{V}_{i + 1},\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  {p - 1}\right\rbrack$。这是因为${V}_{i}$由其下方叶节点的连接定义，因此作为${V}_{i}$后代的视图${V}_{j}$被用于在与其他视图或关系的连接中定义${V}_{i}$。${V}_{i}$的规模至多与$\exists {H}_{i}$相同，因二者模式相同且前者由后者与其他视图连接定义。既然$\exists {H}_{i}$的规模为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$，则${V}_{i}$的规模同样为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$。在实例化$\exists {H}_{i}$时，我们为每个同时存在于$\exists {H}_{i}$和${V}_{i}$的元组$t$创建实例：若$t$不在${V}_{i}$中，则至少存在一个$\exists {H}_{i}$的兄弟节点不包含该元组。在枚举前展开${V}_{i}$的后代时，只有那些同时出现在$\exists {H}_{i}$及其所有兄弟节点中的${V}_{i}$元组才能在后代视图（包括$j > i$对应的所有${V}_{j}$视图）中被扩展。因此$h$个heavy indicators的总实例化数为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$。设${n}_{i}$为$\exists {H}_{i}$的实例数量，则通过UNION算法从$\exists {H}_{i}$实例并集枚举的延迟为$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \leq  j \leq  p}}{n}_{j}$，该算法同时涵盖了从$\exists {H}_{i}$所有后代节点$\exists {H}_{j}$实例的并集枚举所产生的延迟。总体延迟即为$\exists {H}_{1} : \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{1 \leq  j \leq  p}}{n}_{j} = \mathcal{O}\left( {p \times  {N}^{1 - \epsilon }}\right)  = \mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right) .$实例并集对应的延迟。

Consider again the $p$ heavy indicators,but this time their parents ${V}_{1},\ldots ,{V}_{p}$ are not all along the same path in the view tree. Each path is treated as in the previous case. We distinguish two cases. In the first case,there is no parent ${V}_{i}$ that is an ancestor of several other parents in our list. Let $W$ be a common ancestor of several parents. Then, the enumeration algorithm uses each tuple of $W$ (possibly extended by descendant views) as context for the instances of these parents. A next tuple is produced in sequence at each of these parents over their corresponding schemas. These tuples are then composed into a larger tuple over a larger schema at their common ancestor using the PRODUCT algorithm. The number of branches is bounded by the number of atoms in the query, which means that the overall delay remains $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ . In the second case,a parent ${V}_{i}$ is a common ancestor of several other parents in our list. We reason similarly to the one-path case and obtain that the overall delay is $\mathcal{O}\left( {p \times  {N}^{1 - \epsilon }}\right)  = \mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ .

再次考虑$p$这些权重指标，但这次它们的父节点${V}_{1},\ldots ,{V}_{p}$在视图树中并非全部位于同一路径。每条路径的处理方式与先前情况相同。我们区分两种情形：第一种情形中，列表内不存在作为多个其他父节点共同祖先的父节点${V}_{i}$。设$W$为若干父节点的共同祖先，枚举算法将每个$W$的元组（可能通过后代视图扩展）作为这些父节点实例的上下文。随后在每个父节点上按对应模式依次生成下一个元组，并通过PRODUCT算法在共同祖先处将这些元组组合成更大模式下的复合元组。分支数量受限于查询中的原子数，这意味着总体延迟保持$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$。第二种情形中，某个父节点${V}_{i}$是列表内多个其他父节点的共同祖先，其推理过程与单路径情形类似，可得出总体延迟为$\mathcal{O}\left( {p \times  {N}^{1 - \epsilon }}\right)  = \mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$。

So far we discussed the case of enumerating from one view tree. In case of a set of view trees we use the UNION algorithm to enumerate the distinct tuples. In case the query has several connected components, i.e., it is a Cartesian product of hierarchical queries, we use the Product algorithm.

目前我们讨论了单个视图树的枚举情形。对于视图树集合，采用UNION算法枚举不同元组。若查询包含多个连通分量（即层次查询的笛卡尔积），则使用Product算法处理。

Appendix E. Proofs of the Results in Section 7

附录E. 第7节结论的证明

### E.1. Proof of Proposition 7.2.

### E.1. 命题7.2的证明

Proposition 7.2. Given a hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ with dynamic width $\delta$ ,a canonical variable order $\omega$ for $Q$ ,a database of size $N$ ,and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,maintaining the views in the set of view trees $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ under a single-tuple update to any input relation takes $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ time.

命题7.2. 给定具有动态宽度$\delta$的层次查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$、规范变量序$\omega$、规模为$N$的数据库及$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，在任意输入关系单次元组更新时，维护视图树集合$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$中的视图耗时$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$。

We first give the maintenance time for the views constructed by BUILDVT given a ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical query (Lemma E.1). We then show the maintenance time for the views constructed by $\tau$ given a hierarchical query (Lemma E.2). The maintenance time uses a new measure, which we relate to dynamic width (Lemma E.3). We finally show the running times of UPDATEINDTREE and UPDATETREES.

我们首先给出BUILDVT对${\delta }_{0}$层次查询所构建视图的维护时间（引理E.1），接着展示$\tau$对层次查询构建视图的维护时间（引理E.2）。该维护时间采用新度量标准，我们将其与动态宽度关联（引理E.3），最后给出UPDATEINDTREE和UPDATETREES的运行时间。

Lemma E.1. Given a ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ ,a canonical variable order $\omega$ for $Q$ ,and a database of size $N$ ,the views constructed by $\operatorname{BUILDVT}\left( {\cdot ,\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ from Figure 6 in the dynamic mode can be maintained under a single-tuple update to any input relation in $\mathcal{O}\left( 1\right)$ time.

引理E.1. 给定${\delta }_{0}$层次查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$、规范变量序$\omega$及规模为$N$的数据库，动态模式下图6中$\operatorname{BUILDVT}\left( {\cdot ,\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$构建的视图可在任意输入关系单次元组更新时，以$\mathcal{O}\left( 1\right)$时间维护。

Proof. At each node $X$ of a canonical variable order $\omega$ for a ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical query,the set ${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$ of free variables is either $\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$ if $X$ is free,or $\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)$ if $X$ is bound because the set $\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$ of free variables in ${\omega }_{X}$ is empty for ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical queries. The functions AUXVIEW and NEWVT maintain the following invariant for ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical queries in the dynamic mode: If $X$ has a sibling node in $\omega$ ,then the view created at node $X$ has anc(X) as free variables. If $X$ is bound,then already $\mathcal{F} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)$ ; otherwise,AuxVIEW constructs an extra view with anc(X)as free variables.

证明。在规范变量序$\omega$的每个节点$X$上，对于${\delta }_{0}$层次化查询而言，自由变量集合${\mathcal{F}}_{X}$要么是$\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$（当$X$为自由变量时），要么是$\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)$（当$X$为绑定变量时），因为${\delta }_{0}$层次化查询中${\omega }_{X}$的自由变量集合$\mathcal{F} \cap  \operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$为空。在动态模式下，函数AUXVIEW和NEWVT为${\delta }_{0}$层次化查询维护以下不变性：若$X$在$\omega$中存在兄弟节点，则在该节点创建的视图以anc(X)作为自由变量；若$X$已被绑定，则已满足$\mathcal{F} = \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)$；否则AuxVIEW会额外构建以anc(X)为自由变量的视图。

Now consider an update ${\delta R}$ to a relation $R$ . Due to the hierarchical property of the input query,the update ${\delta R}$ fixes the values of all variables on the path from the leaf $R$ to the root to constants. While propagating an update through the view tree, the delta at each node $X$ requires joining with the views constructed for the siblings of $X$ . Each of the sibling views has $\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)$ as free variables,as discussed above. Thus,computing the delta at each node makes only constant-time lookups in the sibling views. Overall, propagating the update through the view tree constructed for a ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical query using BUILDVT takes constant time.

现考虑对关系$R$的更新${\delta R}$。由于输入查询的层次化特性，该更新将路径上从叶节点$R$到根节点的所有变量值固定为常量。在视图树中传播更新时，每个节点$X$处的增量都需要与为该节点兄弟视图进行连接运算。如前所述，这些兄弟视图均以$\operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)$作为自由变量。因此，计算每个节点的增量仅需在兄弟视图中进行常数时间查找。总体而言，使用BUILDVT为${\delta }_{0}$层次化查询构建的视图树传播更新仅需常数时间。

Consider now a canonical variable order $\omega$ for a hierarchical query and a set $\mathcal{F}$ of free variables. Given a node $X$ in $\omega$ ,let ${Q}_{X}$ denote the join of atoms $\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$ . We define $\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ as:

现考察层次化查询的规范变量序$\omega$及其自由变量集合$\mathcal{F}$。给定$\omega$中的节点$X$，令${Q}_{X}$表示原子式$\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)$的连接积。我们将$\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$定义为：

$$
\mathop{\max }\limits_{{X \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( \omega \right)  - \mathcal{F}}}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right) }}\left\{  {{\rho }_{{Q}_{X}}^{ * }\left( {\left( {\operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{F}}\right)  - \mathcal{Y}}\right) }\right\}  ,
$$

The measure $\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ is the maximal fractional edge cover number of ${Q}_{X}$ over the free variables occurring in the subtree ${\omega }_{X}$ of $\omega$ rooted at a bound variable $X$ ,when the variables of one atom $R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$ in ${\omega }_{X}$ are excluded.

测度$\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$是指：当排除${\omega }_{X}$中某个原子式$R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$的变量时，在绑定变量$X$为根的子树${\omega }_{X}$内，自由变量集合${Q}_{X}$的最大分数边覆盖数。

Lemma E.2. Given a hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ ,a canonical variable order $\omega$ for $Q$ ,a database of size $N$ ,and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,the views constructed by $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ from Figure 11 in the dynamic mode can be maintained under a single-tuple update in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ time.

引理E.2。给定层次化查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$、其规范变量序$\omega$、规模为$N$的数据库及$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，图11中$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$在动态模式下构建的视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right) \epsilon }\right)$时间内响应单元组更新。

Proof. If $Q$ is ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical,the function $\tau$ returns a view tree for $Q$ that admits $\mathcal{O}\left( 1\right)$ update time, per Lemma E.1.

证明。若$Q$是${\delta }_{0}$层次化的，根据引理E.1，函数$\tau$返回的视图树可支持$\mathcal{O}\left( 1\right)$时间复杂度的更新。

Consider now a view tree created by $\tau$ for a non- ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical query. Let us restrict this view tree such that the views created in the light case are treated as leaf views. This restricted view tree encodes the result of a ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical query! As the procedure $\tau$ traverses the variable order in a top-down manner,every bound variable $X$ with a free variable below is replaced by a set of view trees where $X$ is free (heavy case) and by a view tree whose root view aggregates away $X$ and includes only free variables (light case). Thus,single-tuple updates to the leaves of this restricted view tree take constant time. That is, updates to the relations that are not part of the views materialized in the light case are constant.

现在考虑由$\tau$为非${\delta }_{0}$层次化查询创建的视图树。我们将该视图树限制为：在轻量情况下创建的视图被视为叶视图。这个受限视图树编码了${\delta }_{0}$层次化查询的结果！由于过程$\tau$以自顶向下方式遍历变量顺序，每个带有下方自由变量的绑定变量$X$会被两种情况替换：一组$X$为自由变量的视图树（重量情况），以及一个根视图聚合掉$X$且仅包含自由变量的视图树（轻量情况）。因此，对此受限视图树叶子的单元组更新只需常数时间。也就是说，对不属于轻量情况下物化视图的关系的更新是常数时间的。

However, updates to the relations that are part of the views materialized in the light case might not be constant. The view tree ltree constructed by BUILDVT at a bound variable $X$ is defined over the light parts of relations partitioned on keys $= \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$ (Line 16 in Figure 11). Each view ${V}_{Z}$ in ltree constructed at a variable $Z$ includes all the free variables in ${\omega }_{Z}$ . A single-tuple update ${\delta R}$ to any relation $R$ in ltree fixes the values of the variables keys,thus reducing the size of other relations in ltree to $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$ . The maintenance cost for ${V}_{Z}$ under the update ${\delta R}$ with schema $\mathcal{Y}$ is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{Z}\epsilon }\right)$ ,where ${m}_{Z} = {\rho }_{{Q}_{Z}}^{ * }\left( {\left( {\operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{Z}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{F}}\right)  - \mathcal{Y}}\right)$ . The maintenance cost for ltree is dominated by the maintenance cost for its root ${V}_{X}$ .

然而，对属于轻量情况下物化视图的关系的更新可能不是常数时间的。BUILDVT在绑定变量$X$处构建的视图树ltree是基于按键$= \operatorname{anc}\left( X\right)  \cup  \{ X\}$分区关系的轻量部分定义的（图11第16行）。在变量$Z$处构建的ltree中每个视图${V}_{Z}$都包含${\omega }_{Z}$中所有自由变量。对ltree中任何关系$R$的单元组更新${\delta R}$会固定键变量的值，从而将ltree中其他关系的大小缩减至$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\epsilon }\right)$。在模式$\mathcal{Y}$的更新${\delta R}$下，视图${V}_{Z}$的维护成本为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{Z}\epsilon }\right)$，其中${m}_{Z} = {\rho }_{{Q}_{Z}}^{ * }\left( {\left( {\operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{Z}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{F}}\right)  - \mathcal{Y}}\right)$。ltree的维护成本主要取决于其根节点${V}_{X}$的维护成本。

The change computed at ${V}_{X}$ for the single-tuple update consist of $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}\epsilon }\right)$ tuples and needs to be propagated further up in the tree. Because there are no further light cases on the path from $X$ to the root,the propagation cost is constant per tuple. The overall time needed to maintain ${V}_{X}$ and propagate the change at ${V}_{X}$ up to the root is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}^{\prime }\epsilon }\right)$ ,where ${m}_{X}^{\prime } = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right) }}\left\{  {{\rho }_{{Q}_{X}}^{ * }\left( {\left( {\operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{F}}\right)  - \mathcal{Y}}\right) }\right\}$ . In the worst case,the root variable of $\omega$ is bound; then,maintaining the root view and its descendants takes $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ time.

为单元组更新在${V}_{X}$处计算的变更包含$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}\epsilon }\right)$个元组，需要向树根方向继续传播。由于从$X$到根节点的路径上不存在更多轻量情况，每个元组的传播成本是常数级的。维护${V}_{X}$并将变更传播至根节点所需的总时间为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{{m}_{X}^{\prime }\epsilon }\right)$，其中${m}_{X}^{\prime } = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right) }}\left\{  {{\rho }_{{Q}_{X}}^{ * }\left( {\left( {\operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{F}}\right)  - \mathcal{Y}}\right) }\right\}$。最坏情况下，$\omega$的根变量是绑定的；此时维护根视图及其派生视图需要$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right) \epsilon }\right)$时间。

The views constructed by $\tau$ in the light cases thus determine the overall maintenance $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ time.

因此，$\tau$在轻量情况下构建的视图决定了整体维护$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right) \epsilon }\right)$时间。

We next relate the measure $\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ to dynamic width.

接下来我们将度量$\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$与动态宽度相关联。

Lemma E.3. Given a canonical variable order $\omega$ for a hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ with dynamic width $\delta$ ,it holds that $\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)  \leq  \delta$ .

引理E.3. 给定具有动态宽度$\delta$的层次化查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$的规范变量序$\omega$，则成立$\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)  \leq  \delta$。

Proof. Given any variable order ${\omega }^{\prime }$ for $Q$ and a variable $X$ in ${\omega }^{\prime }$ ,we denote by ${Q}_{X}^{{\omega }^{\prime }}$ the query that joins the atoms in atoms $\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\prime }\right)$ . To prove $\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)  \leq  \delta$ ,we need to show that

证明。给定$Q$的任意变量序${\omega }^{\prime }$及${\omega }^{\prime }$中的变量$X$，我们用${Q}_{X}^{{\omega }^{\prime }}$表示连接原子集$\left( {\omega }_{X}^{\prime }\right)$中原子的查询。为证明$\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)  \leq  \delta$，需证明

$$
\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)  \leq  \delta \left( {\omega }^{f}\right)  \tag{E.1}
$$

for any free-top variable order ${\omega }^{f}$ for $Q$ . It follows from the definition of $\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ that $\omega$ has a bound variable $X$ and an atom $R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( {Q}_{X}^{\omega }\right)$ such that

对于$Q$的任何自由顶变量序${\omega }^{f}$。根据$\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$的定义可知，$\omega$存在一个约束变量$X$和一个原子$R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( {Q}_{X}^{\omega }\right)$使得

$$
\kappa \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)  = {\rho }_{{Q}_{X}^{\omega }}^{ * }\left( \mathcal{B}\right) 
$$

where $\mathcal{B} = \left( {\operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{F}}\right)  - \mathcal{Y}$ . Since $\omega$ is canonical,it holds:

其中$\mathcal{B} = \left( {\operatorname{vars}\left( {\omega }_{X}\right)  \cap  \mathcal{F}}\right)  - \mathcal{Y}$。由于$\omega$是规范的，故有：

$\left( *\right)$ Each atom in $Q$ containing a variable from $\mathcal{B}$ must contain $X$ .

$\left( *\right)$ $Q$中包含$\mathcal{B}$变量的每个原子都必须包含$X$。

Let ${\omega }^{f} = \left( {T,{de}{p}_{{\omega }^{f}}}\right)$ be a free-top variable order for $Q$ . Property (*) implies that the variables in $\mathcal{B}$ depend on $X$ . Since $X$ is bound and all variables in $\mathcal{B}$ are free,the latter variables cannot be below $X$ in ${\omega }^{f}$ . Hence, $\mathcal{B} \subseteq  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }^{f}}\left( X\right)$ . Since $R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$ contains $X$ ,it must be included in atoms $\left( {\omega }_{X}^{f}\right)$ . To prove Inequality (E.1),it thus suffices to show:

设${\omega }^{f} = \left( {T,{de}{p}_{{\omega }^{f}}}\right)$为$Q$的自由顶变量序。性质(*)表明$\mathcal{B}$中的变量依赖于$X$。由于$X$是约束变量且$\mathcal{B}$中所有变量都是自由的，后者在${\omega }^{f}$中不可能位于$X$之下。因此$\mathcal{B} \subseteq  {\operatorname{dep}}_{{\omega }^{f}}\left( X\right)$。因$R\left( \mathcal{Y}\right)$包含$X$，它必须被包含在原子集$\left( {\omega }_{X}^{f}\right)$中。为证明不等式(E.1)，只需证明：

$$
{\rho }_{Q}^{ * }\left( \mathcal{B}\right)  \geq  {\rho }_{{Q}_{X}^{\omega }}^{ * }\left( \mathcal{B}\right)  \tag{E.2}
$$

By Property $\left( *\right)$ ,each atom in $Q$ covering a variable from $\mathcal{B}$ contains $X$ . Hence,all such atoms are contained in atoms $\left( {Q}_{X}^{\omega }\right)$ . This implies that any fractional edge cover ${\mathbf{\lambda }}^{\prime }$ of $\mathcal{B}$ using atoms in $Q$ can be turned into a fractional edge cover $\mathbf{\lambda }$ of $B$ using atoms in ${Q}_{X}^{\omega }$ such that $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\lambda  \in  \lambda }}\lambda  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{\lambda }^{\prime } \in  {\lambda }^{\prime }}}{\lambda }^{\prime }$ . This implies Inequality (E.2) and hence Inequality (E.1).

根据性质$\left( *\right)$，$Q$中覆盖$\mathcal{B}$变量的每个原子都包含$X$。因此所有这些原子都包含在原子集$\left( {Q}_{X}^{\omega }\right)$中。这意味着使用$Q$中原子的$\mathcal{B}$的任意分数边覆盖${\mathbf{\lambda }}^{\prime }$，都可转化为使用${Q}_{X}^{\omega }$中原子的$B$的分数边覆盖$\mathbf{\lambda }$，使得$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\lambda  \in  \lambda }}\lambda  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{\lambda }^{\prime } \in  {\lambda }^{\prime }}}{\lambda }^{\prime }$。由此可得不等式(E.2)，进而得不等式(E.1)。

Lemma E.4. Given an indicator tree ${T}_{\text{Ind }}$ constructed by INDICATORVTS from Figure 10 and a single-tuple update ${\delta R}$ ,UPDATEINDTREE from Figure 18 runs in $\mathcal{O}\left( 1\right)$ time.

引理E.4。给定由图10中INDICATORVTS构造的指示树${T}_{\text{Ind }}$和单元组更新${\delta R}$，图18中的UPDATEINDTREE算法在$\mathcal{O}\left( 1\right)$时间内运行。

Proof. The tree ${T}_{\text{Ind }}$ encodes the result of a ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical query and admits constant-time updates per Lemma E.1. The remaining operations in UPDATEINDTREE also take constant time.

证明。树${T}_{\text{Ind }}$编码了${\delta }_{0}$层次查询的结果，根据引理E.1允许常量时间更新。UPDATEINDTREE中的其余操作也只需常量时间。

We next analyze the procedure UPDATETREES from Figure 19 under a single-tuple update. Applying the update to each view tree from $\mathcal{T}$ (Line 1) takes $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ time,per Lemmas E. 2 and E.3. We then apply the update to each triple $\left( {{T}_{All},{T}_{L},{T}_{H}}\right)$ of indicator view trees. The tree ${T}_{All}$ is a view tree of a ${\delta }_{0}$ -hierarchical query,thus updating it takes constant time (Line 6). The tree ${T}_{L}$ is updated using UPDATEINDTREE in constant time (Line 12), per Lemma E.4. Both of these changes may trigger a change in $\exists {T}_{H}$ ,and propagating $\delta \left( {\exists H}\right)$ through each view tree from $\mathcal{T}$ (Lines 9 and 14) takes constant time since this change does not affect any view materialized in the light case. Updating each light part of relation $R$ and the affected view trees (Line 11) takes $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ time,per Lemmas E. 2 and E.3.

接下来我们分析图19中的UPDATETREES过程在单元组更新下的表现。根据引理E.2和E.3，对$\mathcal{T}$中的每个视图树应用更新（第1行）需要$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$时间。随后我们将更新应用于指标视图树的三元组$\left( {{T}_{All},{T}_{L},{T}_{H}}\right)$。由于树${T}_{All}$属于${\delta }_{0}$层次查询的视图树，其更新可在常数时间内完成（第6行）。根据引理E.4，树${T}_{L}$通过UPDATEINDTREE在常数时间内完成更新（第12行）。这两处变更可能触发$\exists {T}_{H}$的变化，而将$\delta \left( {\exists H}\right)$传播至$\mathcal{T}$中各视图树（第9和14行）仅需常数时间，因为该变化不会影响轻量级实例化的任何视图。根据引理E.2和E.3，更新关系$R$的轻量部分及受影响视图树（第11行）需要$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$时间。

Overall,the procedure UPDATETREES maintains the views constructed by $\tau$ under a single-tuple update in $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ time.

总体而言，UPDATETREES过程在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$时间内维护了$\tau$构建的视图对单元组更新的响应。

### E.2. Proof of Proposition 7.3.

### E.2. 命题7.3的证明

Proposition 7.3. Given a hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ with static width $\mathrm{w}$ ,a canonical variable order $\omega$ for $Q$ ,a database of size $N$ ,and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,major rebalancing of the views in the set of view trees $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ takes $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {w - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ time.

命题7.3. 给定静态宽度为$\mathrm{w}$的层次化查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$、其规范变量序$\omega$、规模为$N$的数据库及$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，视图树集合$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$中视图的主要再平衡过程耗时$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {w - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$。

Consider the major rebalancing procedure from Figure 20. The light relation parts can be computed in $\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$ time. Proposition 5.4 implies that the affected views can be recomputed in time $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {w - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ .

考虑图20的主要再平衡过程。轻量关系部分可在$\mathcal{O}\left( N\right)$时间内计算完成。根据命题5.4，受影响视图可在$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \left( {w - 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$时间内重新计算。

### E.3. Proof of Proposition 7.4.

### E.3. 命题7.4的证明

Proposition 7.4. Given a hierarchical query $Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$ with dynamic width $\delta$ ,a canonical variable order $\omega$ for $Q$ ,a database of size $N$ ,and $\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,minor rebalancing of the views in the set of view trees $\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$ takes $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\left( {\delta  + 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ time.

命题7.4. 给定动态宽度为$\delta$的层次化查询$Q\left( \mathcal{F}\right)$、其规范变量序$\omega$、规模为$N$的数据库及$\epsilon  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$，视图树集合$\tau \left( {\omega ,\mathcal{F}}\right)$中视图的次要再平衡过程耗时$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\left( {\delta  + 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$。

Figure 21 shows the procedure for minor rebalancing of the tuples with the partitioning value key in the light part ${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$ of relation $R$ . Minor rebalancing either inserts fewer than $\frac{1}{2}{M}^{\epsilon }$ tuples into ${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$ (heavy to light) or deletes at most $\frac{3}{2}{M}^{\epsilon }$ tuples from ${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$ (light to heavy). Each action updates the indicator trees ${T}_{L}$ and ${T}_{H}$ in constant time (lines 5 and 6),per Lemma E.4. Propagating the update to the light part of relation $R$ through each view tree from $\mathcal{T}$ (line 4) takes $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$ time,per Lemmas E. 2 and E.3. Propagating the change $\delta \left( {\exists H}\right)$ through each view tree from $\mathcal{T}$ takes constant time (line 7),as discussed in the proof of Proposition 7.2. Since there are $\mathcal{O}\left( {M}^{\epsilon }\right)$ such operations and the size invariant $\left\lfloor  {\frac{1}{4}M}\right\rfloor   \leq  N < M$ holds,the total time is $\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\left( {\delta  + 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$ .

图21展示了关系$R$的轻量分区${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$中基于分区键值对元组进行微量再平衡的流程。微量再平衡操作要么向${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$插入少于$\frac{1}{2}{M}^{\epsilon }$个元组（从重到轻），要么从${R}^{\mathcal{S}}$删除最多$\frac{3}{2}{M}^{\epsilon }$个元组（从轻到重）。如引理E.4所述，每次操作可在常数时间内更新指示树${T}_{L}$和${T}_{H}$（第5-6行）。根据引理E.2和E.3，通过视图树$\mathcal{T}$将更新传播到关系$R$的轻量分区（第4行）需要$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\delta \epsilon }\right)$时间。如命题7.2证明所述，通过视图树$\mathcal{T}$传播变更$\delta \left( {\exists H}\right)$仅需常数时间（第7行）。由于存在$\mathcal{O}\left( {M}^{\epsilon }\right)$次此类操作且规模不变量$\left\lfloor  {\frac{1}{4}M}\right\rfloor   \leq  N < M$成立，总耗时为$\mathcal{O}\left( {N}^{\left( {\delta  + 1}\right) \epsilon }\right)$。
